Asparagus with Egg & Creamy Sauce, Toast with Peanut Butter and Banana, Tea

~450 kcal9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 100 g asparagus
  • 1 fried egg
  • 2 tbsp light creamy sauce (or yogurt-based sauce)
  • 1 tsp Parmesan cheese
  • Fresh dill
  • 1 slice wholegrain toast (40 g)
  • 10 g peanut butter
  • 1/2 small banana (50 g)
  • 1 cup tea (black or green, no sugar)

Method

  1. Steam or lightly sauté the asparagus until tender.
  2. Place the asparagus on a plate and top with the sauce.
  3. Sprinkle with Parmesan and fresh dill.
  4. Fry the egg and place it on top of the asparagus.
  5. Toast the bread, spread with peanut butter, and top with sliced banana.
  6. Serve with a cup of tea

Avocado & Cottage Cheese Toast with Eggs and Fresh Vegetables

9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Toast
  • Whole grain bread - 80 g (2 slices)
  • Cottage cheese - 100 g
  • Avocado - 80 g (1/2 medium)
  • Chia seeds - 5 g
  • Black pepper - to taste
  • Eggs
  • Eggs - 2 large (120 g without shell)
  • Vegetables
  • Lettuce - 40 g
  • Cherry tomatoes - 80 g
  • Cucumber - 100 g
  • Red bell pepper - 80 g
  • Tea
  • Tea bag or loose-leaf tea - 2 g
  • Hot water - 250 ml
  • Lemon or honey - optional

Method

  1. Toast the bread until golden.
  2. Spread cottage cheese evenly on each slice.
  3. Slice the avocado and place on top. Sprinkle with chia seeds and black pepper.
  4. Boil or poach the eggs to your preferred doneness.
  5. Wash and slice all vegetables, arrange them on the plate.
  6. Prepare the tea: pour hot water over the tea, steep for 3-5 minutes. Add lemon or honey if desired.

Avocado & Egg Toast, Green Tea, and Nuts

4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 slices of whole-grain bread (toasted)
  • 1 ripe avocado
  • 2 eggs (soft- or medium-boiled, as in the photo)
  • Salt, black pepper, chili flakes or paprika (to taste)
  • Lemon juice (1 tsp - optional)
  • 1 cup green tea
  • 8-10 nuts (e.g., almonds, walnuts, cashews)

Method

  1. Mash the avocado in a bowl. Add salt, pepper, and lemon juice if desired. Mix well.
  2. Boil the eggs (6.5-7 minutes for soft-medium center), cool, peel, and slice.
  3. Toast the bread until golden and crispy.
  4. Spread the mashed avocado onto each toast slice. Top with sliced eggs.
  5. Sprinkle with salt, black pepper, and chili flakes or garlic powder if desired.
  6. Serve with a cup of green tea and 8-10 nuts on the side.

Avocado Breakfast Plate

1 serving7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 slice of whole grain or rye bread
  • 2 tbsp cream cheese
  • 1/2 avocado, sliced
  • A few pine nuts (optional)
  • 1 egg
  • A handful of arugula
  • 5-6 cherry tomatoes
  • A handful of blueberries
  • 2-3 slices of cheese
  • Salt and black pepper to taste
  • Olive oil (optional)

Method

  1. Toast the bread lightly and spread the cream cheese evenly on top.
  2. Add avocado slices over the cream cheese and sprinkle with pine nuts.
  3. Fry the egg in a pan with a little oil until the yolk is cooked to your liking. Season with salt and black pepper.
  4. Arrange on a plate: place the toast, fried egg, arugula, cherry tomatoes, blueberries, and cheese slices.
  5. Optionally drizzle a bit of olive oil over the arugula.

Avocado Toast and Prosciutto

1 serving6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 eggs
  • 1 slice of whole grain bread
  • 1/2 ripe avocado
  • 2-3 slices of prosciutto
  • A handful of fresh spinach
  • 3-4 baby corns
  • 1 tsp olive oil or butter
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • Optional: a cup of black coffee and a glass of orange juice

Method

  1. Scramble the eggs
  2. Beat the eggs with a pinch of salt.
  3. Heat olive oil or butter in a pan over medium heat.
  4. Add the eggs and cook, stirring gently, until soft and creamy.
  5. Toast the bread
  6. Toast the bread slice until golden brown.
  7. Prepare the avocado
  8. Mash the avocado with a fork.
  9. Season with a bit of salt, pepper, and lemon juice if desired.
  10. Spread it over the toast.
  11. Add the prosciutto
  12. Place the prosciutto slices on top of the avocado toast.
  13. Serve
  14. Arrange scrambled eggs, baby corn, and fresh spinach on the plate.
  15. Serve with coffee and orange juice.

Avocado Toast with Egg (2 toasts)

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 slices of whole grain bread (toasted)
  • 1 ripe avocado
  • 2 eggs (soft- or medium-boiled)
  • Juice of 1/2 lemon
  • Salt and black pepper to taste
  • Red pepper flakes (optional)
  • Fresh herbs (like chopped chives or green onion)

Method

  1. Toast the bread slices until golden and crispy.
  2. In a bowl, mash the avocado with a fork. Add lemon juice, salt, and pepper.
  3. Spread the mashed avocado evenly over each toast.
  4. Boil the eggs (around 7-8 minutes for a slightly soft yolk), peel, and slice them.
  5. Place the egg slices on top of the avocado toast.
  6. Sprinkle with fresh herbs, red pepper flakes, and more black pepper if desired.
  7. Serve and enjoy while warm.
  8. Afterward: 1 apple

Avocado Toast with Egg, Tomato & Jamón

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 slices of whole-grain or rustic bread
  • 1 ripe avocado
  • 2 hard- or soft-boiled eggs
  • 4-5 cherry tomatoes or 1 medium tomato
  • 3-4 slices of jamón (Spanish cured ham, about 30-40 g)
  • Salt and black pepper to taste
  • A few drops of lemon juice (optional)
  • Olive oil (optional)

Method

  1. Toast the bread until golden and crispy.
  2. Mash the avocado with a fork in a small bowl. Add a pinch of salt, pepper, and a little lemon juice if you like.
  3. Spread the avocado evenly over the toasted bread.
  4. Slice the eggs into wedges and arrange them on top of the avocado.
  5. Cut the tomatoes into small pieces and place them between the egg slices.
  6. Tear the jamón into smaller pieces with your hands and add it on top or between the egg and tomato.
  7. Season with extra pepper (and a little salt if needed). Drizzle with a touch of olive oil if desired.

Avocado Toast with Eggs, Cherry Tomatoes & Arugula

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 slices of bread (whole-grain or sourdough works great)
  • 1 ripe avocado
  • 1-2 eggs (scrambled or fried)
  • 4-6 cherry tomatoes, sliced
  • A handful of arugula
  • Salt and pepper
  • Optional: a drizzle of olive oil
  • Optional: a squeeze of lemon juice
  • 1 nut (e.g., walnut or almond) to place on the plate at the end

Method

  1. Toast the bread in a toaster or on a pan.
  2. Prepare the avocado: mash it with a fork, add salt, pepper, and a few drops of lemon juice.
  3. Cook the eggs: soft scrambled eggs work beautifully - light, creamy, and not overcooked.
  4. Assemble: spread the mashed avocado on each toast, top with eggs, sliced cherry tomatoes, and fresh arugula.
  5. Season with a bit more salt and pepper; drizzle with olive oil if you like.
  6. Finish the plate by adding one nut (like a walnut or almond) as a small final touch.
  7. Finish the plate by adding 8-10 nuts (such as walnuts, almonds, or hazelnuts).

Avocado Toast with Fried Egg

10 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 slice of whole grain bread
  • 1/2 avocado, sliced
  • 1 tsp cream cheese or Greek yogurt (spread on the toast)
  • A pinch of white sesame seeds (for topping)
  • 1 fried egg
  • Fresh vegetables
  • Cucumber (sliced or ribboned)
  • Lettuce leaves
  • Tomatoes (cut into wedges)
  • Drink Options
  • A cup of green tea or matcha
  • Plus 6-8 pieces of your favorite nuts (like almonds, walnuts, or cashews)

Method

  1. Toast the bread until golden and crispy.
  2. Spread cream cheese or Greek yogurt on the toast.
  3. Top with avocado slices and sprinkle with sesame seeds.
  4. Fry the egg to your liking.
  5. Cut the cucumber and tomatoes, and add lettuce.
  6. Arrange everything on a plate and serve with tea or matcha and a small handful of nuts 6-8

Avocado Toast with Poached Egg

1 serving8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 slice of whole-grain or seeded bread
  • 1/2 ripe avocado
  • 1 tablespoon cottage cheese (or ricotta)
  • 2-3 slices of tomato
  • A few fresh spinach leaves
  • 1 egg
  • 1/2 tablespoon vinegar (for poaching)
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • A pinch of paprika or chili flakes (for topping)
  • Optional: a drizzle of olive oil or lemon juice

Method

  1. Toast the bread until golden and crispy.
  2. Mash the avocado in a small bowl with a fork. Add a little salt, pepper, and lemon juice if desired.
  3. Poach the egg
  4. Bring a small pot of water to a gentle simmer.
  5. Add vinegar.
  6. Crack the egg into a cup and carefully slide it into the water.
  7. Cook for about 3 minutes, then remove with a slotted spoon.
  8. Assemble
  9. Spread avocado on the toast.
  10. Add cottage cheese, tomato slices, and spinach.
  11. Top with the poached egg.
  12. Finish
  13. Sprinkle with paprika or chili flakes and a pinch of salt.
  14. Drizzle with olive oil if desired.

Avocado Toast with Poached Eggs

~140 kcal8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Whole grain toast - 40 g (≈ 90 kcal)
  • Avocado - 60 g (≈ 95 kcal)
  • Poached eggs - 2 pcs (≈ 140 kcal)
  • Cucumber - 80 g (≈ 10 kcal)
  • Cherry tomatoes - 80 g (≈ 15 kcal)
  • Lettuce leaves - 30 g (≈ 5 kcal)
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp / 5 g (≈ 45 kcal)
  • Oatmeal - 25 g dry (≈ 90 kcal)
  • Strawberries + blueberries - 50 g (≈ 20 kcal)

Method

  1. Poached eggs
  2. Boil water, add a little vinegar.
  3. Create a small whirlpool with a spoon and carefully pour in the egg.
  4. Cook for 2.5-3 minutes.
  5. Toast
  6. Toast whole grain bread.
  7. Place sliced ​​avocado on top, poached eggs on top.
  8. Add salt and black pepper.
  9. Salad
  10. Cucumber, cherry tomatoes and lettuce.
  11. Drizzle with 1 tsp. olive oil.
  12. Mini-bowl
  13. Pour hot water over oatmeal.
  14. Add berries.
  15. Tea/Coffee

Avocado Toast with Prosciutto & Fried Eggs

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1-2 slices of sourdough or your favorite bread
  • 1 ripe avocado
  • 2 eggs
  • 2-3 slices of prosciutto or jamón
  • 1-2 tsp olive oil or butter
  • Salt & black pepper
  • Chili flakes (optional)
  • Lemon juice (optional)
  • Microgreens or herbs for garnish (optional)

Method

  1. Toast the bread
  2. Toast your bread until golden and crispy.
  3. Prepare the avocado
  4. Mash the avocado in a bowl with a pinch of salt, pepper, and a little lemon juice if you like.
  5. Cook the eggs
  6. Heat oil or butter in a pan and fry the eggs to your preference (sunny-side up works best for this).
  7. Assemble the toast
  8. Spread the mashed avocado generously over the toast.
  9. Add the prosciutto
  10. Layer the prosciutto/jamón on top of the avocado.
  11. Top with eggs
  12. Place the fried eggs on the side or directly on top.
  13. Finish & serve
  14. Sprinkle with black pepper, chili flakes, and garnish with microgreens or herbs.

Avocado Toast with Salmon and Poached Egg

8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 slices of whole grain bread
  • 1 ripe avocado
  • 100 g of lightly salted salmon
  • 2 eggs
  • 1 tbsp vinegar (for poaching the eggs)
  • 1 tsp lemon juice
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • Sesame or flax seeds (optional)
  • A pinch of dried nori or fresh herbs

Method

  1. Peel and mash the avocado with a fork.
  2. Add lemon juice, salt, and pepper; mix well.
  3. Bring water to a boil in a pot, then reduce to a gentle simmer.
  4. Add the vinegar, stir the water to create a whirlpool, and gently drop in the egg.
  5. Cook for 3-4 minutes, then remove with a slotted spoon and place on a paper towel.
  6. Toast the bread in a toaster or on a pan until golden.
  7. Spread the mashed avocado on the toast, add slices of salmon, and top with the poached egg.
  8. Sprinkle with sesame seeds, nori, or fresh herbs.
  9. After breakfast
  10. Green Tea

Avocado Toast with Soft-Boiled Eggs, Nuts & Black Coffee / Green Tea

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 slices of whole-grain or sourdough bread
  • 1 ripe avocado
  • 2 eggs
  • Salt, to taste
  • Black pepper or chili flakes, to taste
  • Lemon juice (optional)
  • 8-10 nuts (almonds, walnuts, or hazelnuts)
  • Black coffee or green tea, without sugar

Method

  1. Toast the bread slices until golden and crispy.
  2. Bring water to a boil, gently add the eggs, and cook for about 6-7 minutes for a soft-boiled center.
  3. While the eggs are cooking, mash the avocado in a bowl with a pinch of salt and a few drops of lemon juice.
  4. Spread the avocado evenly over the toasted bread.
  5. Peel the eggs, cut them in half, and place on top of the avocado toast.
  6. Season with black pepper or chili flakes.
  7. Serve with 8-10 nuts on the side, eaten after the avocado toast.
  8. Enjoy your breakfast with a cup of black coffee or green tea.

Avocado Toast with Tomatoes and Egg

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 slice of whole grain bread (30-40 g).
  • 1/2 avocado (about 70-80 g).
  • 50-70 g of tomatoes
  • A few drops of lemon juice.
  • 2 eggs
  • Salt, pepper to taste.
  • Greens (dill, arugula, spinach) for decoration (optional).

Method

  1. Toast the whole grain bread in a toaster or in a frying pan without oil to make it crispy.
  2. Boil 1 egg
  3. Peel the avocado and mash it with a fork until it is pureed. Add a little lemon juice, salt and pepper to taste.
  4. Spread the avocado puree on the toasted bread.
  5. Put tomatoes on top (sun-dried tomatoes are also possible).
  6. If desired, you can decorate the toast with fresh herbs (arugula, dill or spinach) and drizzle with olive oil.
  7. Boil 2 eggs
  8. Coffee/green tea with berries (60g)

Avocado Toast, Egg White Omelette & Fresh Salad

8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the avocado toast
  • 1 slice of whole grain or rye bread
  • 1/2 ripe avocado
  • Salt & pepper to taste
  • Seeds (sesame, sunflower, or pumpkin) - optional
  • For the omelette
  • 3 egg whites
  • 1 tsp olive oil or a drop of coconut oil
  • A pinch of salt and pepper
  • For the salad
  • A handful of arugula or mixed greens
  • 3-4 cherry tomatoes (or 1 regular tomato)
  • A few pumpkin seeds
  • 1 tsp olive oil (optional)
  • A few drops of lemon juice

Method

  1. Avocado Toast
  2. Toast the bread until golden.
  3. Mash the avocado with a fork, season with salt and pepper.
  4. Spread it over the toast and sprinkle with seeds.
  5. Egg White Omelette
  6. Separate the egg whites from the yolks.
  7. Lightly whisk the egg whites.
  8. Heat the oil in a pan and pour in the egg whites.
  9. Cook on low heat for about 2-3 minutes until set.
  10. Fresh Salad
  11. Slice the tomatoes and mix with arugula.
  12. Add pumpkin seeds.
  13. Drizzle with a bit of olive oil and lemon juice.

Avocado, Cream Cheese & Egg Crispbread Breakfast

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2-3 crispbreads (rye or multigrain)
  • 2 tbsp cream cheese
  • 1/2 avocado
  • 2 eggs (soft or hard-boiled)
  • A handful of arugula or other greens
  • 1 tomato
  • Seeds (pumpkin, sesame, flax - optional)
  • Salt and black pepper - to taste
  • A few drops of lemon juice (optional)

Method

  1. Boil the eggs, let them cool slightly, and cut in halves.
  2. Mash the avocado with a fork, season with salt, pepper, and lemon juice.
  3. Spread a thin layer of cream cheese on the crispbreads.
  4. Top with the avocado mixture.
  5. Sprinkle with seeds.
  6. Serve with eggs, tomato, and greens.
  7. Tea

Avocado, Egg & Veggie Toasts

4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 ripe avocado
  • 2 hard-boiled eggs
  • 5-6 cherry tomatoes or 1 small regular tomato
  • 1-2 tbsp chopped green onion or parsley (optional)
  • 1 tsp lemon juice
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • 2 slices of whole grain bread (with seeds, if desired)
  • Drink of your choice
  • Black coffee (espresso, Americano, or brewed)
  • Tea (green, black, or herbal - your choice)

Method

  1. Peel and mash the avocado in a bowl using a fork.
  2. Dice the boiled eggs and add them to the avocado.
  3. Chop the tomatoes and herbs, then mix them in.
  4. Add lemon juice, salt, and pepper to taste.
  5. Toast the bread slices until golden and crispy.
  6. Spread the mixture evenly over the toasted bread.
  7. Serve with a cup of hot black coffee or tea.

Avocado, Egg & Whole Grain Toast Breakfast

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1/2 ripe avocado
  • 2 boiled eggs (hard-boiled or soft - your choice)
  • 1 slice of whole grain bread
  • 1 tbsp cream cheese (Philadelphia or similar)
  • 2-3 cherry tomatoes (halved) or slices of fresh tomato
  • A pinch of salt and pepper
  • Chopped green onion (for garnish)
  • Optional extras: olive oil, lemon juice, sesame or flax seeds

Method

  1. Boil the eggs - 6-7 min for soft-boiled, 9 min for hard-boiled.
  2. Toast the bread until golden and crispy.
  3. Spread cream cheese over the toast.
  4. Add sliced tomatoes on top. Lightly season with salt.
  5. Slice or mash the avocado and place it on the side or on the toast.
  6. Add the eggs, whole or halved.
  7. Sprinkle with chopped green onion, black pepper, and optional toppings like a drizzle of olive oil or lemon juice.

Avocado, Eggs, Veggies, Tea & Nuts

7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 eggs
  • A handful of fresh spinach
  • A bit of cheese or mozzarella (optional)
  • 1 slice of whole grain bread
  • 1/2 ripe avocado
  • 1 tbsp soft cheese (e.g. cream cheese or ricotta)
  • 2-3 cherry tomatoes
  • 8 nuts (almonds, walnuts, cashews - your choice)
  • 1 cup of herbal or green tea (unsweetened)

Method

  1. Scrambled Eggs with Spinach
  2. Beat the eggs with a pinch of salt.
  3. Lightly sauté spinach in a pan for 1-2 minutes, then pour in the eggs.
  4. Cook over medium heat until set. Sprinkle with cheese if desired.
  5. Avocado Toast
  6. Toast the bread in a toaster or on a pan.
  7. Spread soft cheese on the toast.
  8. Mash the avocado with a fork and spread it on top.
  9. Veggies
  10. Slice the cherry tomatoes.
  11. Nuts & Tea
  12. Add 8 nuts of your choice.
  13. Brew your favorite tea and steep for 3-5 minutes.

Bagel, Egg and Ham

9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 whole grain bagel (or baguette)
  • 1 egg
  • 30 g cream cheese
  • 50 g turkey ham or chicken fillet
  • handful of arugula or salad mix
  • salt, pepper to taste

Method

  1. Cut the bagel in half and brown in a dry frying pan or in a toaster, if desired.
  2. Prepare the egg as you like: poached, scrambled or soft-boiled.
  3. Spread the bottom half of the bagel with cream cheese.
  4. Place the herbs, ham and egg.
  5. Season with salt, pepper and cover with the second half of the bagel.
  6. Serve with coffee or tea.

Banana Pancakes

1 serving5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Banana - 1/2 (about 50 g)
  • Egg - 1
  • Oats (blended into flour) - 3 tbsp (~25 g)
  • Baking powder - 1/2 tsp
  • Cinnamon or vanilla - optional

Method

  1. Mash half the banana with a fork until smooth.
  2. Add the egg and mix well.
  3. Stir in oat flour, baking powder, and spices.
  4. Mix until the batter is thick and creamy.
  5. Cook on a non-stick pan over medium heat for 1-2 minutes on each side.
  6. Add fruits at the end: top the cooked pancakes with the remaining banana slices and any berries you like (blueberries, raspberries, strawberries).
  7. Optional: a teaspoon of Greek yogurt for extra creaminess.

Banana Pancakes with Nuts and Berries

1-2 servings8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Banana - 1 medium (≈100-120 g)
  • Egg - 1
  • Oat flour (or blended oats) - 30 g (≈3 tbsp)
  • Baking powder - 1/2 tsp
  • Nuts (almonds or walnuts) - 10-15 g, chopped
  • Berries (blueberries, raspberries, strawberries) - 50-70 g
  • Cinnamon - a pinch (optional)
  • Oil or butter - 1 tsp for frying

Method

  1. Mash the banana with a fork until smooth.
  2. Add the egg and mix until well combined.
  3. Stir in oat flour, baking powder, and cinnamon.
  4. Fold in the chopped nuts.
  5. Heat a non-stick pan over medium heat and lightly grease with oil or butter.
  6. Spoon the batter onto the pan, forming small pancakes. Cook 2-3 minutes per side until golden.
  7. Serve topped with fresh berries and extra nuts if desired.

Banana Yogurt Bowl

4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 240 g plain or Greek yogurt
  • 1 banana, sliced
  • 2-3 tablespoons granola
  • 1 tablespoon chia seeds

Method

  1. Spoon the yogurt into a bowl and spread it evenly.
  2. Slice the banana and place the slices on top of the yogurt.
  3. Add granola to one side of the bowl.
  4. Sprinkle chia seeds over the yogurt.
  5. Serve immediately and enjoy!

Boiled Eggs with Crispbread, Jamón & Fresh Salad

8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Eggs - 2
  • Crispbread or whole-grain bread - 1 piece
  • Jamón (or other cured ham) - 2-3 thin slices
  • Arugula - 1 handful
  • Mixed salad greens - 1 handful
  • Cherry tomatoes - 6-8
  • Olive oil - a few drops (optional)
  • Salt and black pepper - to taste

Method

  1. Boil the eggs in hot water for 8-9 minutes until hard-boiled. Cool, peel, and cut in half.
  2. Place the crispbread on a plate and top with slices of jamón.
  3. Arrange arugula, mixed salad greens, and cherry tomatoes on the side.
  4. Season the eggs and salad with salt and black pepper. Add a few drops of olive oil if desired.

Boiled Eggs with Fresh Vegetables & Berries

4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 3 boiled eggs
  • 150 g tomatoes, chopped
  • 150 g cucumber, sliced
  • 100 g mixed berries (strawberries, blueberries, raspberries, or any berries you like)
  • Salt & black pepper, to taste

Method

  1. Boil the eggs to your preferred doneness, peel, and serve whole or sliced.
  2. Chop the tomatoes and cucumber, season with salt and black pepper.
  3. Add the fresh berries on the side for a naturally sweet finish.

Ingredients

Breakfast Bowl with Beetroot, Arugula & Egg

8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Cooked or roasted beetroot - 1 piece (or 1/2 if large)
  • Arugula - a generous handful
  • Boiled egg - 1 (soft- or medium-boiled, or poached)
  • Cheese - shaved Parmesan (or crumbled feta/goat cheese for a softer option)
  • Nuts or seeds - pumpkin, walnut, pine nuts (1 tbsp)
  • Olive oil - 1 tbsp
  • Balsamic vinegar or glaze - a few drops
  • Salt and black pepper - to taste
  • (optional) Avocado slices or a spoon of hummus - for extra creaminess and satiety

Method

  1. Slice or cube the beetroot.
  2. Cook the egg to your liking (soft-boiled or poached works best).
  3. In a bowl, layer the arugula, beetroot, and halved egg. Sprinkle with cheese and nuts/seeds.
  4. Drizzle with olive oil and balsamic, and season with salt and pepper.
  5. (Optional) Add a spoonful of hummus or avocado on the side.
  6. Variations
  7. Swap egg for soft goat cheese or roasted pumpkin purée.
  8. Add crunch with crispy chickpeas or whole grain croutons.
  9. Sprinkle with za'atar, sumac, or sesame seeds for extra flavor.

Breakfast Bowl with Boiled Eggs & Avocado

9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 boiled eggs (medium soft)
  • 1 slice of whole grain bread
  • 1 tbsp cream cheese (spread on bread)
  • 1-2 slices of cheese
  • 1/2 avocado, sliced
  • A handful of cherry tomatoes, halved
  • A handful of fresh salad greens (arugula, lettuce, spinach mix)
  • 1 tsp mixed sesame seeds (black & white)
  • Salt and pepper to taste

Method

  1. Boil the eggs for about 6-7 minutes so the yolks stay soft. Peel and cut them in halves.
  2. Spread cream cheese over the slice of whole grain bread.
  3. Place salad greens, avocado slices, cherry tomatoes, and cheese on a plate.
  4. Add the boiled eggs and season with salt, pepper, and sesame seeds.
  5. Serve everything in one bowl for a colorful and nutritious breakfast.
  6. Drink: Serve with a cup of hot tea.

Breakfast Bowl with Eggs, Avocado & Toast

7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 eggs
  • 1/2 avocado
  • 2 slices of bread (rye or whole grain)
  • 2-3 slices of halloumi cheese (or any firm cheese suitable for frying)
  • 3-4 tbsp baked beans in tomato sauce
  • A handful of arugula
  • Seeds (like flax or sesame) - optional
  • Olive oil, salt, pepper

Method

  1. Toast the bread - in a toaster or on a dry skillet until golden and crispy.
  2. Make scrambled eggs - whisk the eggs, season with a pinch of salt, and cook over low heat, stirring gently until creamy and soft (you can use a bit of butter or olive oil).
  3. Fry the halloumi - cook on a non-stick pan for 1-2 minutes per side until golden brown.
  4. Heat the beans - warm them up in a small pan or microwave.
  5. Slice the avocado - season with a pinch of salt and optionally sprinkle with seeds.
  6. Assemble the bowl - place the scrambled eggs, beans, avocado, and halloumi in a bowl. Add arugula on top and serve with toasted bread on the side.

Breakfast in a Glass

1 serving7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 150 g Greek yogurt (or plain natural yogurt, unsweetened)
  • 2 tbsp chia seeds
  • 100 ml milk (regular or plant-based)
  • 1/2 apple or pear (diced)
  • A handful of berries (raspberries, blueberries, strawberries)
  • 20-25 g walnuts or almonds
  • 1 tsp honey or Jerusalem artichoke syrup (optional)
  • A few coconut flakes

Method

  1. The night before, mix chia seeds with milk, stir well, and let sit in the fridge overnight (or at least 2-3 hours).
  2. In the morning, layer the glass: yogurt at the bottom, then fruit, then the chia pudding.
  3. Add berries on top, sprinkle with nuts and coconut flakes.
  4. Drizzle with a bit of honey if you like.

Breakfast Plate with Avocado, Egg & Cheese

1 serving7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 slices whole-grain or rye bread
  • 1 ripe avocado
  • 1 hard-boiled egg
  • 2 slices semi-hard cheese (e.g. Gouda, Edam, or similar)
  • A handful of arugula
  • 5-6 cherry tomatoes

Breakfast Plate with Eggs & Blueberries

Ingredients

  • 4-5 radishes, sliced
  • 1 small yellow tomato, chopped

  • Olive oil (optional)
  • Salt and black pepper, to taste

Method

  1. Toast the whole-grain bread until golden.
  2. Heat a non-stick pan over medium heat. Crack 2 eggs into the pan and fry them to your liking (sunny-side up or over-easy). Season with salt and pepper.

  6. Serve immediately and enjoy a balanced, fresh breakfast.

Breakfast Plate with Prosciutto

9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Whole grain bread - 80 g (2 slices)
  • Avocado - 70 g (about half a medium avocado)
  • Eggs - 2 pcs (approx. 100 g)
  • Prosciutto (dry-cured ham) - 40 g
  • Microgreens (e.g. alfalfa or radish) - 10 g
  • Apple - 100 g (half an apple, sliced)
  • Strawberries - 50 g (2-3 pieces)
  • Blueberries - 70 g

Method

  1. Toast the bread slices in a dry pan or toaster until golden and crispy.

Breakfast Plate with Salmon & Asparagus

9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Eggs - 2 pcs (≈100 g)
  • Diced tomato or bell pepper - 50 g
  • Grated cheese (optional) - 10-15 g
  • Avocado - 1/2 piece (≈80-90 g pulp)
  • Smoked salmon - 60-80 g
  • Fresh or frozen asparagus - 120-150 g
  • Whole-grain or seed bread - 2 slices (≈60-70 g)
  • Olive oil or butter - 5-10 g
  • Salt and black pepper - to taste

Method

  1. Cook the asparagus (fresh or frozen)
  2. Heat a pan with 5 g olive oil.
  3. Add 120-150 g asparagus (frozen can go straight to the pan).
  4. Sauté for 4-6 minutes until tender but slightly crisp.
  5. Season with salt and pepper.
  6. Cook the eggs
  7. In the same pan, sauté 50 g diced tomato or bell pepper for 1 minute.
  8. Crack 2 eggs over the vegetables.
  9. Season with salt and pepper.
  10. Cook until desired doneness.
  11. Sprinkle with 10-15 g grated cheese (optional).
  12. Prepare the sides
  13. Slice 80-90 g avocado and season lightly.
  14. Prepare 60-80 g smoked salmon.
  15. Toast 2 slices (60-70 g) whole-grain bread.
  16. Assemble the plate
  17. Arrange asparagus, eggs, avocado, salmon, and toasted bread beautifully on a plate.

Broccoli & Cherry Tomato Egg Bake

Ingredients

  • 2 eggs
  • 1 tbsp milk (optional)
  • 3-4 broccoli florets (lightly steamed or blanched)
  • 3-4 cherry tomatoes, halved
  • Salt and black pepper to taste
  • A drizzle of olive oil or a small piece of butter

Method

Cheese Omelette with Fresh Salad

9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Omelette
  • 2 eggs
  • 20 g parmesan or hard cheese, grated
  • 1 tsp butter or olive oil
  • Chives or fresh herbs
  • Salt & black pepper

  • Handful of lamb's lettuce / mixed greens
  • 3-4 cherry tomatoes
  • 1 tsp olive oil
  • Salt & pepper
  • Extra
  • 1 small kiwi or apple
  • Green tea / black coffee / americano without sugar

Method

  1. Make the omelette
  2. Crack the eggs into a bowl and whisk well.
  3. Add half of the grated cheese, salt, and pepper.

Chia Pudding Bowl with Granola and Banana

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the chia pudding
  • 3 tbsp chia seeds
  • 200 ml plant-based or regular milk
  • 1 tsp honey or agave syrup (optional)
  • For topping
  • 1/2 banana, sliced
  • 3-4 tbsp granola
  • 1 tbsp sliced almonds
  • A few freeze-dried raspberries or other dried berries

Method

  1. Make the chia pudding
  2. Mix the chia seeds with milk and sweetener if using.
  3. Stir well and let sit in the fridge for 4-6 hours or overnight.
  4. Stir again after 10 minutes to prevent clumping.
  5. Assemble the bowl
  6. Spoon the chia pudding into a bowl.
  7. Add granola on one side.
  8. Place the banana slices in the center.
  9. Sprinkle with almond flakes and freeze-dried raspberries.

Chia Pudding with Oatmeal, Raspberries & Granola

7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For chia pudding (prepare the night before)
  • 3 tbsp chia seeds
  • 150 ml plant-based milk (almond, coconut, etc.)
  • 1 tsp honey or maple syrup (optional)
  • For oatmeal
  • 3-4 tbsp rolled oats
  • 100 ml water or milk
  • A pinch of salt
  • Toppings
  • A handful of fresh raspberries
  • 2-3 tbsp granola or muesli

Method

  1. The night before: Mix the chia seeds with milk and honey. Stir well and refrigerate overnight.
  2. In the morning: Cook the oatmeal - bring water or milk to a boil, add the oats, and simmer for 3-5 minutes until soft.
  3. In a bowl, layer the following
  4. Cooked oatmeal
  5. Chia pudding
  6. Fresh raspberries
  7. Granola (on top or on the side)
  8. Optional: Add a drizzle of honey, some seeds, or coconut flakes on top.

Chia Pudding with Yogurt, Kiwi & Nuts

4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 3 tbsp chia seeds
  • 150 ml water (filtered or boiled and cooled)
  • 100 g unsweetened plant-based yogurt (e.g. coconut, soy, almond)
  • 1-2 kiwis, peeled and chopped
  • 1-2 tbsp chopped nuts (e.g. walnuts, hazelnuts, almonds, cashews)
  • Optional: cacao nibs or grated dark chocolate for topping

Method

  1. Prepare chia pudding
  2. Mix chia seeds with water. Stir well, wait 5 minutes, then stir again to avoid clumps. Refrigerate for at least 4-6 hours or overnight until it forms a gel-like consistency.
  3. Prep the toppings
  4. Chop the kiwi. Lightly toast the nuts in a dry pan (optional) and chop them.
  5. Assemble the breakfast in layers
  6. Layer of chia pudding
  7. Layer of yogurt
  8. Layer of kiwi
  9. Repeat if desired
  10. Top with chopped nuts and cacao nibs

Chia Yogurt with Fruit

1 serving2 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Natural (unsweetened) yogurt - 150 g
  • Chia seeds - 10 g (about 1 tablespoon)
  • Honey or syrup (optional) - 5 g (about 1 teaspoon)
  • Fresh fruits (any you like) - 100 g
  • Nuts or granola (optional) - 10 g

Method

  1. In a small bowl or jar, mix 150 g of yogurt with 10 g of chia seeds.
  2. Add 5 g of honey or syrup (optional) and stir well.
  3. Let it sit for 15-20 minutes, or refrigerate overnight to allow the chia seeds to expand and soften.
  4. Before serving, top with 100 g of chopped fresh fruit.
  5. Optionally, sprinkle 10 g of nuts or granola on top for added crunch.

Cottage Cheese Bake

2 servings6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Cottage cheese (5% fat) - 200 g
  • Egg - 1
  • Oat flour (or semolina/rice flour) - 1.5 tbsp
  • Plain unsweetened yogurt - 2 tbsp
  • Erythritol or stevia - to taste (about 1-2 tsp)
  • Vanilla or cinnamon - optional
  • A pinch of salt
  • For serving
  • Yogurt - 1-2 tbsp
  • Nuts (almonds, hazelnuts, etc.) - 10-15 g
  • Banana - 1/2 piece

Method

  1. In a bowl, mix the cottage cheese, egg, oat flour, yogurt, sweetener, and vanilla.
  2. Blend until smooth (a hand blender works great).
  3. Pour the mixture into a baking dish lined with parchment paper or lightly greased.
  4. Bake at 180°C for about 30-35 minutes, until lightly golden.
  5. Let it cool completely - it will become firmer.
  6. Serve with a spoon of yogurt, banana slices, and chopped nuts on top.

Cottage Cheese Casserole

11 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 400 g cottage cheese (low-fat)
  • 2 eggs
  • 2-3 tbsp oat flour (or blended oats)
  • 1-2 tbsp Greek yogurt
  • 1-2 tbsp honey or any natural sweetener
  • 1 tsp vanilla extract
  • A pinch of salt
  • 80-100 g berries (blueberries, raspberries, or mixed)
  • Powdered erythritol (optional, for topping)
  • Additional fruits (per serving)
  • 1 apple or 1 pear sliced
  • A handful of grapes or seasonal fruit
  • Tea pairing
  • Hot black tea or green tea
  • Optional: lemon slice, mint, or a teaspoon of honey

Method

  1. Preheat the oven to 180°C.
  2. In a bowl, mix cottage cheese, eggs, oat flour, Greek yogurt, honey, vanilla, and salt until smooth.
  3. Pour the mixture into a small baking dish lined with parchment or lightly greased.
  4. Evenly add berries on top (press them lightly into the mixture).
  5. Bake for 30-35 minutes until golden and set.
  6. Let it cool slightly, then cut into 2 equal portions - one for each day.
  7. Before serving, add fresh fruits and a sprinkle of powdered erythritol if desired.
  8. Serve with a cup of hot tea.

Cottage Cheese Crepes

1 serving · ~300-350 kcal8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the crepes
  • 1 egg
  • 2 tbsp oat flour (or finely ground oats)
  • 2-3 tbsp milk (regular or plant-based)
  • 1/2 tsp olive oil or coconut oil
  • A pinch of salt
  • Sweetener or a few drops of vanilla extract (optional)
  • For the filling
  • 100 g low-fat cottage cheese (or ricotta)
  • 1/2 tsp honey or any natural sweetener
  • A few drops of vanilla extract
  • Optional: a few berries for topping

Method

  1. In a bowl, whisk the egg, milk, oat flour, salt, and oil until smooth.
  2. Heat a non-stick pan and pour a thin layer of batter - cook 1-2 minutes per side until golden.
  3. In another bowl, mix the cottage cheese with honey and vanilla.
  4. Spread the filling on the crepe and roll or fold it.
  5. Serve with fresh berries or a drizzle of Greek yogurt if you like.

Cottage Cheese Crepes with Fruit and Honey

1 serving · ~400 kcal7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the crepes
  • 1 egg
  • 60 ml low-fat milk (1.5%)
  • 25 g (about 3 tbsp) oat or whole-grain flour
  • A pinch of salt
  • A few drops of olive oil or non-stick spray for cooking
  • For the filling
  • 100 g low-fat cottage cheese
  • A few drops of vanilla extract (optional)
  • 1 tsp honey
  • For topping
  • 1/2 small banana or a handful of berries (about 50-70 g)
  • 1 tsp honey (optional drizzle)

Method

  1. Make the batter: Whisk the egg, milk, flour, and salt until smooth.
  2. Cook the crepes: Pour a thin layer of batter onto a non-stick pan and cook on both sides until golden. You'll get about 2-3 small crepes.
  3. Prepare the filling: Mix cottage cheese with vanilla and 1 tsp honey.
  4. Assemble: Spread the filling inside each crepe, roll them up, and top with fruit and a drizzle of honey.

Cottage Cheese Pancakes

1 serving3 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Low-fat or 5% cottage cheese - 150 g
  • Egg - 1 (you can use just the white for lower fat)
  • Oat or rice flour - 1.5-2 tbsp (or coconut flour for a gluten-free version)
  • Sweetener or honey - to taste (e.g. 1 tsp honey or 1/2 tsp erythritol)
  • Optional: vanilla, cinnamon, pinch of salt
  • Oil for frying (coconut, olive, or none on a non-stick pan) - 1/2 tsp or skip

Method

  1. Mix cottage cheese, egg, and sweetener until smooth (use a fork or blender).
  2. Add flour and form small round pancakes.
  3. Cook on a non-stick pan over medium heat for 2-3 minutes per side, until golden.
  4. Serve with
  5. Greek yogurt (1-2 tbsp)
  6. Fresh berries (e.g. blueberries, strawberries)
  7. 1 tsp honey or maple syrup
  8. A sprinkle of chopped nuts (for crunch)

Cottage Cheese Pancakes with Fruit

3 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Cottage cheese (5-9% fat) - 150 g
  • Egg - 1 small (or use half of a large one)
  • Semolina - 20-30 g (about 1.5-2 tbsp)
  • Sugar - 1 tbsp (or to taste)
  • Vanilla sugar - 1/2 tsp (optional)
  • A pinch of salt
  • Flour - a little, for coating only
  • Oil - 1-2 tbsp for frying

Method

  1. Prepare the batter
  2. (Optional) Press the cottage cheese through a sieve for a smoother texture.
  3. Add the egg, sugar, salt, and vanilla sugar. Mix well.
  4. Stir in the semolina and let the mixture rest for 10-15 minutes to thicken.
  5. Shape the pancakes
  6. Lightly wet or oil your hands.
  7. Divide the mixture into 4-6 portions, roll into balls, and flatten into small pancakes.
  8. Coat each piece lightly with flour.
  9. Fry
  10. Heat a non-stick pan with a small amount of oil over medium heat.
  11. Fry the pancakes for 2-3 minutes on each side, until golden and cooked through.
  12. Serve warm with fresh fruit (50grams) and tea.

Cottage Cheese with Fruits and Nuts

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Cottage cheese (low-fat, around 5%) - 120 g

  • Seasonal fruits (apple, pear, kiwi, strawberries, blueberries, raspberries) - 100-120 g
  • Nuts (walnuts, almonds, or hazelnuts) - 8 -10
  • Honey or stevia - 1/2 tsp (optional)

Method

  1. Mash cottage cheese with a fork or mix with yogurt until creamy.
  2. Cut fruits into small cubes or slices.
  3. Combine cottage cheese with fruits.
  4. Sprinkle with nuts.
  5. Add a little honey or stevia if you like.
  6. Drink
  7. Green or black tea with a few nuts on the side (5-6 almonds or walnuts).

  • 1 tsp. butter
  • 1 tbsp. milk or cream (optional)
  • salt, pepper
  • Cut the croissant lengthwise (not all the way through) and dry it a little in a dry frying pan or in the oven for 3-4 minutes at 180°C.

12 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the omelet
  • 2 eggs
  • 1 handful of fresh spinach
  • 1 tbsp plant-based milk (optional - oat, almond, etc.)
  • Salt, pepper to taste
  • A pinch of turmeric (optional, for color & health benefits)
  • A little oil for cooking (e.g., coconut or olive oil) or non-stick pan
  • For the filling
  • Fresh spinach leaves - a small handful
  • Corn (boiled or canned, no sugar) - 2 tbsp
  • Light cheese (e.g., feta light, cottage cheese, or grated mozzarella) - 1-2 tbsp
  • Jamón (Spanish cured ham), thinly sliced - ~20-30 g

  4. Pour the mixture into a heated non-stick pan. Cover with a lid and cook on low until the top is set.
  5. Add the filling
  6. On one half of the omelet, add spinach leaves, corn, cheese, and turkey (if using). Fold the omelet in half.
  7. Serve and decorate
  8. Sprinkle with sesame seeds. Serve with a small side of seaweed, veggie roll, or your favorite healthy toppings.

Healthy Banana & Nut Oatmeal

4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Rolled oats (not instant) - 50 g (about 4 tbsp)
  • Milk or water (or a mix of both) - 200 ml
  • Banana - 1 ripe medium
  • Nuts (walnuts, almonds, cashews, etc.) - 10-15 g (1 tbsp)
  • Chia or flax seeds (optional) - 1 tsp
  • Honey or syrup (agave, maple) - 1 tsp (optional)
  • Cinnamon - a pinch (to taste)
  • A pinch of salt

Method

  1. Prepare the base
  2. Combine oats with milk or water in a saucepan.
  3. Add a pinch of salt and cinnamon.
  4. Bring to a boil, then simmer on low heat for 5-7 minutes, stirring occasionally.
  5. Add the banana
  6. Mash half of the banana and stir it into the oats while cooking - this will make the oatmeal naturally sweet and creamy.
  7. Slice the other half into rounds for topping.
  8. Finishing touches
  9. Once the oats are cooked, remove from heat.
  10. Transfer to a bowl. Top with banana slices, chopped nuts, and optionally honey or syrup, and chia or flax seeds.

Healthy Berry & Almond Oatmeal Bowl

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 40 g rolled oats
  • 150 ml milk (or almond/oat milk)
  • 100 ml water
  • 50 g fresh or frozen raspberries
  • 50 g mixed berries (blueberries, strawberries, blackberries)
  • 1 tbsp sliced or chopped almonds (lightly toasted if preferred)
  • 1 tsp chia seeds (optional)
  • Pinch of ground cinnamon
  • 1/2 tsp vanilla extract (optional)
  • A few drops of lemon juice (optional, to brighten the berries)
  • 1 tsp honey or maple syrup (optional, if you want a hint of sweetness)

Method

  1. Prepare the base
  2. In a small pot, combine oats, milk, and water. Bring to a gentle boil over medium heat.
  3. Simmer the oats
  4. Reduce the heat to low and cook for 5-7 minutes, stirring occasionally, until the oats are soft and creamy. Add cinnamon and vanilla in the last minute of cooking.
  5. Prep the berries
  6. If using frozen berries, you can warm them slightly in a pan or microwave with a few drops of lemon juice. If fresh - just rinse and pat dry.
  7. Assemble the bowl
  8. Pour the cooked oatmeal into a serving bowl. Top with raspberries, mixed berries, and almonds. Sprinkle chia seeds on top.
  9. Optional finish
  10. Drizzle with a little honey or maple syrup if desired, but it's naturally sweet from the berries.

Healthy Cottage Cheese Casserole (No Flour)

4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Cottage cheese (low-fat, ~5%) - 200 g
  • Egg - 1
  • Plain yogurt or kefir - 2 tbsp
  • Honey or erythritol - to taste (1-2 tsp)
  • Optional: vanilla extract, cinnamon, lemon zest
  • Berries (e.g. blueberries, raspberries) - a small handful

Method

  1. Mix cottage cheese, egg, yogurt, sweetener, and optional flavorings until smooth (use a fork or blender).
  2. Pour the mixture into a greased baking dish or silicone mold.
  3. Add berries on top, or gently mix them in.
  4. Bake at 180°C for 25-30 minutes, until golden on top.
  5. Let it cool slightly before serving - it will firm up as it cools.
  6. This version is soft and fluffy - almost like a soufflé. You can also add 1 tsp of cocoa powder for a chocolate twist

Healthy Oatmeal with Fruits and Nuts

10 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Rolled oats - 50 g
  • Milk or water - 200 ml (can use plant-based: almond, soy, oat milk)
  • Banana - 1/2 piece
  • Apple or pear - 1/2 piece
  • Berries (raspberries, blueberries, strawberries) - a handful (fresh or frozen)
  • Walnuts or almonds - 1 tbsp
  • Flax seeds or chia seeds - 1 tsp
  • Cinnamon - a pinch
  • Honey or Jerusalem artichoke syrup - to taste (optional, about 1 tsp)

Method

  1. In a small pot, bring the milk or water to a boil.
  2. Add the oats, reduce the heat, and cook for 5-7 minutes, stirring occasionally until creamy.
  3. Chop the banana and apple (or pear) into small pieces.
  4. Once the oatmeal is ready, pour it into a bowl.
  5. Top with fruits, berries, nuts, seeds, and a sprinkle of cinnamon.
  6. Add a bit of honey or syrup if you want a sweeter taste.
  7. Tips
  8. For a quicker morning routine, prepare as overnight oats by soaking them in milk overnight.
  9. You can replace fresh fruit with dried fruit (like dates or apricots).
  10. For extra protein, add a spoonful of Greek yogurt or protein powder.

  • 1 thin flatbread or tortilla
  • 50-70 g cheese (feta, cottage cheese, mozzarella, or farmer's cheese)
  • 1 tomato
  • 1/2 bell pepper or cucumber (optional)
  • 1/4 avocado (optional)
  • A handful of greens (arugula, spinach, parsley, dill, etc.)
  • 1-2 tbsp Greek yogurt or hummus (for spread)
  • A few drops of lemon juice or balsamic vinegar
  • Salt & pepper to taste
  • 1 tsp olive oil (optional)

Method

  1. Prepare the ingredients
  2. Dice or crumble the cheese.
  3. Slice the tomato and other veggies.
  4. Wash and dry the greens.
  5. Slice the avocado (if using).
  6. Assemble the wrap
  7. Spread yogurt or hummus on one side of the flatbread.
  8. Add greens, veggies, and cheese.
  9. Season with salt, pepper, and a bit of lemon juice or balsamic.
  10. Toast it
  11. Roll up tightly like a burrito or shawarma.
  12. Toast on a dry pan or grill for 1-2 minutes on each side until crispy.
  13. Serve
  14. Cut in half and enjoy with your coffee or tea.

Lavash Wrap with Tuna

8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Lavash flatbread
  • 70 g canned tuna in its own juice (drained) (or 1/2 avocado - as a vegetarian option)
  • 1 hard-boiled egg
  • Cream cheese (e.g. Philadelphia)
  • 30 g soft cheese (like spreadable or shredded cheese)
  • 70 g cucumber
  • 50 g tomato
  • 30 g lettuce leaves
  • Ground black pepper (to taste)
  • Fresh dill (optional)

Method

  1. Hard-boil the egg and chop it finely.
  2. Drain the tuna and break it into small chunks with a fork.
  3. Dice the cucumber and tomato. Chop fresh dill if using.
  4. Spread cream cheese over one side of the lavash.
  5. Layer with lettuce leaves.
  6. Add chopped egg, tuna, cucumber, and tomato.
  7. Sprinkle with dill and season with pepper to taste.
  8. Fold the top and bottom edges inward, then roll into a tight wrap.
  9. Toast in a dry, preheated pan for 2 minutes on each side, pressing lightly.
  10. Serve with
  11. Hot tea and a few nuts on the side

Oat Pancakes

1 serving10 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the pancakes (3-4 pieces)
  • 40 g rolled oats (blended into flour)
  • 1 egg
  • 50 ml low-fat kefir or plain unsweetened yogurt
  • 1/2 tsp baking powder
  • A pinch of cinnamon or vanilla (optional)
  • For serving
  • A handful of fresh spinach
  • 40 g low-fat cheese (light mozzarella, feta, or cottage cheese)
  • 1 poached or soft-boiled egg
  • 2 tbsp plain unsweetened yogurt (as a sauce)

Method

  1. Blend oats into flour, then mix with egg, kefir (or yogurt), baking powder, and spices. Let rest for 5 minutes to thicken.
  2. Cook pancakes on a non-stick pan for 1-2 minutes per side, without oil.
  3. Prepare a poached or soft-boiled egg.
  4. Arrange pancakes on a plate with spinach, cheese, egg, and yogurt as a sauce.

Oatmeal Breakfast with Berries, Banana, and Peach

1 serving6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 40 g rolled oats
  • 200 ml milk or plant-based milk
  • 1/2 banana, sliced
  • 1/2 peach, sliced
  • A small handful of berries (e.g., blueberries, raspberries, or strawberries)
  • Optional: 1 tsp honey or maple syrup, cinnamon to taste

Method

  1. In a small pot, combine the oats and milk.
  2. Cook over medium heat, stirring occasionally, for about 5-7 minutes, until the oats are soft and creamy.
  3. Transfer the oatmeal to a bowl.
  4. Top with sliced banana, peach, and berries.
  5. Add a drizzle of honey or sprinkle of cinnamon if desired.

Oatmeal with Berries

1 serving4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 40 g rolled oats
  • 150 ml water or unsweetened plant-based milk (e.g. almond, oat, soy)
  • A pinch of salt
  • 1/2 banana (for natural sweetness, optional)
  • 50-70 g fresh or frozen berries (blueberries, raspberries, strawberries etc.)
  • Optional toppings: chia seeds, nuts, coconut flakes, cinnamon

Method

  1. Cook the oats
  2. In a small saucepan, combine oats, water (or plant milk), and a pinch of salt.
  3. Bring to a boil, then reduce the heat and simmer for 5-7 minutes, stirring occasionally, until thickened.
  4. Sweeten naturally (optional)
  5. Mash in half a banana during the last minute of cooking for natural sweetness - or skip it for a more neutral flavor.
  6. Add the berries
  7. If using frozen berries, you can stir them into the pot in the last minute to warm them.
  8. If using fresh berries, just add them on top when serving.
  9. Top and serve
  10. Pour the oatmeal into a bowl.
  11. Add your berries and optional toppings like a sprinkle of chia seeds, a few crushed nuts, or a pinch of cinnamon.

Ingredients

  • Raspberries - 5-6 pieces

  • A pinch of salt

Method

  1. In a small pot, bring the milk or water to a boil.
  2. Add the oats and a pinch of salt. Cook for 5-7 minutes, stirring occasionally until the oats are soft and creamy.
  3. Remove from heat. Add honey or syrup if desired.
  4. Pour the oatmeal into a bowl.
  5. Top with
  6. Sliced banana
  7. Sliced strawberries
  8. Fresh raspberries and blueberries
  9. Optionally, sprinkle with chia seeds, nuts, or shredded coconut.

Omelette with Avocado & Greens

4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 eggs
  • 1/2 avocado
  • A handful of fresh spinach
  • A handful of arugula
  • 1 teaspoon olive oil
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • Optional: a splash of lemon juice

Method

  1. Heat the olive oil in a pan over medium heat.
  2. Add the spinach and sauté for 1-2 minutes until wilted.
  3. In a separate bowl, whisk the eggs with salt and pepper.
  4. Pour the eggs into the pan with the spinach and gently stir, cooking the scrambled eggs until they reach your preferred consistency.
  5. Slice or mash the avocado and top the scrambled eggs along with the arugula.
  6. Optional: drizzle with a bit of lemon juice for freshness.

Ingredients

  • Eggs - 2

  • Sesame seeds (white and black) - 1 tsp

  • Cooking oil - 1 tsp
  • Salt and pepper - to taste

Method

  1. Beat the eggs with a pinch of salt and pepper.

  3. Once nearly cooked, spread feta or hummus on top (optional).

  6. Sprinkle sesame seeds over the top before serving.

Method

  1. Crack the eggs into a bowl, add a pinch of salt and pepper, and beat well.
  2. (No milk - just eggs.)
  3. Chop the tomato into small cubes or slices.
  4. Tear the prosciutto into bite-sized pieces.
  5. Heat the olive oil in a non-stick pan over medium heat.
  6. Add the chopped tomato and sauté for 1-2 minutes until some liquid evaporates.
  7. Add the prosciutto and cook for another 30-60 seconds.
  8. Pour in the beaten eggs and let cook without stirring. Cover with a lid if you want the top to cook evenly.
  9. Once set, fold the omelet or leave it open-style.
  10. Sprinkle with fresh

Pancakes with Berries

~240-280 kcal8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Oat flour - 100 g (or blended rolled oats)
  • Egg - 1 piece
  • Milk (or plant-based) - 150 ml
  • Banana - 1 (for sweetness)
  • Baking powder - 1 tsp
  • Berries (blueberries, strawberries, raspberries) - 50-100 g (Instead of berries, you can use mango puree - it adds natural sweetness and a tropical twist.)
  • Coconut oil or other for frying - 1 tsp

Method

  4. Serve the pancakes with berries on top. Optionally, drizzle with natural yogurt or honey.
  5. Portion: 3-4 pancakes
  6. Calories: 240-280 kcal
  7. Tea

Pancakes with Fruit and Honey (Healthy and Light)

8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 ripe banana
  • 2 eggs
  • 2 tbsp. oatmeal or oat flour
  • 1/2 tsp. baking powder (optional)
  • Cinnamon or vanilla - to taste
  • A little coconut or olive oil for cooking
  • Fresh fruit or berries (e.g. blueberries, strawberries, peaches, apples)
  • 1 tsp. honey

Method

  1. Mash the banana with a fork until smooth.
  2. Add the eggs and beat with a whisk.
  3. Add the oatmeal, cinnamon/vanilla and baking powder. Let the dough rest for 5 minutes.
  4. Heat a non-stick frying pan with a little oil.
  5. Place the dough in small pancakes and cook over medium heat, 1-2 minutes on each side until golden brown.
  6. Serving example
  7. Add your favorite fresh fruit or berries.
  8. Drizzle with 1 teaspoon of honey.
  9. Add a spoonful of Greek yogurt, if desired.
  10. Serve with green tea or black coffee (without sugar).

Poached Egg & Avocado Salad with Rice Cake and Cream Cheese

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 poached egg
  • 1/2 avocado, sliced
  • A handful of arugula (rocket)
  • A few cherry tomatoes, halved
  • Salt and black pepper, to taste
  • 1 rice cake
  • 1 tbsp cream cheese (Philadelphia or similar)

Method

  1. Prepare the poached egg: bring water to a gentle simmer, add a splash of vinegar, and poach the egg for 3-4 minutes until the white is set but the yolk remains runny.
  2. On a plate or bowl, arrange arugula and cherry tomatoes.
  3. Add the sliced avocado on top of the greens.
  4. Place the poached egg in the center, season with salt and freshly ground black pepper.
  5. Serve with a rice cake spread with cream cheese on the side.

Ingredients

  • 1/2 tsp baking powder
  • A splash of milk or water if needed
  • Toppings
  • 100 g Greek yogurt
  • 80 g blueberries
  • 100 g peach slices
  • 10 g honey

Scrambled Eggs with Spinach, Cheese and Avocado

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 eggs
  • handful of fresh spinach
  • 30-40 g of hard cheese (any that melts well can be used)
  • 1/2-1 avocado
  • 1 tsp. olive oil or butter
  • salt, pepper - to taste
  • sesame seeds (white/black) - for serving

Method

  1. Heat a frying pan with oil or butter.
  2. Add spinach and fry for 1-2 minutes until it decreases in volume.
  3. Crack the eggs directly onto the spinach. Cook over medium heat.
  4. Add pieces of cheese on top so that it melts a little.
  5. Season with salt and pepper. You can cover with a lid for 1-2 minutes if you want a more tender yolk.
  6. At the same time, cut the avocado into slices.

Shakshuka

1 serving6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Eggs - 2
  • Fresh tomatoes or canned tomatoes - 2 medium
  • Bell pepper - 1/2 (optional)
  • Onion - 1/2
  • Garlic - 1 clove
  • Olive oil or vegetable oil - 1 tbsp
  • Sweet paprika - 1/2 tsp
  • Ground cumin - a pinch (optional)
  • Salt and pepper - to taste
  • Fresh herbs (parsley or cilantro) - for garnish
  • Bread or toast - for serving (optional)

Method

  1. Prep the vegetables
  2. Finely chop the onion and pepper. Grate or dice the tomatoes. Mince the garlic.
  3. Sauté
  4. Heat the oil in a skillet.
  5. Sauté the onion until soft, add the bell pepper, and cook for 2-3 minutes.
  6. Add garlic, paprika, cumin (optional), salt, and pepper.
  7. Make the tomato sauce
  8. Add the tomatoes and simmer for 5-7 minutes until the mixture thickens slightly.
  9. Add the eggs
  10. Make small wells in the sauce and crack the eggs into them.
  11. Cover and cook for 4-5 minutes, or until the egg whites are set and yolks remain soft (or to your preference).
  12. Serve
  13. Sprinkle with fresh herbs and serve with bread or toast.

Method

  1. Prepare the ingredients
  2. Beat the eggs with milk, salt, and pepper.
  3. Dice the tomato and wash the spinach.
  4. Cook the omelette
  5. Heat a small amount of oil or butter in a non-stick pan over medium heat.
  6. Pour in the egg mixture and cook for 1-2 minutes until it starts to set.
  7. Add the filling
  8. On one half of the omelette, place the cheese, tomato, and spinach.
  9. Fold the omelette over to cover the filling.
  10. Finish cooking
  11. Let it cook for another 1-2 minutes until the cheese melts and the omelette is fully cooked.
  12. Serve
  13. Slide the omelette onto a plate and garnish with a spinach leaf if desired.
  14. After the meal
  15. Enjoy a cup of hot tea with a small handful of nuts (such as almonds, walnuts, or cashews) for a healthy and energizing finish to your breakfast.

Syrnyky (Cottage Cheese Pancakes)

1 serving10 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 150 g cottage cheese (farmer's cheese or ricotta-style, not watery)
  • 1 small egg
  • 1 tbsp sugar (or honey)
  • 2 tbsp flour (+ a little more for shaping)
  • A pinch of salt
  • Oil for frying
  • Optional for serving
  • Sour cream, yogurt, berries, banana, powdered sugar

Method

  1. In a bowl, mash the cottage cheese until smooth.
  2. Add the egg, sugar, and a pinch of salt. Mix well.
  3. Add flour and mix until you get a soft, slightly sticky dough.
  4. Sprinkle some flour on your hands, form small pancakes.
  5. Heat a pan with a little oil over medium heat.
  6. Fry the syrnyky for about 2-3 minutes per side until golden brown.
  7. Serve warm with sour cream, yogurt, or fruit.

Veggie Salad and Tuna

7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Main plate
  • 4-5 egg whites
  • A pinch of turmeric or your favorite spices
  • Canned tuna in water (about 100-120 g)
  • 2 crispbread slices (e.g., rye or whole grain)
  • Salad
  • 1 cucumber
  • 1 tomato
  • 1/2 bell pepper
  • A handful of fresh spinach
  • 1 tsp olive oil
  • Lemon juice or balsamic vinegar (optional)
  • Salt & pepper to taste

Method

  1. Egg Whites
  2. Separate the egg whites from the yolks (use 4-5 whites).
  3. Whisk lightly with a pinch of salt and turmeric.

Zucchini & Avocado Breakfast

4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 medium zucchini
  • 2 eggs
  • 1 ripe avocado
  • 1 tsp olive oil
  • Salt and black pepper - to taste
  • Chili flakes (optional)
  • A small handful of nuts (e.g. almonds, walnuts, or cashews - about 10-15 g)
  • 1 cup of tea (green, black, or herbal - your choice)

Method

  1. Prepare the zucchini
  2. Use a spiralizer (or a julienne peeler or knife) to cut the zucchini into long thin strips (zoodles).
  3. Sprinkle a little salt over them and let sit for 5-10 minutes.
  4. Squeeze out excess moisture using paper towels or a clean cloth.
  5. Make the nests
  6. Heat 1 tsp of olive oil in a non-stick pan over medium heat.
  7. Divide the zucchini into two portions and shape each into a small nest, leaving a space in the center.
  8. Carefully crack one egg into the center of each nest.
  9. Season with salt, pepper, and chili flakes if desired.
  10. Cover the pan with a lid and cook for 4-6 minutes, until the egg whites are set and the yolks are still runny (or longer if you prefer firmer yolks).
  11. Slice the avocado
  12. Cut the avocado in half, remove the pit, and slice thinly.
  13. Arrange neatly on the plate next to the nests.
  14. Add nuts
  15. Serve a small handful of raw or lightly toasted nuts on the side.
  16. Optional: sprinkle some crushed nuts on top of the avocado.
  17. Prepare the tea
  18. Brew your favorite tea.
  19. Drink plain, or add a slice of lemon or a touch of honey if you like.

Zucchini Egg Nests with Avocado

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 small zucchini
  • 1 small yellow squash (optional, for color)
  • 2 eggs
  • 1 ripe avocado
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • Chili flakes (optional)
  • Hemp seeds or sesame seeds for garnish
  • Olive oil for cooking

Method

  1. Prepare the vegetable noodles
  2. Use a spiralizer or a vegetable peeler to create thin strips from the zucchini and yellow squash.
  3. Make the nests
  4. Heat a little olive oil in a pan over medium heat.
  5. Form two small "nests" from the veggie strips in the pan.
  6. Add the eggs
  7. Crack one egg into the center of each nest.
  8. Cook until the egg whites are set but the yolk is still runny (or to your desired doneness). You can cover the pan with a lid to help the eggs cook evenly.
  9. Prepare the avocado
  10. Slice the avocado thinly and fan it out on the plate.
  11. Assemble and season
  12. Transfer the zucchini egg nests to a plate. Add the sliced avocado.
  13. Sprinkle with salt, pepper, chili flakes, and hemp seeds.

Zucchini Fritters with Cream Cheese and Cured Salmon

9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the fritters
  • 1 small zucchini (about 200 g)
  • 1 egg
  • 5-2 tbsp oat flour (or ground oats)
  • Fresh dill or green onion - to taste
  • Salt and pepper - to taste
  • A little olive oil for frying (optional if using a non-stick pan)
  • For serving
  • 1-2 tbsp cream cheese or Greek yogurt
  • 40-50 g lightly salted salmon or trout
  • ( If you don't like fish - you can skip it and just serve with cream cheese or add a boiled egg or avocado instead - still delicious!)

Method

  1. Grate the zucchini finely, sprinkle with a little salt, and let sit for 5 minutes. Then squeeze out the excess liquid.
  2. Add the egg, oat flour, chopped herbs, salt, and pepper. Mix until combined.
  3. Heat a non-stick pan and cook the fritters for 2-3 minutes on each side until golden brown.
  4. Serve with cream cheese (or Greek yogurt) and salmon (or one of the alternatives).
  5. Optionally, sprinkle with black pepper or dried herbs.

Zucchini Pancakes

3 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 zucchini
  • 4 tbsp rice flour (or wheat flour)
  • 1 egg
  • Herbs (to taste)
  • Salt and pepper (to taste)

Method

  1. Grate the zucchini finely and let it sit for 10-15 minutes to drain excess liquid.
  2. Add the egg, flour, herbs, salt, and pepper. Mix well.
  3. Heat a non-stick pan. For the first batch, grease the pan with coconut oil; afterward, you can cook without oil.
  4. Spoon the batter onto the pan and fry the pancakes on both sides until golden brown.
  5. for 1 serving (150-160g)

Zucchini Pancakes with Yogurt Sauce

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 small zucchini (150-200 g)
  • 1 egg
  • 1 tbsp oats or flour
  • Salt, pepper - to taste
  • 2 tbsp plain Greek yogurt

Method

  1. Grate the zucchini and squeeze out excess liquid.
  2. Mix with the egg, oats (or flour), and seasoning.
  3. Cook small pancakes in a non-stick pan with little or no oil until golden on both sides.

Baked Potatoes with Zucchini and Mushrooms

2 servings6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 300 g potatoes (2 medium potatoes)
  • 100 g zucchini (about 1/2 medium zucchini)
  • 100 g mushrooms (e.g., white button or cremini)
  • 60 g onion (about 1/2 of a small onion)
  • 2 garlic cloves
  • 2 tsp olive oil
  • 2 tbsp Greek yogurt (2%) - optional, for serving
  • Salt and pepper - to taste
  • Spices - paprika, rosemary, thyme - to taste
  • Fresh herbs (parsley, dill) - for serving

Method

  1. Preheat the oven to 200°C.
  2. Wash and peel the potatoes, then cut them into small cubes or wedges.
  3. Chop the zucchini and mushrooms into bite-sized pieces.
  4. Finely chop the onion and garlic.
  5. In a large bowl, combine all vegetables with olive oil, garlic, onion, salt, pepper, and your preferred spices.
  6. Spread the mixture evenly on a baking tray lined with parchment paper or lightly greased.
  7. Bake for 30-35 minutes, or until the potatoes are golden and cooked through.
  8. Optionally, serve with 1 tbsp of Greek yogurt per portion and sprinkle with fresh chopped herbs like parsley or dill.

Baked Sweet Potato with Crispy Chickpeas & Broccoli

4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Sweet potatoes - 2 medium
  • Cooked or canned chickpeas - 200 g
  • Broccoli - 200 g
  • Olive oil - 2 tbsp
  • Salt and pepper - to taste
  • Paprika, turmeric, or your favorite spices - to taste
  • Arugula or other greens - a handful (for serving)

Method

  1. Preheat the oven to 200°C.
  2. Wash the sweet potatoes, pierce them with a fork, and bake for 40-50 minutes until soft.
  3. Meanwhile, pat the chickpeas dry, mix with 1 tbsp olive oil, spices, and salt. Roast in the oven for 20-25 minutes until crispy (can be done alongside the sweet potatoes).
  4. Steam or lightly boil the broccoli for 5-7 minutes until bright green and slightly crunchy.
  5. Once the sweet potatoes are ready, cut them open lengthwise and gently mash the inside.
  6. Fill with crispy chickpeas, broccoli, and top with fresh greens.
  7. Drizzle with olive oil before serving.

Bowl with Chicken, Quinoa & Veggies

2 servings6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 250 g chicken breast (cut into cubes)
  • 170 g uncooked quinoa (≈420 g cooked)

  10. Assemble the bowls
  11. Divide quinoa, chicken, and veggies evenly between two bowls.

2 servings6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 120 g dry rice (≈ 300 g cooked)
  • 1 can tuna in water (≈ 200 g drained)
  • 100 g light mozzarella (or 125 g regular, divided into 2 portions)
  • 1 medium cucumber (≈ 150 g)
  • 6-8 cherry tomatoes (≈ 200 g)
  • 2 handfuls lettuce or kale (≈ 80-100 g)
  • Salt, pepper, lemon juice - to taste
  • 1 tsp olive oil (split between 2 portions)

Method

  1. Cook the rice and divide it into two bowls.
  2. Add tuna on top.
  3. Cut or tear mozzarella into small pieces and add to each bowl.
  4. Slice the cucumber, add cherry tomatoes and leafy greens.
  5. Season with salt, pepper, a drizzle of olive oil, and lemon juice.

Buckwheat + Chicken Patties + Fresh Cabbage Salad

9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the chicken patties
  • Chicken breast or minced chicken - 300 g
  • Onion - 50 g
  • Egg - 1 pc
  • Salt - 1/3 tsp
  • Pepper - to taste
  • Paprika or your favorite spices - 1/2 tsp
  • A little chopped dill or parsley (optional)
  • For the buckwheat
  • Buckwheat groats - 150 g (dry)
  • Water - 300 ml
  • Salt - a pinch
  • For the salad
  • White cabbage - 200 g
  • Carrot - 80 g
  • Fresh dill - 10 g
  • Lemon juice - 1 tbsp
  • Olive oil - 1-2 tsp (optional, for weight loss you can skip)
  • Salt - to taste

Method

  1. Make the chicken patties
  2. Blend chicken and onion or use ready minced chicken.
  3. Add egg, salt, pepper, spices, and greens.
  4. Form small patties with wet hands.
  5. Cook in one of the weight-loss-friendly ways
  6. Pan, no oil: on a non-stick pan for 4-5 min per side.
  7. Baked: 180°C for 20-25 min.
  8. Steamed: 20 minutes.
  9. (baked or braised)
  10. Cook the buckwheat
  11. Rinse the buckwheat.
  12. Bring 300 ml water to a boil, add buckwheat and a pinch of salt.
  13. Simmer on low heat for 15 minutes until water is absorbed.
  14. Leave covered for 5 minutes to fluff.
  15. Prepare the salad
  16. Thinly slice cabbage and grate the carrot.
  17. Add salt and lightly massage by hand to soften.
  18. Add lemon juice, dill, and optional 1-2 tsp olive oil.
  19. Mix.
  20. Assemble the plate
  21. Divide the buckwheat into 2 servings.
  22. Add 2-3 chicken patties to each plate.
  23. Add a generous portion of fresh cabbage salad.
  24. Top with a little chopped greens.

Buckwheat Power Bowl

2 servings11 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Buckwheat - 170 g dry (or ~420 g cooked)
  • Broccoli - 1 medium head, steamed
  • Avocado - 1 ripe, sliced
  • Fresh veggies
  • Iceberg lettuce - 1-2 handfuls, shredded
  • Red bell pepper - 1/2, sliced
  • Cherry tomatoes - a handful, halved
  • Soft cheese or tofu - 150 g (e.g. cottage cheese, feta, or plain tofu)
  • Optional replacement for eggs
  • 80 g mashed chickpeas with turmeric
  • or vegan scrambled tofu with broccoli bits

Method

  1. Cook the buckwheat
  2. Rinse 170 g of buckwheat and cook in 480 ml of water with a pinch of salt. Simmer for 15-20 minutes until tender. Let cool slightly.
  3. Steam the broccoli
  4. Cut broccoli into florets and steam for 5-6 minutes until just tender and bright green.
  5. Prepare fresh veggies
  6. Chop lettuce, bell pepper, and cherry tomatoes. Keep them crisp.
  7. Prepare creamy topping (instead of eggs)
  8. Option 1: Lightly mash chickpeas with a fork, mix with olive oil, turmeric, and a dash of garlic powder.
  9. Option 2: Crumble tofu and sauté with a bit of oil, turmeric, black pepper, and finely chopped steamed broccoli for a "scrambled" effect.
  10. Assemble each bowl
  11. Base: 170 g of cooked buckwheat
  12. Add: broccoli, sliced avocado, cheese/tofu, fresh salad mix
  13. Add the chickpea mash or tofu scramble in place of the egg
  14. Season with olive oil, lemon juice, salt & pepper

Buckwheat with Chicken & Fresh Vegetables

8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Chicken breast - 270g
  • Buckwheat - 100g (dry)
  • Tomatoes - 2 pcs
  • Cucumber - 1 pc
  • Avocado - 1/2 pc (optional)
  • Lettuce leaves - 1 handful
  • Red onion - 1/4 pc
  • Olive oil - 1 tbsp
  • Salt, pepper - to taste
  • Lemon juice or balsamic vinegar - a little, for dressing

Method

  1. Buckwheat. Rinse well, add 240 ml of water, a pinch of salt. Cook covered for 15-18 minutes until tender.
  2. Chicken. Cut chicken breast into pieces, season with salt and pepper. Pan-fry in a dry skillet or with 1/2 tbsp of olive oil until golden (7-10 minutes).
  3. Salad. Chop tomatoes, cucumber, avocado, lettuce, and onion. Dress with olive oil and lemon juice/vinegar.
  4. Plate the meal: buckwheat + chicken + salad.

Buckwheat with Chickpeas and Fresh Vegetables

Buckwheat with Eggs, Avocado & Fresh Cabbage Salad

7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 160 g buckwheat (dry)
  • 4 eggs
  • 1 ripe avocado
  • 1/4 head of cabbage
  • 1-2 tomatoes
  • Sauce of your choice (e.g. mustard + a little ketchup, or Greek yogurt)
  • Salt, pepper, olive oil

Method

  1. Buckwheat: Rinse and cook in 480 ml of water with a pinch of salt. Simmer for 15-20 minutes until tender.
  2. Eggs: Boil until hard (about 8-10 minutes), cool, peel, and cut into halves or quarters.
  3. Avocado: Slice or cube, lightly salt, and optionally drizzle with lemon juice.
  4. Salad: Shred the cabbage, cut the tomatoes into wedges, and mix together. Season with olive oil, salt, and pepper.
  5. Assemble each plate with portions of buckwheat, eggs, avocado with sauce, and the fresh salad.

Bulgur & Chickpea Salad Bowl

10 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 180 g cooked bulgur
  • 160 g cooked chickpeas (or canned, drained and rinsed)
  • 60 g mixed salad greens (lettuce, arugula, spinach)
  • 1 small cucumber, diced
  • 1 small carrot, grated
  • 6 cherry tomatoes, halved
  • 1/2 red bell pepper, diced
  • 1-2 tsp olive oil
  • 1 tsp lemon juice
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • Optional: fresh herbs (parsley, cilantro, or dill)

Method

  1. Cook bulgur according to package instructions. Let it cool slightly.
  2. In a bowl, mix bulgur and chickpeas.
  3. In a separate bowl, prepare the salad: combine salad greens, cucumber, carrot, cherry tomatoes, and bell pepper.
  4. Make a light dressing with olive oil, lemon juice, salt, and pepper. Toss the salad with the dressing.
  5. Serve half of the bulgur-chickpea mixture with half of the salad. Repeat for the second serving the next day.
  6. Optional: sprinkle fresh herbs on top for extra flavor.
  7. Tip: You can add 1 boiled egg or 50 g of feta cheese per portion for more protein if you like.

Bulgur Bowl with Chickpeas & Avocado

Ingredients

  • Cooked or canned chickpeas - 200 g
  • Bulgur (or quinoa/rice) - 120 g (dry weight)

Method

Bulgur, Braised Cabbage & Fresh Cucumber

7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the bulgur
  • Bulgur - 100 g (dry weight)
  • Water - 200 ml
  • Salt and pepper - to taste
  • Optional: 1 tsp olive oil
  • For the braised cabbage
  • White cabbage - 300-350 g
  • Carrot - 1 medium
  • Onion - 1 small
  • Tomato paste - 1 tbsp (or 1 grated fresh tomato)
  • Water - 50-70 ml
  • Oil - 1 tsp
  • Salt, pepper, bay leaf - to taste
  • For serving
  • Fresh cucumbers - 2 pcs (sliced into sticks)

Method

  1. Bulgur
  2. Rinse the bulgur, add water in a 1:2 ratio.
  3. Cook over medium heat for 10-12 minutes until tender. Add salt and a little olive oil if desired.
  4. Braised cabbage
  5. Shred the cabbage, grate the carrot, and finely chop the onion.
  6. Sauté the onion and carrot in 1 tsp of oil until soft.
  7. Add the cabbage, tomato paste or grated tomato, a splash of water, and spices. Cover with a lid.
  8. Simmer for 20-25 minutes over medium heat until the cabbage is soft and flavorful.
  9. Serving
  10. Plate the bulgur, top or side with braised cabbage, and add fresh cucumber sticks.

Bulgur, Cucumber & Braised Cabbage

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Bulgur base
  • 160 g dry bulgur
  • 320 ml water
  • A pinch of salt
  • Cucumber salad
  • 2 medium fresh cucumbers
  • 2 tsp olive oil
  • 1-2 tsp lemon juice or apple cider vinegar
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • Braised cabbage
  • 400 g white cabbage (about 1/2 a medium head)
  • 2 medium carrots
  • 1 small onion (optional)
  • 2 tsp olive oil
  • 2 tbsp tomato paste or juice (optional)
  • Salt, black pepper, bay leaf, favorite herbs (e.g. paprika, garlic powder)

Method

  1. Bulgur
  2. Bring water to a boil, add bulgur and a pinch of salt. Reduce heat, cover, and simmer for about 10-12 minutes until tender. Let it rest covered for a few minutes.
  3. Cucumber salad
  4. Slice the cucumbers, season with salt and pepper, drizzle with olive oil and lemon juice (or vinegar), then toss gently.
  5. Braised cabbage
  6. Shred the cabbage and grate the carrots. Finely chop the onion if using.
  7. In a large pan, heat olive oil. Sauté onion (if using), then add carrots and cabbage.
  8. Season with salt, pepper, herbs, and tomato paste or juice.
  9. Add a splash of water, cover, and simmer for 20-25 minutes on low heat, stirring occasionally, until soft.
  10. Serve
  11. Plate the bulgur, add a generous portion of braised cabbage and a side of cucumber salad.

Caesar Salad

8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 60 g romaine lettuce, chopped
  • 100 g grilled chicken breast, thinly sliced
  • 2 small tomatoes or 6-8 cherry tomatoes, halved
  • 1 small cucumber, sliced (optional, for extra volume)
  • 20 g Parmesan cheese, grated
  • 2 whole-wheat croutons (or 4 small ones per serving)
  • Dressing
  • 1 tbsp Greek yogurt
  • 1 tsp Dijon mustard
  • 1 tsp olive oil
  • 1 tsp lemon juice
  • 1 small garlic clove, minced
  • Salt and pepper to taste

Method

  1. Prepare the chicken
  2. Season chicken breast lightly with salt, pepper, and optional paprika.
  3. Grill or pan-cook for 5-7 minutes per side until fully cooked.
  4. Let cool slightly, then slice thinly.
  5. Make the dressing
  6. In a small bowl, mix Greek yogurt, Dijon mustard, olive oil, lemon juice, minced garlic, salt, and pepper until smooth.
  7. Assemble the salad
  8. In a large bowl, combine chopped romaine, tomatoes, cucumber, and croutons.
  9. Add sliced chicken.
  10. Drizzle with dressing and toss gently.
  11. Sprinkle with grated Parmesan.
  12. Serving & storage
  13. Serve immediately for best texture.
  14. If preparing for later, store components separately (lettuce, dressing, chicken) in the fridge and combine just before eating to avoid soggy lettuce.

Carbonara Pasta

2 servings8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 150 g whole wheat spaghetti
  • 100 g turkey bacon or lean pancetta, diced
  • 2 large eggs
  • 50 g grated Parmesan cheese (or Pecorino Romano)
  • 1 clove garlic, minced
  • 100 ml low-fat milk or unsweetened almond milk
  • Salt and black pepper to taste
  • Fresh parsley (optional, for garnish)

Method

  1. Cook the whole wheat spaghetti in salted boiling water according to package instructions until al dente. Reserve about 120 ml of pasta water, then drain the pasta.
  2. In a bowl, whisk together the eggs, grated cheese, milk, salt, and a generous amount of black pepper. Set aside.
  3. In a large pan, cook the diced turkey bacon over medium heat until crispy. Add the minced garlic and cook for another minute.
  4. Remove the pan from heat. Quickly add the hot pasta to the pan with the bacon and garlic, then pour the egg and cheese mixture over it.
  5. Toss everything together quickly, adding some reserved pasta water little by little to create a creamy sauce (without scrambling the eggs).
  6. Serve immediately, garnished with fresh parsley if desired.

Chicken & Potato Bowl with Fresh Salad

2 servings10 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 300 g potatoes, diced
  • 200 g chicken breast, diced
  • 1 tbsp olive oil
  • 1 tsp dried herbs (dill, parsley, oregano)
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • For the fresh salad
  • 1 small red bell pepper, sliced
  • 6 cherry tomatoes, quartered
  • A handful of mixed salad leaves
  • For the cabbage-corn salad
  • 200 g Chinese cabbage, shredded
  • 1 small cucumber, chopped
  • 50 g canned sweet corn (drained)
  • 2 tbsp Greek yogurt (or natural yogurt)
  • A pinch of salt and fresh herbs to taste

Method

  1. Preheat the oven to 180°C.
  2. Place diced potatoes and chicken in a baking dish. Drizzle with olive oil, season with salt, pepper, and herbs. Mix well and bake for 25-30 minutes until golden and cooked through.
  3. Prepare the fresh salad by arranging bell pepper, tomatoes, and salad leaves on the plate.
  4. Make the cabbage-corn salad by mixing cabbage, cucumber, corn, yogurt, and seasonings in a bowl.
  5. Divide baked potatoes and chicken between two plates, add both salads, and serve immediately.
  6. Pair with still water

Chicken & Roasted Vegetables Meal Prep

2 servings · ~500 kcal5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 300 g chicken breast, diced
  • 400 g baby potatoes, halved
  • 200 g broccoli florets
  • 150 g carrots, sliced
  • 100 g onion, chopped
  • 1 tbsp olive oil (15 ml)
  • 1 tsp garlic powder
  • 1 tsp paprika
  • Salt and black pepper to taste

Method

  1. Preheat the oven to 200°C.
  2. Toss the potatoes, carrots, broccoli, and onion with olive oil and seasonings.
  3. Spread the vegetables on a baking tray and roast for 25-30 minutes.
  4. Meanwhile, season the chicken and cook in a non-stick pan over medium heat for 8-10 minutes until golden and fully cooked.
  5. Divide the chicken and roasted vegetables evenly between two meal-prep containers.
