Chicken Spring Rolls

2 servings11 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 6-8 rice paper wrappers
  • 150 g cooked chicken breast (sliced into thin strips)
  • 1/2 carrot (cut into thin matchsticks)
  • 1/2 cucumber (cut into thin matchsticks)
  • 1/2 red bell pepper (thinly sliced)
  • A handful of lettuce or spinach leaves
  • A handful of fresh herbs (mint, cilantro, or basil)
  • 50 g rice noodles (optional, for extra fullness)
  • Light Dipping Sauce
  • 2 tbsp soy sauce
  • 1 tsp lime or lemon juice
  • 1 tsp rice vinegar (optional)
  • A pinch of chili flakes or fresh chili
  • 1 tsp sesame seeds (optional)

Method

  1. Cook the rice noodles (if using) according to package instructions, drain, and let cool.
  2. Prepare the vegetables: cut carrot, cucumber, and pepper into thin strips.
  3. Slice the cooked chicken breast into thin pieces.
  4. Soften a rice paper wrapper by dipping it in warm water for a few seconds.
  5. Place the wrapper on a plate and layer: a bit of lettuce, noodles, vegetables, herbs, and chicken.
  6. Fold the sides and roll tightly. Repeat with the rest.
  7. Serve fresh with the dipping sauce.

Chicken Stir-Fry with Bell Peppers, Broccoli & Rice

7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 chicken breast, sliced into thin strips
  • 1/2 red bell pepper, sliced
  • 1/2 green bell pepper, sliced
  • 1 small onion, sliced
  • 140 g broccoli florets
  • 1 tbsp soy sauce
  • 1 tsp sesame oil or any neutral oil
  • 1 garlic clove, minced
  • 1 tsp grated fresh ginger (optional)
  • Salt and pepper, to taste
  • 180 g uncooked jasmine or basmati rice
  • 480 ml water

Method

  1. Cook the rice
  2. Rinse the rice, then cook with 480 ml of water until tender (about 12-15 minutes).
  3. Fluff with a fork and set aside.
  4. Steam or blanch the broccoli
  5. In boiling water or a steamer for 2-3 minutes, until just tender but still bright green.
  6. Drain and set aside.
  7. Cook the chicken
  8. Heat half the oil in a large skillet or wok over medium-high heat.
  9. Add chicken strips, season with a pinch of salt and pepper, and cook until golden and fully cooked (5-6 minutes).
  10. Remove from the pan and set aside.
  11. Stir-fry the vegetables
  12. Add the rest of the oil to the pan.
  13. Sauté onion, garlic, and ginger for 1-2 minutes.
  14. Add bell peppers and cook until slightly softened (3-4 minutes).
  15. Combine everything
  16. Return the chicken to the pan.
  17. Add soy sauce and broccoli, stir well, and cook for another 1-2 minutes until warmed through.
  18. Serve
  19. Plate the rice, then top with the stir-fry mix.
  20. Optionally garnish with sesame seeds, green onions, or a drizzle of extra soy sauce.

Chicken Stir-Fry with Ginger & Vegetables

9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Chicken breast - 100 g (about 50 g per serving, or even less)
  • Carrot - 1 small (50 g)
  • Zucchini - 100 g
  • Bell pepper - 1/2 piece
  • Broccoli - 100 g
  • Green onion - 2-3 stalks
  • Fresh ginger - 1 small piece (optional)
  • Light soy sauce - 1 tbsp
  • Garlic - 1 clove
  • Shirataki noodles (or rice noodles, but shirataki are almost calorie-free) - 200 g cooked
  • Salt, pepper, spices - to taste
  • A drop of oil for frying (or use a non-stick pan)

Method

  1. Cut chicken into small cubes and quickly fry on a dry or lightly oiled pan.
  2. Add chopped vegetables (carrot, pepper, zucchini, broccoli) and garlic, sauté briefly.
  3. Add cooked shirataki noodles (they usually just need rinsing and heating).
  4. Season with soy sauce, ginger, and spices. Mix well.
  5. Serve hot, topped with chopped green onion.

Chicken with Buckwheat and Tomato-Mozzarella Salad

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the chicken
  • 300 g chicken breast, cubed
  • 1 tbsp olive oil or cooking oil
  • 1/2 tsp sweet paprika
  • 1/2 tsp garlic powder (optional)
  • Salt and black pepper, to taste
  • For the buckwheat
  • 100 g dry buckwheat
  • 200 ml water
  • Salt, to taste
  • For the tomato & mozzarella salad
  • 2 medium tomatoes, diced
  • 100 g mini mozzarella balls
  • 1 tbsp olive oil (optional)
  • 1/2 tsp dried oregano or basil
  • Salt and pepper, to taste

Method

  1. Cook the buckwheat
  2. Rinse the buckwheat under cold water.
  3. In a saucepan, bring the water to a boil. Add a pinch of salt.
  4. Add the buckwheat, reduce heat to low, cover with a lid.
  5. Simmer for 15-20 minutes, or until the water is fully absorbed.
  6. Remove from heat and let sit covered for another 5 minutes. Fluff with a fork before serving.
  7. Prepare the chicken
  8. Cut the chicken breast into bite-sized cubes.
  9. Season with paprika, garlic powder, salt, and pepper.
  10. Heat oil in a pan over medium heat.
  11. Cook the chicken for 6-8 minutes, stirring occasionally, until golden and fully cooked through.
  12. Make the salad
  13. Dice the tomatoes and halve or leave whole the mozzarella balls.
  14. Combine them in a bowl.
  15. Add salt, pepper, oregano or basil, and olive oil (if using).
  16. Toss gently and set aside.
  17. Serving
  18. Divide the cooked buckwheat evenly between two plates.
  19. Add half of the chicken to each plate.
  20. Serve with a generous portion of the tomato and mozzarella salad.

Chicken with Green Beans & Mashed Potatoes

2 servings7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the chicken and vegetables
  • 400 g chicken breast, cut into pieces
  • 120 g green beans
  • 15-20 g oil
  • Salt, pepper, and spices to taste
  • For the mashed potatoes
  • 600 g potatoes
  • 20-30 g butter
  • 40-50 g warm milk
  • Salt to taste
  • For the sides
  • 200 g sauerkraut
  • 150-200 g boiled diced beets (optional)

Method

  1. Cook the chicken
  2. Heat the oil in a pan. Add the chicken, season with salt, pepper, and spices.
  3. Cook until browned, then add the green beans and sauté until soft.
  4. Make the mashed potatoes
  5. Peel and boil the potatoes until tender.
  6. Mash with butter, milk, and salt until smooth.
  7. Prepare the sides
  8. Portion out the sauerkraut. Add the optional boiled beets.
  9. Assemble the plates
  10. Divide the chicken with vegetables, mashed potatoes, sauerkraut, and optional beets into two servings.

Chicken with Mashed Potatoes and Fresh Salad

2 servings9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Main
  • Skinless chicken thighs or fillets - 400 g
  • Potatoes - 500 g
  • Milk 1-2.5% or cream - 70 ml
  • Butter - 10 g
  • Salt, pepper, bay leaf
  • Salad
  • White cabbage - 300 g
  • Carrot - 1 medium (80 g)
  • Cucumber (optional) - 1 pc
  • Olive oil - 1 tbsp. l. (10 g)
  • Lemon juice or apple cider vinegar

Method

  1. Chicken
  2. Bake at 180°C for 35-40 min or simmer under the lid until cooked.
  3. Season with salt, pepper, and you can add paprika and garlic.
  4. Mashed potatoes
  5. Boil potatoes until soft.
  6. Drain water, add hot milk and butter.
  7. Mash until creamy.
  8. Salad
  9. Shred cabbage, grate carrots.
  10. Add oil, lemon juice, a pinch of salt, mash well with your hands.

Chicken with Pesto Pasta

2 servings7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the chicken
  • 2 chicken breasts (about 300-350 g total)
  • 1 tsp olive oil
  • Salt, pepper, dried basil or garlic to taste
  • For the pasta
  • 120 g dry whole wheat penne pasta (or regular if preferred)
  • 1/2 tsp salt (for boiling water)
  • For the pesto
  • 2 handfuls fresh basil leaves
  • 2 tbsp olive oil
  • 1-2 tbsp grated Parmesan or any hard cheese
  • 1-2 tsp pine nuts or walnuts
  • 1 small garlic clove
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • 1-2 tbsp water (to lighten the sauce)

Method

  1. Chicken
  2. Season the chicken breasts with salt, pepper, and herbs.
  3. Heat a non-stick pan with olive oil and cook the chicken for about 5-6 minutes per side until golden and cooked through.
  4. Let it rest for a couple of minutes, then slice into strips.
  5. Pasta
  6. Cook the pasta in salted water until al dente, then drain.
  7. Pesto Sauce
  8. Blend all pesto ingredients until smooth.
  9. Add water if needed to make the sauce lighter and creamier.
  10. Assemble
  11. Mix the cooked pasta with the pesto sauce.
  12. Top with sliced grilled chicken.
  13. Garnish with a little extra grated cheese if desired.

  5. After: 1 fruit of your choice (orange, kiwi, apple, pear)

Chicken with Potatoes & Fresh Vegetables

7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 270 g chicken breast
  • 250 g potatoes
  • 1-2 tbsp olive oil (for mashed potatoes and salad)
  • 1 bell pepper
  • 2 cucumbers
  • 2-3 tomatoes
  • A handful of arugula or mixed greens
  • Salt, pepper, spices to taste

Method

  1. Chicken
  2. Cut the chicken breast into small cubes or strips.
  3. Season with salt, pepper, and your favorite spices.
  4. Pan-fry on a non-stick skillet with minimal oil, or bake in the oven at 180°C (~180°C) for 15-20 minutes.
  5. Mashed potatoes
  6. Boil the potatoes until tender.
  7. Mash them with a little olive oil and some hot water instead of butter/milk (for a lighter, PP-friendly version).
  8. Add salt to taste.
  9. Salad
  10. Chop the tomatoes, cucumbers, and bell pepper.
  11. Add arugula or mixed greens.
  12. Dress with a few drops of olive oil and, optionally, some lemon juice.
  13. Serving
  14. Divide into two plates: a portion of mashed potatoes, fresh salad, and pieces of chicken.

Chicken with Rice & Steamed Vegetables

7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 150 g uncooked rice (preferably brown or jasmine)
  • 250 g chicken breast, cut into small cubes
  • 300 g mixed vegetables (broccoli, carrots, bell pepper, peas - fresh or frozen)
  • 1 tbsp olive oil or avocado oil
  • Salt, black pepper, garlic powder, and herbs (oregano, basil, or paprika) - to taste
  • Optional: a squeeze of lemon juice or a few drops of soy sauce for flavor

Method

  1. Cook the rice according to package instructions until soft and fluffy.
  2. Prepare the chicken
  3. Heat olive oil in a non-stick pan.
  4. Add chicken cubes, season with salt, pepper, and spices.
  5. Cook for 6-8 minutes, stirring occasionally, until golden and fully cooked.
  6. Add vegetables
  7. In the same pan, add the mixed vegetables (they can be frozen).
  8. Cover and let them simmer for 7-10 minutes until soft but not overcooked.
  9. Combine everything
  10. Mix chicken with the vegetables or serve them side by side.
  11. Add a little lemon juice or soy sauce for extra flavor.

Chicken with Roasted Sweet Potato & Guacamole

8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the Chicken
  • Chicken breast - 300 g
  • Olive oil - 10 g (2 tsp)
  • Salt - 2 g
  • Ground black pepper - 1 g
  • Paprika (optional) - 1 g
  • Garlic powder or granulated garlic - 1 g
  • For the Roasted Sweet Potato
  • Sweet potato - 400 g
  • Olive oil - 10 g
  • Salt - 2 g
  • Paprika - 1 g
  • Italian herbs or oregano - 1 g
  • For the Guacamole
  • Avocado - 1 piece (about 150 g of flesh)
  • Cherry tomatoes - 80 g (about 8 pcs)
  • Red onion - 30 g
  • Lemon juice - 10 g (1 tbsp)
  • Salt - to taste
  • Fresh cilantro or parsley - 5 g (optional)
  • For Serving
  • Cherry tomatoes - another 80 g (halved or quartered)
  • Fresh herbs (cilantro or parsley) - 5 g

Method

  1. Chicken
  2. Finely dice the chicken breast.
  3. Mix with olive oil and spices, let marinate for 10-15 minutes.
  4. Sauté in a hot pan for 6-8 minutes until golden and cooked through.
  5. Sweet Potato
  6. Cut into fries or wedges.
  7. Toss with olive oil and seasoning.
  8. Roast in the oven at 200°C for about 25 minutes, flipping once.
  9. Guacamole
  10. Mash the avocado with a fork.
  11. Add diced onion, chopped cherry tomatoes, lemon juice, salt, and herbs. Mix well.
  12. To Serve
  13. Arrange the chicken, roasted sweet potato, and fresh cherry tomatoes on a plate.
  14. Serve guacamole in a small bowl on the side.

Chickpea-Spinach Patties with Rice and Steamed Frozen Vegetables

2 servings12 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the chickpea-spinach patties (6-8 small patties)
  • 1 can chickpeas (240 g drained)
  • 1 small onion (50 g), finely chopped
  • 1 clove garlic, minced
  • 1 handful fresh spinach (40-50 g), chopped
  • 1 egg (or 1 tbsp flaxseed + 3 tbsp water for vegan version)
  • 2-3 tbsp oat flour or breadcrumbs
  • 1/2 tsp salt
  • Black pepper to taste
  • Optional: paprika, turmeric, or dried herbs
  • For the rice
  • 150 g uncooked white rice
  • 300-330 ml water
  • Pinch of salt
  • For the vegetables
  • 300-350 g mixed frozen vegetables (broccoli, cauliflower, carrots, romanesco)

Method

  1. Make the patties
  2. Mash the chickpeas in a bowl (or blend lightly, leaving some texture).
  3. Add chopped onion, garlic, spinach, salt, pepper, and spices.
  4. Mix in the egg and flour/breadcrumbs until the mixture holds together.
  5. Form small patties with wet hands.
  6. Pan-fry on a lightly oiled pan for 3-4 minutes per side, or bake at 190°C for 15-20 min until golden.
  7. Cook the rice
  8. Rinse the rice under cold water.
  9. Add it to a pot with water and a pinch of salt.
  10. Bring to a boil, reduce heat, cover, and cook 12-15 minutes.
  11. Turn off the heat and let rest 5 minutes.
  12. Cook the vegetables
  13. Bring a pot of water to a boil.
  14. Add the frozen vegetables and cook 4-6 minutes until tender but not mushy.
  15. Drain and season lightly with salt.
  16. Assemble
  17. Place rice on each plate, add steamed vegetables, then 3-4 patties.
  18. Serve with a drizzle of olive oil, Greek yogurt sauce, or soy sauce if desired.

Couscous with Chicken & Vegetables

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 100 g couscous (dry)
  • 200 ml water or chicken broth
  • 1 small broccoli, cut into small florets
  • 1 medium carrot, diced or julienned
  • 1 small bell pepper, diced
  • 200 g chicken breast, baked and finely chopped
  • 1 tbsp olive oil
  • Salt and pepper, to taste
  • Optional: garlic, paprika, or your favorite spices

Method

  1. Prepare the couscous
  2. Bring 200 ml of water or chicken broth to a boil.
  3. Place the couscous in a bowl and pour the hot liquid over it.
  4. Cover and let it sit for 5 minutes, then fluff with a fork.
  5. Cook the vegetables
  6. In a pan, heat 1 tbsp of olive oil over medium heat.
  7. Add diced carrot and bell pepper, sauté for 3-4 minutes.
  8. Add broccoli florets and cook for another 3-5 minutes until tender but still crisp.
  9. Prepare the chicken
  10. Bake chicken breast (seasoned with salt, pepper, and optional spices) at 180°C for 20-25 minutes or until fully cooked.
  11. Let it cool slightly, then chop finely.
  12. Combine everything
  13. Mix the cooked couscous, sautéed vegetables, and chopped chicken in a large bowl.
  14. Adjust seasoning with salt, pepper, and spices.
  15. Storage for two days
  16. Divide into two airtight containers.
  17. Store in the fridge for up to 2 days.
  18. Reheat gently in a microwave or on a pan before eating.

Glass Noodles with Chicken & Mushrooms

2 servings10 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 200 g chicken breast (cut into strips)
  • 100 g glass noodles (dry)
  • 1 red bell pepper (sliced)
  • 150 g mushrooms (any type, sliced)
  • 2 tbsp soy sauce (low sodium)
  • 1 tsp olive oil (for frying)
  • 1 tbsp sesame seeds
  • Spices: garlic powder, black pepper, paprika
  • For the fresh salad
  • 1 cucumber
  • 2 medium tomatoes
  • 100 g lettuce leaves
  • A pinch of dried herbs (oregano or basil)

Method

  1. Cook the glass noodles according to package instructions, then rinse with cold water.
  2. Heat olive oil in a pan, add chicken breast, and cook until golden.
  3. Add bell pepper and mushrooms, stir-fry for a few minutes.
  4. Add soy sauce, spices, and the cooked noodles. Toss well.
  5. Serve with fresh salad: sliced cucumber, tomatoes, and lettuce. Sprinkle herbs on top.
  6. Garnish the noodles with sesame seeds before serving.

Healthy Chicken & Vegetable Risotto

2 servings10 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Arborio rice (or short-grain rice) - 120 g
  • Chicken breast - 200 g
  • Onion - 1/2 piece
  • Carrot - 1 small
  • Bell pepper - 1/2 piece
  • Zucchini or broccoli - 100 g
  • Olive oil - 1 tbsp
  • Vegetable or chicken broth (or water) - 400-450 ml
  • Garlic - 1 clove
  • Salt & pepper - to taste
  • Turmeric or paprika - optional
  • Fresh herbs (parsley, basil) - for serving
  • A small amount of hard cheese (like Parmesan) - optional for clean eating, can be skipped

Method

  1. Dice the chicken and chop all vegetables.
  2. In a deep pan, heat the olive oil and sauté the chopped onion and garlic until soft.
  3. Add the chicken and cook for about 5-6 minutes until golden.
  4. Add the carrot, bell pepper, and zucchini (or broccoli), and sauté for another 3-4 minutes.
  5. Stir in the rice and mix well to coat it with the flavors.
  6. Gradually add hot broth (about 100-150 ml at a time), stirring constantly. Let the rice absorb the liquid before adding more.
  7. Cook for 20-25 minutes until the rice is soft and creamy.
  8. Season with salt, pepper, and spices. Add cheese at the end if using.
  9. Serve warm, topped with chopped fresh herbs.

Healthy Chicken Pasta in Creamy Tomato Sauce

2 servings9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Whole grain pasta (rigatoni, penne, or fusilli) - 150 g (dry weight)
  • Chicken breast (finely diced) - 250 g
  • Onion - 1 medium
  • Garlic - 1 clove
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp
  • Tomato puree or canned tomatoes - 200 ml
  • Greek yogurt (2-5%) or light cream (10%) - 2-3 tbsp
  • Parmesan cheese (grated or shaved) - 20 g
  • Salt, black pepper, paprika, garlic powder, Italian herbs - to taste
  • Fresh herbs (parsley, basil) - for topping

Method

  1. Boil the pasta in salted water until al dente. Drain and save 2-3 tbsp of the cooking water.
  2. In a pan, sauté the chopped onion in olive oil for about 2 minutes until soft.
  3. Add garlic and diced chicken, cook until fully cooked and golden (~5-6 minutes).
  4. Pour in the tomato puree, add spices, and simmer for 5 minutes.
  5. Stir in the Greek yogurt or cream and mix well into a creamy sauce.
  6. Add the cooked pasta and a bit of the reserved pasta water. Stir and simmer together for 1-2 more minutes.
  7. Serve topped with Parmesan and fresh herbs.

Healthy Gazpacho

2 servings6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Ripe tomatoes - 4 medium
  • Cucumber - 1/2 piece
  • Red bell pepper - 1/2 piece
  • Garlic - 1 small clove
  • Red onion - 1/4 piece
  • Extra virgin olive oil - 1 tbsp
  • White wine vinegar (or apple cider vinegar) - 1 tsp
  • Cold water - 100-150 ml (adjust for consistency)
  • Salt - to taste
  • Black pepper - to taste
  • Ice cubes - optional, for instant chilling

Method

  1. Blanch the tomatoes in boiling water for 30 seconds, then peel off the skins.
  2. Roughly chop all the vegetables (no need to be precise - everything will be blended).
  3. Add chopped veggies to a blender with garlic, olive oil, vinegar, salt, pepper, and water.
  4. Blend until smooth. For a silkier texture, strain through a fine mesh sieve (optional).
  5. Chill in the refrigerator for 1-2 hours or add ice cubes if you want it cold right away.
  6. Serve in bowls or glasses. Garnish with diced veggies, herbs, or a small drizzle of olive oil.
  7. For added protein, serve with a boiled egg or whole-grain toast with hummus.

Ingredients

Method

  2. Add grated carrot, chopped onion, egg, spices, and oat flour. Mix well.
  3. Shape into 4 patties. Bake at 180°C for 20-25 minutes or pan-fry on a non-stick skillet without oil.
  4. Buckwheat
  5. Dry buckwheat - 80 g
  6. Water - 160 ml
  7. Salt - to taste
  8. Preparation: Rinse, then simmer in water with a pinch of salt for ~15 minutes until soft.
  9. Fresh Veggie Salad

  11. Carrot - 30 g
  12. Cucumber - 70 g
  13. Olive oil - 1 tsp per serving
  14. Lemon juice or apple cider vinegar - 1 tsp
  15. Salt - to taste
  16. Preparation: Shred or grate all veggies, mix, and dress with oil and lemon juice/vinegar.

Ingredients

  • 1/2 fresh cucumber

  • 1/2 tsp lemon juice
  • Salt and pepper to taste

Method

  3. Drain the tuna and corn.
  4. Combine everything in a bowl and top with avocado slices if using.

Herb-Roasted Potatoes with Cherry Tomatoes & Cheese

2 servings4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Potatoes - 500-600 g (about 3-4 medium)
  • Cherry tomatoes - 150 g
  • Hard cheese (e.g. parmesan, gouda, gruyère) - 50-60 g
  • Olive oil - 1.5 to 2 tbsp
  • Dried oregano - 1/2 tsp
  • Dried basil - 1/2 tsp
  • Salt & pepper - to taste
  • Garlic powder or 1 garlic clove - optional
  • Fresh herbs (parsley, basil) - for garnish

Method

  1. Prepare the potatoes
  2. Wash them (no need to peel), and cut into wedges. Place them on a baking tray lined with parchment paper.
  3. Season
  4. Drizzle with olive oil, then sprinkle with salt, pepper, dried herbs, and garlic (if using). Toss to coat evenly.
  5. Bake - Step 1
  6. Roast in a preheated oven at 200°C for about 25-30 minutes, until golden and tender.
  7. Add cherry tomatoes & cheese
  8. Cut cherry tomatoes in halves and scatter them over the potatoes. Sprinkle with grated cheese.
  9. Bake - Step 2
  10. Return to oven for another 5-7 minutes, just until the cheese melts and tomatoes soften slightly.
  11. Serve
  12. Garnish with fresh herbs and enjoy warm or at room temperature

Lean Chicken Plate with Brown Rice, Broccoli, Cucumber & Cherry Tomatoes

2 servings5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 medium chicken breast (~300 g), skinless
  • 120 g brown rice (dry weight)
  • 1 medium broccoli head (~250 g)
  • 1 small cucumber
  • 10-12 cherry tomatoes
  • 1 tsp olive oil (for cooking)
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • Optional: garlic powder, paprika, chili flakes

Method

  1. Cook the rice
  2. Rinse 120 g of brown rice under cold water.
  3. Add to a pot with 2.5x water (about 300 ml).
  4. Bring to a boil, reduce heat, cover, and simmer for ~30-35 minutes until tender. Drain if needed.
  5. Cook the chicken
  6. Cut the chicken breast into bite-sized cubes.
  7. Heat 1 tsp olive oil in a non-stick pan.
  8. Add the chicken, season with salt, pepper, and optional spices.
  9. Cook on medium heat for 8-10 minutes, stirring occasionally, until fully cooked.
  10. Steam or boil the broccoli
  11. Cut into florets and steam/boil for ~5-6 minutes until bright green and tender but not mushy.
  12. Season lightly with salt or a squeeze of lemon juice.
  13. Prepare the veggies
  14. Slice the cucumber.
  15. Halve the cherry tomatoes.
  16. Assemble the plates
  17. Divide rice, chicken, and broccoli evenly onto two plates.
  18. Add fresh cucumber slices and cherry tomatoes on the side.

Light Chicken & Vegetable Paella

~320-350 kcal10 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Chicken breast - 200 g (100 g per serving)
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp (5 g)
  • Onion - 50 g
  • Garlic - 1 clove
  • Bell pepper - 100 g
  • Cherry tomatoes - 100 g
  • Zucchini - 100 g
  • Carrot - 50 g
  • Rice (preferably basmati or brown) - 80 g (dry)
  • Chicken broth or water - 250 ml
  • Turmeric or saffron - for color
  • Salt and black pepper - to taste
  • Fresh herbs (parsley, basil) - for garnish

Method

  1. Slice the chicken breast into thin strips, season with salt and pepper.
  2. Heat 1 tsp of olive oil in a non-stick pan, sear the chicken until golden, then set aside.
  3. In the same pan, sauté chopped onion, garlic, carrot, zucchini, and bell pepper until slightly soft.
  4. Add rice, stir well, then pour in chicken broth. Season with turmeric/saffron, salt, and pepper.
  5. Simmer on low heat for about 15-18 minutes, until rice is tender.
  6. Return the chicken to the pan, add cherry tomatoes, and cook for another 2-3 minutes.
  7. Sprinkle with fresh herbs before serving.
  8. About 320-350 kcal per serving,

Lunch Bowl with Fish, Roasted Veggies & Arugula

2 servings8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • White fish fillet (cod, hake, or tilapia) - 350-400 g
  • Baby potatoes - 200 g
  • Cherry tomatoes - 200 g
  • Arugula - 60 g
  • Red onion - 40 g
  • Capers - 15 g
  • Olive oil - 20 g (≈ 11/2 tbsp)
  • Lemon juice - 5 g (≈ 1 tsp)
  • Salt - 2 g
  • Black pepper - 1 g
  • Garlic powder - 1 g
  • Dried herbs (oregano or thyme) - 1 g
  • Fresh dill or parsley - 5 g

Method

  1. Prepare the fish
  2. Rinse and pat dry 400 g white fish fillet.
  3. Rub with 1/2 tbsp olive oil (5 g), lemon juice, salt, pepper, garlic powder, and herbs.
  4. Let it marinate for 10-15 minutes.
  5. Roast the vegetables
  6. Preheat oven to 200°C.
  7. Cut 200 g baby potatoes in halves.
  8. Place potatoes on a tray, drizzle with 1/2 tbsp olive oil (5 g), season with salt and pepper.
  9. Roast for 15 minutes.
  10. Add 200 g cherry tomatoes and roast together for another 10-12 minutes.
  11. Cook the fish
  12. Heat a non-stick pan with 10 g olive oil.
  13. Sear fish for 3-4 minutes per side, until golden and flaky.
  14. Assemble the bowls
  15. Divide 60 g arugula and 40 g red onion between two plates.
  16. Add roasted potatoes, cherry tomatoes, and fish.
  17. Top with 15 g capers and 5 g chopped herbs.
  18. Drizzle with a few drops of lemon juice before serving.

Lunch Bowl with Grilled Chicken, Roasted Veggies & Arugula

2 servings8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Chicken breast - 400 g
  • Baby potatoes - 200 g
  • Cherry tomatoes - 200 g
  • Arugula - 60 g
  • Red onion - 40 g
  • Capers - 15 g
  • Olive oil - 20 g (≈ 11/2 tbsp)
  • Lemon juice - 5 g (≈ 1 tsp)
  • Salt - 2 g (a pinch, to taste)
  • Black pepper - 1 g
  • Dried Italian herbs (optional) - 1 g
  • Fresh dill or parsley - 5 g (for garnish)

Method

  1. Prepare the ingredients
  2. Rinse and pat dry the chicken breasts (400 g).
  3. Marinate with 10 g olive oil, 5 g lemon juice, salt, pepper, and Italian herbs for about 15-20 minutes.
  4. Wash 200 g baby potatoes and cut them in halves.
  5. Slice 200 g cherry tomatoes and 40 g red onion thinly.
  6. Roast the vegetables
  7. Preheat the oven to 200 °C.
  8. Place the potatoes on a baking tray, drizzle with 5 g olive oil, season with salt and pepper, and roast for 15 minutes.
  9. Then add the tomatoes and roast together for another 10-12 minutes, until soft and golden.
  10. Cook the chicken
  11. Grill or pan-fry the marinated chicken breasts for 5-6 minutes per side, until fully cooked (internal temperature ~75 °C).
  12. Let rest for 2-3 minutes, then slice.
  13. Assemble the bowls
  14. Divide 60 g arugula and 40 g red onion between two plates.
  15. Add roasted potatoes, tomatoes, and sliced chicken.
  16. Top with 15 g capers and 5 g fresh herbs.
  17. Drizzle with the remaining 5 g olive oil before serving.

Paella

2 servings11 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Olive oil - 1 to 1.5 tbsp
  • Onion - 1/2 medium
  • Garlic - 1-2 cloves
  • Bell pepper (any color) - 1 medium
  • Carrot - 1/2 medium
  • Green beans or peas - 50 g
  • Tomatoes (grated or chopped) - 1 medium
  • Paella rice or short-grain rice - 125 g
  • Vegetable broth or water - 300-350 ml
  • Saffron threads or turmeric - a pinch
  • Salt - to taste
  • Black pepper - to taste
  • Paprika (smoked or sweet) - 1/2 tsp
  • Lemon - for serving

Method

  1. Prepare the vegetables
  2. Finely chop the onion.
  3. Mince the garlic.
  4. Slice the bell pepper into strips.
  5. Dice or julienne the carrot.
  6. Cut green beans into 2 cm (about 1 inch) pieces.
  7. Sauté
  8. Heat the olive oil in a wide pan or paella pan over medium heat.
  9. Add the onion and garlic, sauté until translucent, about 3-4 minutes.
  10. Add carrot, bell pepper, and green beans, cook for another 5 minutes.
  11. Add rice and spices
  12. Stir in the rice and paprika, coating the rice well with oil and spices.
  13. Pour in the vegetable broth or water, add saffron or turmeric, salt, and pepper.
  14. Cook the paella
  15. Bring to a boil, then reduce heat to low.
  16. Cook uncovered without stirring until the rice absorbs the liquid and is tender, about 15-20 minutes.
  17. About 5 minutes before done, add the grated or chopped tomatoes gently on top and mix lightly.
  18. Serve
  19. Remove from heat, cover with a clean towel, and let rest for 5 minutes.
  20. Serve with lemon wedges.

Pasta Carbonara

2 servings · ~330-360 kcal6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Whole grain / chickpea / durum wheat pasta - 120 g (dry weight)
  • Egg - 1
  • Egg whites - 2
  • Hard cheese (e.g., light Parmesan or Grana Padano) - 30-40 g, grated
  • Turkey breast or lean ham - 60-80 g, diced
  • Garlic - 1 clove (optional)
  • Salt and black pepper - to taste
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp (optional)
  • Fresh herbs - optional, for garnish
  • Calories (per serving): ~330-360 kcal
  • Total calories (for 2 servings): ~660-720 kcal

Method

  1. Cook the pasta until al dente. Reserve a few tablespoons of the pasta water.
  2. In a non-stick pan, sauté the turkey or ham (and garlic if using) until lightly golden.
  3. In a bowl, whisk together the whole egg, egg whites, and grated cheese. Add black pepper to taste.
  4. Add the cooked pasta to the pan, remove from heat, and pour in the egg mixture. Mix quickly and thoroughly to create a creamy texture. Add a bit of reserved pasta water if needed.
  5. Serve immediately, garnished with herbs, extra cheese, or black pepper if desired.

Pasta Salad with Pesto, Spinach & Cheese

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Pasta (fusilli or penne) - 120 g (dry weight)
  • Mozzarella balls - 100 g
  • Feta cheese - 60-80 g
  • Cherry tomatoes - 150 g
  • Fresh spinach - 2 large handfuls (about 50 g)
  • Pesto (store-bought or homemade) - 2 tbsp
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp (optional)
  • Salt & pepper - to taste

Method

  1. Cook the pasta in salted water until al dente. Drain and let it cool completely.
  2. Cut feta into small cubes, halve the cherry tomatoes.
  3. In a large bowl, combine the cooled pasta, tomatoes, spinach, mozzarella, and feta.
  4. Add pesto (and olive oil, if using), mix well. Season with salt and pepper.
  5. Done! Store in the fridge in an airtight container. Eat cold or at room temperature.

Pasta with Pesto & Finely Chopped Chicken (2-Day Meal Prep)

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 150 g whole wheat pasta (instead of regular pasta - for more fiber and fewer calories)
  • 250 g chicken breast (reduce portion size)
  • 1 tbsp olive oil (instead of 2 tbsp)
  • Salt & pepper to taste
  • 1 garlic clove (optional)
  • For the pesto
  • 20 g fresh basil (a bit less)
  • 15 g nuts (pine nuts, walnuts, or cashews) - halve the amount
  • 1 small garlic clove
  • 30 ml olive oil (instead of 50 ml)
  • 1 tbsp nutritional yeast or 15 g low-fat cheese (optional)
  • Salt & pepper to taste

Method

  1. Make the pesto by blending basil, nuts, garlic, olive oil, salt, pepper, and nutritional yeast or cheese until smooth.
  2. Cook whole wheat pasta in salted water until al dente. Drain, reserving 2-3 tbsp pasta water.
  3. Finely dice the chicken breasts. Sauté in 1 tbsp olive oil with salt, pepper, and optional garlic until golden and fully cooked (about 8-10 minutes).
  4. In a large bowl, combine pasta, pesto, a splash of pasta water, and chicken. Toss well until evenly coated.

Pasta with Tomatoes & Chicken

2 servings8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Cherry tomatoes - 65 g
  • Chicken thigh fillet - 190 g
  • Garlic - 2.5 g (about 1 small clove)
  • Pasta (orzo or other small pasta) - 80 g
  • Tomato paste - 20 g
  • Cream (10%) - 75 g
  • Vegetable oil - 2.5 g
  • Cheese (grated) - 25 g
  • Fresh spinach - 35 g
  • Fresh herbs, salt, pepper - to taste

Method

  1. Heat a pan and lightly grease it with oil.
  2. Slice the garlic and halve the cherry tomatoes. Sauté them in the pan.
  3. Cook the pasta 2 minutes less than the package instructions.
  4. Cut the chicken into small cubes. Add it to the pan with tomatoes, along with fresh spinach and tomato paste. Simmer for about 10 minutes, until the chicken is fully cooked.
  5. Add the grated cheese, cream, seasonings, salt, and the partially cooked pasta.
  6. Cook for another 3-4 minutes on low heat, until the cheese melts and the sauce thickens.
  7. After the meal
  8. 1 fruit (e.g., apple)

Pasta with Tomatoes & Mozzarella

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 140 g dry pasta
  • 300 g tomato sauce/passata
  • 300 g cherry tomatoes
  • 150 g mozzarella
  • 10 g olive oil
  • 1-2 cloves of garlic
  • Fresh basil
  • Salt and pepper to taste

Method

  1. Cook the pasta according to the package instructions until al dente.
  2. Heat the olive oil in a pan, add the garlic and cherry tomatoes, and cook for a few minutes.
  3. Add the tomato sauce, salt and pepper, and simmer for 5-7 minutes.
  4. Add the cooked pasta and mix everything together. You can add a little pasta water to make the sauce creamier.
  5. Remove from the heat and add the mozzarella and fresh basil.
  6. Divide the finished pasta into 2 equal portions - one for today and one for tomorrow.
  7. Snack after lunch
  8. If you feel hungry between lunch and dinner, you can have
  9. 150 g Greek yogurt + 1 banana
  10. 150 g protein yogurt + 1 banana.

Pasta with Tuna & Fresh Mixed Salad

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the pasta with tuna
  • Whole-wheat pasta (dry) - 120 g
  • Tuna in water (drained) - 160 g
  • Cherry tomatoes - 150 g

  • Garlic - 1 small clove

  • Dried oregano or basil - optional
  • For the light mixed salad
  • Mixed salad greens - 100 g
  • Cucumber - 150 g
  • Lemon juice - to taste
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp (5 g)
  • Salt - to taste

Method

  1. Cook the pasta in salted water according to package instructions. Drain and set aside.
  2. Heat olive oil in a pan, lightly sauté the garlic, add tuna and halved cherry tomatoes. Cook for 2-3 minutes.

  5. Divide the pasta and salad evenly between two plates.

Protein Salad with Jamón, Poached Egg & Chickpeas

2 servings8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 large handfuls of arugula
  • 100 g cherry tomatoes, halved
  • 4 slices of Jamón Serrano or Prosciutto (~50 g)
  • 2 eggs
  • 1/2 ripe avocado, sliced
  • 100 g canned chickpeas (drained and rinsed)
  • 1 tsp olive oil
  • 1 tsp balsamic vinegar or lemon juice
  • Salt & pepper to taste
  • Optional: a few parmesan shavings or pumpkin seeds for crunch

Method

  1. Prepare the eggs (poached)
  2. Bring water to a gentle simmer in a saucepan.
  3. Add a splash of vinegar (optional, helps keep egg shape).
  4. Crack one egg into a small bowl, swirl the water, and gently drop the egg in.
  5. Poach for ~3 minutes, remove with a slotted spoon. Repeat with the second egg.
  6. Assemble the salad
  7. In a large bowl, mix arugula, cherry tomatoes, avocado slices, and chickpeas.
  8. Drizzle with olive oil and balsamic (or lemon juice), toss gently.
  9. Add salt and pepper to taste.
  10. Plate the dish
  11. Divide salad between two plates.
  12. Drape slices of jamón on top.
  13. Place one poached egg in the center of each salad.
  14. Sprinkle with seeds or parmesan if using.

Quinoa Bowl with Chickpeas & Veggies

2 servings7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 85 g dry quinoa (yields about 260 g cooked)
  • 80 g cooked or canned chickpeas (rinsed and drained)
  • 1 medium cucumber
  • 10-12 cherry tomatoes
  • 1 ripe avocado
  • 2 tbsp olive oil
  • Juice of 1/2 lemon
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • 100 g of fresh berries (e.g., blueberries, strawberries, raspberries) - for dessert or on the side

Method

  1. Cook the quinoa
  2. Rinse the quinoa under cold water.
  3. Add 85 g quinoa and 240 ml water to a pot, bring to a boil.
  4. Reduce heat, cover, and simmer for 15 minutes.
  5. Let sit for 5 minutes, then fluff with a fork.
  6. Prepare the veggies
  7. Dice the cucumber into chunks.
  8. Halve the cherry tomatoes.
  9. Cut the avocado into cubes and drizzle with lemon juice to prevent browning.
  10. Assemble the bowl
  11. Divide the cooked quinoa between two bowls.
  12. Top with chickpeas, cucumber, cherry tomatoes, and avocado.
  13. Drizzle with olive oil, and season with salt and pepper to taste.
  14. Finish with berries
  15. Serve 100 g of fresh berries per person as a refreshing finish or enjoy them on the side.

Quinoa Bowl with Eggs, Broccoli & Pickled Cucumbers

2 servings5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 120 g dry quinoa
  • 2 large eggs
  • 200 g broccoli florets
  • 2-3 pickled cucumbers, sliced
  • 1 tbsp olive oil
  • 1 tsp mixed seeds (sesame, pumpkin or sunflower seeds)
  • Salt & black pepper, to taste
  • Optional: lemon juice or a little mustar

Method

  1. Cook the quinoa
  2. Rinse quinoa well. Cook in salted water according to package instructions. Drain and set aside.
  3. Cook the eggs
  4. Boil eggs for 6-7 minutes for a soft yolk or longer if you prefer them firm. Peel and cut in half.
  5. Prepare the broccoli
  6. Steam or lightly pan-fry broccoli with a little olive oil until tender but still bright green.
  7. Prepare the cucumbers
  8. Slice the pickled cucumbers. No extra seasoning is needed because they already add flavor.
  9. Assemble the bowls
  10. Divide quinoa between two bowls.
  11. Add broccoli, eggs, and pickled cucumbers.
  12. Sprinkle with seeds, add pepper, and a few drops of olive oil if needed.

Quinoa Bowl with Tofu & Veggies

2 servings11 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Quinoa or bulgur - 100 g (dry)
  • Firm tofu - 200 g
  • Cherry tomatoes - 6-8
  • Celery stalk - 1-2

  • For marinade/dressing
  • Olive oil - 2 tbsp

Method

  11. Assemble the bowl

  13. quinoa greens veggies tofu herbs.

Quinoa Veggie Bowls with Egg

2 servings7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Quinoa - 100 g (dry)
  • Avocado - 1
  • Broccoli - 150 g (fresh or frozen)
  • Eggs - 2
  • Cherry tomatoes - 10-12
  • White sesame seeds - 1 tsp (for garnish)
  • Salt & pepper - to taste
  • Olive oil - 1-2 tbsp
  • Lemon juice - 1-2 tsp (for avocado)
  • Optional drink: plant-based milk with cinnamon (like in the photo)

Method

  1. Quinoa
  2. Rinse the quinoa under running water.
  3. Add 100 g quinoa to 200 ml of salted water.
  4. Simmer, covered, for about 15 minutes until tender. Let it rest for 5 more minutes off the heat.
  5. Eggs
  6. Boil the eggs until soft-boiled (about 6-7 minutes after water starts boiling).
  7. Cool under cold water and peel.
  8. Broccoli
  9. Lightly steam or blanch the broccoli for 3-5 minutes so it stays bright green and a bit crisp.
  10. Avocado
  11. Slice thinly, drizzle with lemon juice, and sprinkle with sesame seeds.
  12. Cherry tomatoes
  13. Slice in half or leave whole.
  14. Assembly
  15. Place quinoa in the center of each bowl.
  16. Arrange avocado, cherry tomatoes, eggs, and broccoli around it.
  17. Drizzle with a bit of olive oil and season with salt and pepper to taste.

Quinoa with Chicken & Vegetables

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 120 g quinoa (uncooked)
  • 250 g chicken breast, diced
  • 1 small cucumber, diced
  • 1 red bell pepper, diced
  • 1 small red onion, chopped
  • 1 tbsp olive oil
  • Salt & pepper to taste
  • Fresh herbs (parsley, mint, or cilantro) for garnish

Method

  1. Rinse quinoa thoroughly, then cook according to package instructions (usually 1:2 ratio with water, simmer for ~15 min).
  2. In a pan, heat olive oil and sauté chicken until golden and fully cooked. Season with salt and pepper.
  3. Add chopped onion and bell pepper, cook for a few minutes until softened.
  4. Mix cooked quinoa with chicken and vegetables. Add cucumber for freshness.
  5. Garnish with fresh herbs and serve warm.

Quinoa with Chicken and Fresh Spinach Salad

Ingredients

  • For the quinoa with chicken
  • 120 g dry quinoa

  • A splash of lemon juice or balsamic vinegar (optional)
  • For the salad
  • A handful of fresh spinach leaves
  • 10-12 cherry tomatoes
  • 1/2 cucumber
  • 1-2 tsp pumpkin seeds or sesame seeds
  • 1 tsp olive oil
  • Salt, pepper, lemon juice or balsamic vinegar - to taste

Method

  1. Quinoa with Chicken
  2. Rinse the quinoa thoroughly. Cook it in a 1:2 ratio with water (~15 minutes) until soft and fluffy.
  3. Cut the chicken breast into bite-sized pieces. Season with salt, pepper, paprika, and garlic powder. Sauté or bake with a little olive oil until golden and cooked through.
  4. Mix the cooked quinoa with the chicken. Optionally, drizzle with a little lemon juice or low-sodium soy sauce.
  5. Salad
  6. Wash and slice the vegetables.
  7. Mix with fresh spinach and seeds.
  8. Dress with olive oil, salt, pepper, and a splash of lemon juice or balsamic vinegar.
  9. Serving
  10. Serve half of the quinoa-chicken mix and half of the salad on a plate.
  11. Store the remaining portion in the fridge for the next day.

  • 1 cucumber

Rice with Chicken & Vegetables

2 servings10 ingredients

Ingredients

  • basmati or long-grain rice - 120 g (dry)
  • chicken fillet or turkey - 250-300 g
  • carrot - 1 medium
  • onion - 1 small
  • bell pepper - 1/2-1 pc
  • zucchini or cauliflower - 150-200 g
  • olive oil - 1 tsp
  • garlic - 1-2 cloves
  • salt - to taste
  • spices: turmeric, paprika, cumin (zeera), black pepper
  • water or low-fat broth - 250-300 ml
  • pickled cucumbers (1-2 pcs per serving) if desired
  • Or fresh 70g

Method

  1. Rinse the rice well and pour hot water for 10 minutes (then drain).
  2. Cut the meat into cubes.
  3. Heat 1 tsp. olive oil in a frying pan or pot, fry the meat for 3-4 minutes until white.
  4. Add finely chopped onion, carrot strips - cook for another 2-3 minutes.
  5. Add pepper, zucchini/cabbage, spices and garlic - mix.
  6. Pour in the rice, pour water so that it barely covers the rice.
  7. Cover with a lid, cook over low heat for 15-18 minutes, do not stir.
  8. Remove from heat, let stand for 5 minutes

Rice with Vegetables

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Rice (brown, parboiled, or white) - 150-180 g (uncooked)
  • Broccoli - 200 g
  • Bell pepper - 1-2 pcs
  • Carrot - 1 large or 2 small
  • Onion - 1/2 to 1 pc (optional)
  • Olive or coconut oil - 2 tsp
  • Salt, pepper, your favorite spices - to taste
  • Soy sauce - 1-2 tsp (optional)

Method

  1. Cook the rice in salted water until done (you can do this ahead of time). For a healthier option, use brown or parboiled rice.
  2. Cut the vegetables
  3. Carrot - into thin strips or rounds
  4. Broccoli - into small florets
  5. Bell pepper - into thin slices
  6. Onion - into half-rings
  7. Heat 2 tsp of oil in a pan. Sauté the vegetables for about 5-7 minutes over medium heat, stirring occasionally.
  8. You can add a splash of water and cover with a lid to steam slightly.
  9. Add the cooked rice, season with salt, pepper, spices, and soy sauce if using. Mix everything well.
  10. Heat together for another 2-3 minutes - and it's ready!

Risotto

8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Arborio rice (or other short-grain) - 150 g
  • Vegetable broth (homemade) - 600 ml or 200 g of seafood. (if you don't like seafood, you can use vegetables or chicken)
  • Onion - 1 pc.
  • Garlic - 1 clove
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp
  • Broccoli or spinach - 150 g
  • Parmesan cheese (or its PP alternative) - 20-30 g
  • Salt, pepper to taste

Method

  1. Preparation: Cut the onion into small cubes, chop the garlic, cut the mushrooms into slices.
  2. Frying: Heat the olive oil in a frying pan, fry the onion until transparent, add the garlic. After a minute, add the mushrooms and seafood, simmer until cooked.
  3. Rice: Add the rice to the pan and fry for 1-2 minutes until the grains become transparent.
  4. Vegetables: Add broccoli (pre-boiled) or spinach at the final stage of cooking.
  5. When the rice is soft, remove from heat, add the grated Parmesan and mix well.

Roasted Sweet Potato & Feta Salad

2 servings7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 medium sweet potato, peeled and cut into cubes (~220 g)
  • 4-5 tbsp crumbled feta cheese (~65 g)
  • A handful of leafy greens (like rocket, spinach, or salad mix) (~40 g)
  • 10 cherry tomatoes, halved (~150 g)
  • 1/2 cucumber, sliced or chopped (~100 g)
  • 2 tsp olive oil (~9 g) plus extra for roasting
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • Optional: a sprinkle of dried basil or oregano
  • Optional add-ins: a few tbsp of chickpeas or toasted seeds (like pumpkin or sunflower) (~30 g)

Method

  1. Roast the sweet potato
  2. Toss the cubed sweet potato with a little olive oil, salt, and pepper.
  3. Roast in the oven at 200°C for about 25 minutes or until golden and tender.
  4. Prepare the salad
  5. While the sweet potato is roasting, slice the cucumber and tomatoes, and wash the greens.
  6. Assemble the salad
  7. In a large bowl, combine the roasted sweet potato, greens, tomatoes, and cucumber.
  8. Add crumbled feta on top and drizzle with olive oil. Season with salt, pepper, and herbs if using.
  9. (Optional): Add some chickpeas for protein or sprinkle with seeds for crunch.

Stuffed Peppers with Bulgur, Vegetables, and Cheese

8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 3-4 bell peppers
  • 90 g bulgur
  • 1 medium onion
  • 1 carrot
  • 1 tomato or 5-6 cherry tomatoes
  • 100 g hard cheese (e.g., Gouda, Mozzarella, or Suluguni)
  • 1-2 tbsp olive oil
  • Salt, pepper, oregano, basil to taste
  • Fresh herbs (parsley, dill, optional)

Method

  1. Cook the bulgur
  2. Rinse the bulgur and cook it until tender (usually 2 parts water to 1 part bulgur, cook for 10-12 minutes).
  3. Prepare the vegetables
  4. Chop the onion, grate the carrot, and sauté both in oil until soft.
  5. Add chopped tomato and simmer for another 5 minutes. Season with salt, pepper, and spices.
  6. Mix the filling
  7. Combine the bulgur with the vegetables, add 2/3 of the grated cheese, and mix well. You can add some fresh herbs too.
  8. Prepare the peppers
  9. Cut the peppers in half and remove the seeds. Place them in a baking dish.
  10. Stuff and bake
  11. Fill the peppers with the filling. Sprinkle the remaining cheese on top.
  12. Bake at 180°C for about 25-30 minutes until the cheese is golden brown.
  13. Serving
  14. Garnish with fresh herbs before serving. Delicious hot or slightly cooled.

Stuffed Zucchini with Chickpeas or Chicken

2 servings9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Base
  • Zucchini - 2 medium
  • Olive oil - 1 tbsp
  • Salt & pepper - to taste
  • Filling
  • Cooked chickpeas or finely chopped cooked chicken - 100 g
  • Onion - 1/2 small
  • Garlic - 1 clove (optional)
  • Fresh tomatoes or cherry tomatoes - 1-2 medium
  • Tomato sauce or crushed tomatoes - 2-3 tbsp
  • Shredded cheese (mozzarella, gouda, or suluguni) - 40-50 g
  • Fresh herbs (parsley, dill, or basil) - 1-2 tbsp, chopped

Method

  1. Prep the zucchini
  2. Cut the zucchini in half lengthwise and scoop out the center with a spoon.
  3. Sprinkle with salt and brush with olive oil.
  4. Pre-bake at 180°C for 10 minutes until slightly softened.
  5. Make the filling
  6. Sauté chopped onion (and garlic if using) in a little oil.
  7. Add the chickpeas or finely chopped chicken and cook for a few minutes.
  8. Stir in diced tomatoes and tomato sauce. Season with salt, pepper, and herbs.
  9. Simmer for a few minutes until thickened.
  10. Mix in a bit of fresh chopped herbs.
  11. Assemble
  12. Fill the pre-baked zucchini boats with the warm filling.
  13. Sprinkle with shredded cheese on top.
  14. Bake
  15. Bake at 180°C for 15-20 minutes, until the cheese is melted and golden.
  16. Serve
  17. Top with remaining fresh herbs.
  18. Optional: drizzle with yogurt, pesto, or balsamic glaze.

Sweet Potato with Asparagus & Creamy Cheese

2 servings4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 medium sweet potatoes
  • 200-250 g green asparagus
  • 120-150 g creamy soft cheese (burrata, ricotta, or cream cheese)
  • 1-2 tbsp olive oil
  • Salt and black pepper, to taste
  • Smoked paprika or chili flakes (optional)
  • Sesame seeds or grated hard cheese (optional, for topping)

Method

  1. Preheat the oven to 200°C / 200°C.
  2. Wash the sweet potatoes, pierce them with a fork, and bake whole for 40-50 minutes until soft inside.
  3. Trim the asparagus. Toss with olive oil, salt, and pepper. Roast on a baking tray for 12-15 minutes until tender.
  4. Once the sweet potatoes are ready, slice them lengthwise and gently mash the inside with a fork.
  5. Season the sweet potato flesh with salt, pepper, and optional smoked paprika or chili flakes.
  6. Top each sweet potato with creamy cheese and roasted asparagus.
  7. Finish with sesame seeds or a little grated cheese if desired.
  8. Serve warm as a balanced, satisfying lunch.

Tortellini with Spinach & Cream

2 servings9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 200 g spinach tortellini (fresh or whole-grain if available)
  • 30 g fresh spinach (optional - for extra fiber)
  • 100 ml low-fat cream or Greek yogurt (2-5%)
  • 1 tsp olive oil
  • 1 garlic clove, minced
  • Salt and black pepper to taste
  • 1 tbsp grated Parmesan (optional, for flavor)
  • 2 boiled or poached eggs (1 per serving)
  • 1 medium fruit (apple, orange, or kiwi - 1/2 per serving)

Method

  1. Cook the tortellini according to package directions until al dente. Drain and set aside.
  2. In a small pan, heat olive oil and sauté garlic for 1 minute.
  3. Add spinach (if using) and cook until wilted. Stir in the cream or Greek yogurt and season with salt and pepper.
  4. Mix in the tortellini and heat for another 1-2 minutes. Sprinkle a little Parmesan if desired.
  5. Serve each portion with one boiled or poached egg on the side.
  6. Enjoy a piece of fruit (like half an apple or orange) 15-20 minutes after your meal for a light, balanced finish.

Tuna or Chickpea Bowl with Avocado & Eggs

7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 200g tuna or chickpeas
  • 1 avocado
  • 2 eggs
  • 120g spinach (a handful per serving)
  • 120-150g cherry tomatoes
  • 2 tsp. grainy mustard
  • 1-2 tsp. olive oil
  • salt, pepper
  • optional: a few drops of lemon juice

Method

  1. Boil the eggs for 7-8 minutes, so that the yolk is slightly creamy. Cool and cut into quarters.
  2. Fry the shrimp in a pan with 1 tsp. olive oil for 2-3 minutes until golden brown. Season with salt and pepper.
  3. Cut the avocado into cubes.
  4. Cut the cherries in half.
  5. Place the spinach on a plate, top with the avocado, tomatoes, eggs and warm shrimp.
  6. Drizzle with the grainy mustard, add a little more olive oil and lemon juice if desired.

Vegetable Rice

2 servings6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Cooked white rice - 200 g
  • Green beans - 200 g
  • Green peas - 150 g
  • Sweet corn - 150 g
  • Red bell pepper - 80 g
  • Olive oil - 10 g
  • Garlic - 5 g (optional)
  • Salt & black pepper - to taste

Method

  1. Heat olive oil in a large pan over medium heat.
  2. Add garlic (if using) and cook for 30 seconds.
  3. Add green beans and bell pepper, sauté for 3-4 minutes.
  4. Add peas and corn, cook for another 2-3 minutes.
  5. Add the cooked rice, season with salt and pepper, and mix well.
  6. Cook for 2-3 minutes until everything is heated through.
  7. Divide into 2 portions and serve warm

Ingredients

  • Rice - 100 g (uncooked)
  • Green beans - 100 g
  • Corn (frozen or canned) - 80 g
  • Green peas - 70 g
  • Red bell pepper - 1/2
  • Carrot (optional) - 1/2 (grated or finely chopped)
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp
  • Salt, pepper - to taste
  • Garlic - 1 clove (optional)

Method

  1. Cook the rice according to package instructions.
  2. In a large pan, lightly sauté the vegetables (fresh or frozen) in 1 tsp of olive oil for 5-7 minutes.

  • Lemon juice or apple cider vinegar - 1 tbsp
  • Salt - to taste
  • Black pepper - to taste
  • Paprika or smoked paprika - 1/2 tsp
  • Dried or granulated garlic - 1/2 tsp

Whole-Wheat Spaghetti with Chicken & Cherry Tomatoes

2 servings · ~380-420 kcal5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 120 g dry whole-wheat spaghetti
  • 250 g chicken breast, thinly sliced
  • 200 g cherry tomatoes, halved
  • 1 tbsp olive oil
  • 2 cloves garlic, minced
  • 1 tsp dried Italian herbs
  • Salt & black pepper, to taste
  • Fresh parsley, chopped
  • Optional: chili flakes or lemon juice

Method

  1. Cook the pasta
  2. Cook spaghetti in salted water until al dente. Reserve a little pasta water and drain.
  3. Cook the chicken
  4. Heat olive oil in a non-stick pan over medium heat.
  5. Add chicken, season with salt and pepper, and cook for 5-6 minutes until fully cooked.
  6. Make the sauce
  7. Add garlic to the pan and cook for 30 seconds.
  8. Add cherry tomatoes and herbs, cook until tomatoes soften.
  9. Combine
  10. Add spaghetti and a little pasta water to the pan. Toss gently.
  11. Finish
  12. Adjust seasoning, add chili flakes or lemon if desired.
  13. Sprinkle with fresh parsley and serve.

Avocado, Feta, and Herb Salad with Lemon Dressing

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 ripe avocado
  • 100 g feta cheese
  • A handful of fresh herbs (parsley, dill, basil - your choice)
  • 1 medium cucumber
  • 1 tablespoon lemon juice
  • 1 tablespoon olive oil
  • A handful of walnuts or pine nuts (for crunch)
  • Salt and black pepper to taste

Method

  1. Peel and dice the avocado.
  2. Wash the cucumber, peel if you like, and slice into half-moons or cubes.
  3. Finely chop the herbs.
  4. Toast the nuts lightly in a dry pan for extra flavor.
  5. In a bowl, combine the avocado, cucumber, herbs, and nuts.
  6. Add the feta, crumbled or cut into small cubes.
  7. Drizzle with lemon juice and olive oil, then season with salt and pepper to taste.
  8. Gently toss everything together, being careful not to mash the avocado.

Baked Fish with Asparagus, Tomatoes and Parmesan

2 servings5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Fish (salmon / trout / cod / pike perch) - 300 g fillet
  • Asparagus - 200 g (6-8 stalks)
  • Cherry tomatoes - 150 g
  • Parmesan - 30 g
  • Olive oil - 1 tbsp. l.
  • Salt - 1/2 tsp. l.
  • Black pepper - 1/4 tsp. l.
  • Paprika or Italian herbs - 1/2 tsp. l.
  • Garlic - 1 clove (optional)
  • Lemon juice - 1 tsp. l. (optional)

Method

  1. Preheat the oven to 200°C.
  2. Prepare a baking sheet - line with parchment.
  3. Wash the asparagus, cut off the hard ends. Place on a baking sheet.

  5. Pour 1/2 tbsp. olive oil over the vegetables, add a little salt and pepper.

  7. Place in the oven for 12-15 minutes.

  9. You can sprinkle with lemon juice before serving.

Baked Potato with Chicken & Cheese Filling

2 servings6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Potato - 400-500 g (2 medium potatoes)
  • Chicken breast (skinless, plain) - 100 g
  • Low-fat cheese - 40 g (for filling) + 20 g (for topping)
  • Cream cheese or plain unsweetened yogurt - 50 g
  • Fresh herbs (parsley or dill) - 10 g
  • Butter - 5 g or a small amount of olive oil (optional)
  • Salt, spices (paprika, black pepper, dried herbs) - to taste

Method

  1. Wash the potatoes, wrap each in foil, and bake at 180°C for about 1.5 hours.
  2. Meanwhile, prepare the filling
  3. Boil or bake the chicken breast, then finely chop it.
  4. Grate 40 g of low-fat cheese.
  5. Mix with cream cheese (or plain yogurt) and chopped herbs. Stir well.
  6. Once the potatoes are slightly cooled, make a cut in the middle (don't slice all the way through).
  7. Add a little salt, spices, and a small piece of butter (optional), then mash lightly with a fork.
  8. Fill the potatoes with the prepared mixture, sprinkle 20 g of cheese on top, and bake for an additional 10 minutes at 180°C.

Baked Potatoes, Asparagus, and Fish Fillet

7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 350 g potatoes (about 3 medium)
  • 200 g asparagus
  • 300 g fish fillet (e.g., cod, pike-perch, or salmon)
  • 2 tbsp olive oil
  • 1 garlic clove
  • 1/2 lemon (juice and zest)
  • Salt and black pepper to taste
  • 1 tsp dried herbs (oregano, basil, or thyme)
  • Fresh parsley or dill for garnish

Method

  1. Preheat the oven to 200°C.
  2. Wash the potatoes well and cut into cubes or slices. Place them on a baking tray, drizzle with 1 tbsp olive oil, season with salt, pepper, and dried herbs. Toss to coat evenly. Bake for 25-30 minutes until golden and crispy.
  3. Wash the asparagus and trim the tough ends. Lightly coat with olive oil and season with salt. Add the asparagus to the baking tray with potatoes about 10 minutes before the potatoes are done.
  4. Season the fish fillet with salt, pepper, lemon juice, and lemon zest. Let it marinate for 5 minutes.
  5. Place the fish on a separate baking tray or in a baking dish lightly greased with olive oil. Bake in the oven for 15-20 minutes (depending on fillet thickness) until the fish is tender and flakes easily with a fork.
  6. Serve the baked potatoes, asparagus, and fish together, garnished with fresh herbs.

Baked Red Salmon with a Crispy Green Salad

2 servings9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the salmon
  • 2 pieces of red salmon fillet (150-180 g each)
  • 1 tbsp olive oil
  • Juice of 1/2 lemon
  • 1 garlic clove (minced or grated)
  • Salt and black pepper, to taste
  • Dried oregano or thyme (optional)
  • Lemon wedges for serving
  • For the salad
  • A handful of arugula or mixed salad greens
  • 1/2 cucumber (thinly sliced into half-moons)
  • 4-5 radishes (thinly sliced)
  • 50 g canned or cooked green peas
  • 1 tsp olive oil
  • 1 tsp apple cider vinegar or lemon juice
  • 1 tsp Dijon mustard (optional)
  • Salt and pepper, to taste

Method

  1. Bake the salmon
  2. Preheat the oven to 180°C with top and bottom heat or fan mode.
  3. Line a baking dish with parchment paper or lightly grease it.
  4. Place the salmon fillets skin-side down.
  5. Brush each piece with olive oil and drizzle with lemon juice.
  6. Sprinkle with salt, pepper, oregano, and add a bit of garlic on top.
  7. Bake for 12-15 minutes until the fish is tender and flakes easily with a fork.
  8. Prepare the salad
  9. In a bowl, combine arugula, sliced cucumber, radishes, and green peas.
  10. In a separate small bowl, whisk together olive oil, vinegar or lemon juice, salt, pepper, and (if using) Dijon mustard.
  11. Dress the salad and toss just before serving.
  12. To serve
  13. Place a piece of baked salmon on each plate.
  14. Add a portion of the salad alongside.
  15. Garnish with a lemon wedge and fresh herbs if desired.

Baked Salmon with Broccoli

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 salmon fillets (about 150-180 g each)
  • 1 medium head of broccoli, cut into florets (~250 g)
  • 1 medium zucchini, sliced (~150 g)
  • 2 tbsp olive oil
  • 1 lemon (juice and zest)
  • 2 garlic cloves, finely chopped
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • Fresh herbs (parsley or dill) for garnish (optional)

Method

  1. Preheat the oven to 200°C.
  2. Place the broccoli florets and zucchini slices on a baking tray or in a roasting dish. Drizzle with 1 tbsp olive oil, sprinkle with salt, pepper, and half of the chopped garlic. Toss to coat evenly.
  3. Roast the vegetables in the oven for 10 minutes.
  4. After 10 minutes, add the salmon fillets to the tray. Drizzle the remaining olive oil over the fish, season with salt, pepper, lemon zest, and the rest of the garlic.
  5. Squeeze lemon juice over everything.
  6. Roast for another 12-15 minutes, until the salmon is cooked through and flakes easily with a fork.
  7. Remove from the oven and garnish with fresh herbs if desired.

  18. 1 salmon fillet
  19. Half the roasted tomatoes
  20. Half the potatoes (with dill or onion)

Baked Salmon with Sweet Potato Fries & Green Salad

2 servings · ~450-500 kcal8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 200-250 g salmon fillet (or any fish you enjoy)
  • 350 g sweet potato, cut into fries
  • 1 tsp olive oil
  • Salt, black pepper, paprika, garlic powder
  • 2 handfuls of mixed greens (rocket, spinach, lettuce)
  • 1/2 cucumber, sliced
  • Lemon juice for serving

Method

  1. Preheat the oven to 200°C.
  2. Toss the sweet potato fries with olive oil and spices. Bake for 25-30 minutes until golden and crispy.
  3. Season the fish with salt, pepper, and your favorite herbs. Bake alongside the sweet potatoes for 12-15 minutes, depending on thickness.
  4. Prepare a simple salad with mixed greens and cucumber. Add a squeeze of lemon juice before serving.
  5. Divide everything between two plates and enjoy.
  6. Coach's note
  7. You can simply take a small piece of any fish you enjoy, even if you don't eat fish very often. Pair it with sweet potato and a generous portion of greens for a balanced, nourishing dinner. The greens don't have to be complicated - just a simple mix of leafy vegetables and cucumber works perfectly.

Baked Stuffed Peppers with Cheese & Zucchini

2 servings7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 large or 3 medium bell peppers
  • 1 small zucchini (or 1/2 medium), grated
  • 200 g cottage cheese (5-9%)
  • 60-80 g hard cheese, grated (e.g. mozzarella, gouda, cheddar)
  • 1 tomato (or 3-4 cherry tomatoes), finely chopped
  • 1/2 small onion, finely chopped
  • 1 garlic clove (optional)
  • Fresh herbs (parsley, dill, basil) - to taste
  • 1 tsp olive oil
  • Salt, pepper, paprika, Italian herbs - to taste

Method

  1. Prepare the peppers
  2. Slice peppers in half lengthwise or cut off the tops ("lids").
  3. Remove seeds and inner membranes.
  4. Make the filling
  5. Grate the zucchini (no need to peel if it's young).
  6. Mix together the cottage cheese, grated hard cheese, zucchini, tomato, onion, garlic, and herbs.
  7. Add olive oil and season with salt, pepper, and spices.
  8. If the filling seems watery, let it sit 5-10 minutes and drain excess liquid.
  9. Stuff the peppers
  10. Fill each pepper generously with the cheese mixture.
  11. Optionally sprinkle a little more grated cheese on top.
  12. Bake
  13. Place peppers in a baking dish. Add a few tablespoons of water or vegetable broth to the bottom to prevent burning.
  14. Cover with foil and bake at 180°C for 30-35 minutes.
  15. Uncover and bake an additional 5-10 minutes until golden on top.
  16. Serving
  17. Serve warm with a side of salad or a spoon of plain yogurt or Greek yogurt.

Baked Sweet Potato with Chickpeas, Spinach & Hummus

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 medium sweet potatoes
  • 160 g cooked chickpeas (or canned, drained & rinsed)
  • 1 small red onion, sliced
  • 2 handfuls fresh spinach
  • 3-4 tbsp hummus
  • 1 tbsp olive oil
  • Salt & black pepper to taste
  • Optional: paprika, cumin, garlic powder for chickpeas

Method

  1. Preheat oven to 200°C. Wash sweet potatoes, pierce with a fork, and bake for 40-50 minutes until soft.
  2. Meanwhile, heat olive oil in a pan. Add onion and sauté until soft. Add spinach and cook briefly until wilted.
  3. In another pan, roast chickpeas with spices until golden and slightly crispy.
  4. Cut baked sweet potatoes in half. Top with spinach-onion mixture, roasted chickpeas, and a spoonful of hummus.
  5. Season with salt, pepper, and a drizzle of olive oil if desired.

Baked Vegetables with Hummus and Crispy Pita Bread

7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Eggplant - 150 g (can be replaced with pepper )
  • Zucchini - 150 g
  • Cherry tomatoes - 120 g
  • Hummus - 60 g
  • Thin pita bread - 40 g
  • Olive oil - 5 g
  • Garlic, paprika, oregano
  • Greens

Method

  1. Cut the vegetables into large pieces.
  2. Mix with oil and spices.
  3. Bake for 25 minutes at 200°C.
  4. Dry the pita bread in a pan or in the oven until crispy.
  5. Serve the vegetables with hummus and pieces of pita bread.

Baked Vegetables with Parmesan (2-Day Portions)

4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 600 g cauliflower florets
  • 2 medium zucchinis (sliced into rounds or half-moons)
  • 2 medium sweet potatoes (peeled and cut into cubes or slices)
  • 3 tbsp olive oil
  • 2 tsp dried thyme or rosemary
  • 1 tsp paprika
  • Salt and pepper, to taste
  • 2 garlic cloves (minced, optional)
  • 50 g grated Parmesan cheese

Method

  1. Preheat oven to 200°C.
  2. In a large bowl, toss the vegetables with olive oil, herbs, paprika, salt, pepper, and garlic (if using).
  3. Spread the veggies out in a single layer on a baking sheet.
  4. Roast for 25 minutes.
  5. Remove the baking sheet, sprinkle the grated Parmesan evenly over the vegetables, then return to the oven for another 5-7 minutes until the cheese melts and browns slightly.
  6. Divide into 2 portions and store in the fridge.

Baked Zucchini Boats

2 servings9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Medium zucchini - 2 pcs
  • Mushrooms - 150 g (can be replaced with bell pepper)
  • Spinach (fresh or frozen) - 120 g
  • Chicken fillet - 200 g
  • Mozzarella light or suluguni cheese - 120 g
  • Greek yogurt 2-5% - 2 tbsp. l. (40 g)
  • Garlic - 1 clove
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp. l.
  • Salt, pepper, paprika, Italian herbs - to taste

Method

  1. Preparing the zucchini
  2. Cut in half lengthwise, scoop out the middle with a spoon. Lightly salt.
  3. Filling
  4. Cut the chicken into small cubes.
  5. Fry in a dry frying pan for 4-5 minutes.
  6. Add mushrooms, cook until liquid evaporates.
  7. Add spinach and garlic, simmer for another 1-2 minutes.
  8. Finally, stir in Greek yogurt and spices.
  9. Baking
  10. Fill the zucchini with the filling, sprinkle with cheese on top.
  11. Bake at 180°C for about 25-30 minutes until golden brown.

Baked Zucchini with Feta, Tomatoes, and Parmesan

2 servings6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 medium zucchinis, sliced
  • 150 g cherry tomatoes, halved
  • 100 g feta cheese, crumbled
  • 50 g Parmesan cheese, grated
  • 2 tbsp olive oil
  • 1 garlic clove, minced
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • Fresh herbs (basil or oregano) for garnish

Method

  1. Preheat oven to 180°C.
  2. In a baking dish, arrange the zucchini slices in a single layer.
  3. Scatter the cherry tomatoes over the zucchini.
  4. Sprinkle minced garlic, crumbled feta, and grated Parmesan evenly on top.
  5. Drizzle with olive oil, season with salt and pepper.
  6. Bake for 20-25 minutes until zucchini is tender and cheese is golden.
  7. Garnish with fresh herbs before serving. video omitted

Beef with Rice

2 servings5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Beef - 300 g (150 g × 2)
  • Olive oil
  • Asparagus - 200 g (100 g × 2)
  • Fresh garlic - 6 cloves (3 × 2)
  • Soy sauce - 4 tbsp (2 × 2)
  • Salt - to taste
  • Ground black pepper - to taste
  • Rice - 140 g (70 g × 2)

Method

  1. Cook the rice until tender.
  2. Prepare the beef: Slice the beef into thin strips. Season with salt, pepper, and add soy sauce (if using). Let it marinate for 10-15 minutes.
  3. Heat olive oil in a pan over medium heat. Add garlic and sauté for 1-2 minutes until golden, then remove the garlic to avoid burning.
  4. Add the beef to the pan and fry for about 5 minutes until browned on all sides. Remove the beef from the pan and set aside.
  5. In the same pan, add asparagus and cook for about 5-10 minutes until tender but still crisp.
  6. Return the beef to the pan, mix well, and heat through for another 1-2 minutes.
  7. Serve the beef and asparagus over the cooked rice.

Boiled Potatoes with Dill + Side

8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Potatoes - 400 g
  • Fresh dill - 10 g (a small bunch)
  • Salt - to taste
  • A drizzle of olive oil or a small knob of butter (optional, 5 g per serving)
  • Option 1: With Herring
  • Lightly salted herring fillet - 150 g
  • Onion - 50 g (sliced into rings)
  • Option 2: With Fresh Sweet Salad
  • Mixed lettuce leaves - 100 g
  • Cherry tomatoes - 150 g
  • Sweet corn (canned) - 80 g (drained)
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp
  • Lemon juice - 1 tsp
  • A pinch of salt

Method

  1. Peel the potatoes, cut into chunks, and boil in salted water until tender (about 15 minutes). Drain and toss with chopped dill and a little olive oil or butter.
  2. If using herring: Slice fillet into pieces, serve with onion rings on the side.
  3. If using salad: Mix lettuce, halved cherry tomatoes, and corn. Dress with olive oil, lemon juice, and a pinch of salt.
  4. Serve potatoes hot with your chosen side.

Braised Cabbage with Beans

2 servings · ~700 kcal5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Cooked beans (kidney beans, chickpeas, or white beans) - 200 g
  • Braised cabbage - 400 g
  • Olive oil - 10 g (2 tsp)
  • Carrot - 60 g
  • Onion - 60 g
  • Spices to taste (salt, pepper, coriander, paprika, bay leaf)
  • Calories: approximately 700 kcal (for 2 servings)

Method

  1. Wash and chop the carrot and onion. Sauté in a pan with 1 tsp olive oil until soft.
  2. Add the braised cabbage and cook for 15-20 minutes over medium heat, adding a little water if needed.
  3. Add the cooked beans, salt and pepper to taste, and the spices.
  4. Simmer together for another 5-10 minutes to allow the flavors to meld.
  5. Serve warm.

Bulgar with Vegetables, Chickpeas & Optional Tofu

2 servings8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the bulgur with vegetables
  • Bulgur - 200 g
  • Water or vegetable broth - 400 ml
  • Carrot - 1 medium
  • Red bell pepper - 1
  • Onion - 1 small
  • Garlic - 1 clove (optional)
  • Cooked or canned chickpeas - 1/2 can (approx. 150 g)
  • Olive oil - 1-2 tbsp
  • Salt, pepper, turmeric or paprika - to taste
  • For the tofu (optional)
  • Firm tofu - 200-250 g
  • Soy sauce - 2 tbsp
  • Paprika (smoked or sweet) - 1 tsp
  • Garlic powder - 1/2 tsp
  • Vegetable oil - for frying
  • Note: You can skip the tofu if you prefer - the dish will still be delicious and filling!

Method

  1. Prepare the tofu (if using)
  2. Cut tofu into small cubes.
  3. Marinate in soy sauce, paprika, and garlic powder for 10-15 minutes.
  4. Fry in a pan until golden brown, or bake in the oven at 200°C for ~20 minutes.
  5. Cook the bulgur with vegetables
  6. Finely chop the onion, carrot, and bell pepper.
  7. Sauté onion in olive oil until translucent. Add the carrot and bell pepper. Cook until slightly soft.
  8. Add bulgur and stir for 1-2 minutes.
  9. Pour in water or broth, add salt and spices. Cover and simmer on low heat for ~12-15 minutes, until the bulgur absorbs all liquid.
  10. Stir in the chickpeas and cook for another 2-3 minutes to warm them through.
  11. Serve
  12. Spoon the bulgur mixture onto a plate. Top with crispy tofu (if using). Garnish with herbs if desired.

Cabbage Salad

8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • White cabbage or Peking cabbage - 200 g
  • Carrot - 50 g (1/2 small carrot)
  • Cucumber - 1 pc. (100 g)
  • Lemon juice - 1 tsp
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp
  • Salt and pepper - to taste
  • Flax or sesame seeds (for sprinkling) - 1 tsp
  • Greens (parsley, dill) - as desired

Method

  1. Preparing vegetables
  2. Thinly slice the cabbage.
  3. Grate the carrots on a fine grater or cut into thin strips.
  4. Cut the cucumber into circles or strips.
  5. Dressing
  6. In a small bowl, whisk together the olive oil, lemon juice, salt, and pepper.
  7. Toss
  8. Combine all vegetables in a bowl, pour over the dressing, and toss well.
  9. Sprinkle the salad with flax or sesame seeds.

Cabbage Salad with Baked Chicken Fillet

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the cabbage salad
  • White cabbage - 300 g, finely shredded
  • Cucumber - 1 medium
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp
  • Lemon juice or apple cider vinegar - to taste
  • Salt and black pepper - to taste
  • Protein option 1 - Chicken
  • Chicken breast fillet - 250 g
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp
  • Paprika, garlic powder, black pepper - to taste
  • Protein option 2 - Chickpeas (vegetarian)
  • Cooked chickpeas - 200 g
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp
  • Paprika, cumin, black pepper - to taste

Method

  1. Prepare the salad
  2. In a large bowl, combine shredded cabbage and sliced cucumber. Add olive oil, lemon juice, salt, and pepper. Mix well and set aside.
  3. Chicken option
  4. Season the chicken fillet with olive oil and spices. Bake at 180°C for 20-25 minutes until fully cooked. Slice before serving.
  5. Chickpea option
  6. Toss the chickpeas with olive oil and spices. Bake at 180°C for 15-20 minutes until slightly crispy.
  7. Divide the cabbage salad between two plates and add the baked chicken slices or roasted chickpeas on the side.

Caesar Salad

2 servings8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 300 g chicken breast
  • (or 4 boiled eggs if you prefer a meat-free version)
  • 200 g salad mix (romaine, iceberg, or other leafy greens)
  • 20-30 cherry tomatoes
  • 60 g Parmesan cheese (grated)
  • 2 tbsp olive oil
  • Salt and pepper - to taste
  • For the dressing
  • 4 tbsp plain Greek yogurt
  • 2 tsp Dijon mustard
  • 2 garlic cloves (optional, minced)
  • 2 tsp lemon juice
  • Salt and pepper - to taste

Method

  1. Cook the chicken
  2. Season the chicken breast with salt and pepper. Heat olive oil in a pan and cook the chicken for about 5-7 minutes on each side, until fully cooked. Slice into thin strips.
  3. (Alternatively, boil the eggs for 8-10 minutes, peel, and slice or quarter them.)
  4. Prepare the dressing
  5. In a bowl, combine the Greek yogurt, Dijon mustard, lemon juice, and optional garlic. Season with salt and pepper to taste.
  6. Assemble the salad
  7. In a large bowl, add the salad mix and halved cherry tomatoes. Top with either the sliced chicken or boiled eggs.
  8. Drizzle with dressing, sprinkle with grated Parmesan, and toss gently before serving.

Caesar with Chicken and Chickpeas

Ingredients

  2. Dry the chickpeas, if desired - brown in the oven or in a dry pan with paprika.

Cauliflower Steak with Chickpeas

3 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Cauliflower - 150-200 g (1 steak)
  • Boiled or canned chickpeas - 100-120 g
  • Olive oil - 1-2 tbsp. l.
  • Garlic - 1-2 cloves (or to taste)
  • Paprika (smoked or sweet) - 1 tsp. l.
  • Oregano or thyme - 1 tsp. l.
  • Salt, pepper - to taste
  • Lemon juice - 1-2 tsp. l.
  • Hard cheese (optional) - 20-30 g, grated

Method

  1. Prepare the cabbage
  2. Cut the cabbage into steaks, wash and dry.
  3. Prepare the marinade
  4. Mix olive oil, minced garlic, paprika, dried herbs, salt and pepper.
  5. Add lemon juice for freshness.
  6. Marinate the cabbage and chickpeas
  7. Coat the cabbage steak with the marinade.
  8. Mix the chickpeas with spices and 0.5 tbsp. of olive oil.
  9. Bake
  10. Place the cabbage and chickpeas on a baking sheet lined with parchment.
  11. Bake in an oven preheated to 200°C for 20-25 minutes.
  12. 5 minutes before the steak is done, sprinkle the steak with cheese (if using).

Chicken & Vegetable Stew

7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 200 g chicken breast, cut into small cubes
  • 90 g broccoli florets
  • 1 small carrot, sliced thinly
  • 1/2 red bell pepper, diced
  • 1 small zucchini, diced (optional)
  • 1 small onion, chopped
  • 1 clove garlic, minced
  • 1 tsp olive oil
  • 120 ml low-sodium vegetable or chicken broth
  • Salt, pepper, paprika, or other spices to taste
  • Optional: fresh herbs (parsley or thyme)

Method

  1. Heat olive oil in a pan over medium heat. Add onion and garlic, sauté until soft.
  2. Add chicken cubes and cook until lightly browned.
  3. Add carrot, bell pepper, and zucchini (if using). Cook 3-4 minutes, stirring occasionally.
  4. Add broccoli and pour in the broth. Cover and simmer 8-10 minutes, until all vegetables are tender and chicken is cooked through.
  5. Season with salt, pepper, and spices to taste.
  6. Serve half the stew for dinner today. Keep the second half in the fridge for the next day. Reheat gently before serving.
  7. Optional: sprinkle fresh herbs on top for extra flavor.
  8. Tip: You can add a small portion of quinoa, brown rice, or cauliflower rice for a more filling meal without adding too many calories.

Chicken with Sweet Potato & Zucchini

2 servings9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Chicken fillet / turkey / beef - 300 g (150 g per serving) if you don't want meat, take 2 eggs
  • Sweet potato or potato - 300 g (150 g per serving)
  • Zucchini - 150 g (75 g per serving)
  • Onion - 40 g (1 small)
  • Parmesan - 20 g (10 g per serving)
  • Olive oil - 10 ml (1 tsp per serving)
  • Salt, pepper, spices - to taste

Method

  1. Cut the meat, season with salt, pepper, and your favorite spices.
  2. Cut the sweet potato into cubes, the zucchini into circles, the onion into half rings, the mushrooms into slices.
  3. Mix the vegetables with 1 tsp. oil, a little salt and pepper.

Chicken with Vegetables in Soy Sauce

2 servings8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Chicken breast - 300 g
  • Broccoli - 200 g
  • Red bell pepper - 1 pc
  • Carrot - 1 pc
  • Onion (optional) - 1/2 pc
  • Olive oil - 1 tbsp
  • Soy sauce (low-sodium, no added sugar) - 2-3 tbsp
  • Garlic - 1 clove
  • Ginger (fresh or ground) - 1/2 tsp
  • Salt, pepper - to taste

Method

  1. Cut the chicken into cubes. Sauté in olive oil until golden.
  2. Add sliced carrot and bell pepper, then broccoli (fresh or frozen).
  3. Stir in garlic and ginger.
  4. Pour in soy sauce, stir-fry for another 5-7 minutes until the veggies are tender but still crisp.
  5. Serve hot.

Couscous with Vegetables and Chickpeas

2 servings9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 120 g couscous (dry)
  • 150 g cooked or canned chickpeas (rinsed)
  • 1 small zucchini
  • 1 red or yellow bell pepper
  • 1 carrot
  • 1 small red onion
  • 1 garlic clove
  • 2 tbsp olive oil
  • 1/2 tsp turmeric or curry powder (optional)
  • Salt and black pepper, to taste
  • Fresh herbs (parsley, cilantro, or dill)
  • Lemon juice, for serving

Method

  1. Pour boiling water over the couscous (1:1 ratio), cover, and let sit for 5 minutes. Fluff with a fork.
  2. Cut zucchini, carrot, bell pepper, and onion into small cubes or strips.
  3. Heat olive oil in a pan, add onion and garlic, and sauté lightly.
  4. Add carrot, bell pepper, and zucchini. Cook for 7-10 minutes until tender.
  5. Stir in chickpeas, season with salt, pepper, and spices. Cook for another 2-3 minutes.
  6. Combine the couscous with the vegetables and chickpeas. Sprinkle with fresh herbs.
  7. Serve with a squeeze of lemon juice.

Dinner Bowl with Tuna, Millet & Steamed Vegetables

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 160-200 g canned tuna (in water or olive oil)
  • 100 g dry millet
  • 200 g broccoli
  • 150 g zucchini (sliced)
  • 150 g cherry tomatoes
  • 1-2 tablespoons olive oil
  • Salt and black pepper to taste
  • Optional: lemon juice, fresh herbs like dill or parsley

Method

  1. Cook the millet
  2. Rinse the millet thoroughly under running water. Add it to a pot with 250 ml of water and a pinch of salt. Bring to a boil, then reduce the heat and simmer covered for about 15 minutes, or until the water is absorbed and the millet is fluffy. Let it rest for 5 minutes.
  3. Steam the vegetables
  4. Steam the broccoli and zucchini for 5-7 minutes until tender but still bright. Season with salt and pepper.
  5. Prepare the tuna
  6. Drain the tuna. You can leave it plain or mix it with a little olive oil, lemon juice, and chopped herbs if you like.
  7. Assemble the bowls
  8. Divide the millet between two bowls. Add the steamed vegetables and the tuna. Top with halved cherry tomatoes. Drizzle a little olive oil on top, and season with extra black pepper if desired.

Dinner Salad with Halloumi, Avocado & Cucumber

2 servings7 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Arugula - 50 g
  • Avocado - 1 ripe (about 150 g without pit)
  • Cucumber - 1 medium (about 150 g)
  • Cherry tomatoes - 120 g (about 10-12 pieces)
  • Halloumi cheese - 150 g (or substitute with firm feta or bryndza)
  • Olive oil - 1-2 tbsp (15-30 ml)
  • Lemon juice - 1 tsp (5 ml)
  • Salt & black pepper - to taste

Method

  1. Grill the cheese
  2. Cut halloumi into cubes and grill or pan-fry on a dry skillet over medium heat for 1-2 minutes per side, until golden.
  3. Prep the veggies
  4. Slice avocado thinly.
  5. Peel the cucumber into ribbons using a vegetable peeler.
  6. Halve the cherry tomatoes (or leave them whole if small).
  7. Assemble the salad
  8. In a large bowl or plate, arrange a base of arugula.
  9. Top with cucumber ribbons, avocado slices, cherry tomatoes, and grilled cheese cubes.
  10. Drizzle with olive oil and lemon juice. Season with salt and pepper.

Dinner Salad with Spinach, Mozzarella & Jamón

Ingredients

  • Fresh spinach - 120 g
