Farfalle Pasta with Arugula, Cherry Tomatoes, Mozzarella, and Grilled Chicken Breast

2 servings5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Farfalle pasta (whole grain or durum wheat) - 120-140 g (dry)
  • Chicken breast (cooked/grilled) - 160-200 g
  • Arugula - 2 handfuls (~40-50 g)
  • Cherry tomatoes - 200 g
  • Mozzarella (light, mini balls or regular) - 80-100 g
  • Olive oil - 2 tsp
  • Salt, black pepper, oregano, basil - to taste
  • (Optional: a few drops of balsamic vinegar)

Method

  1. Boil the pasta until al dente. Drain and let cool slightly.
  2. Cut the cooked or grilled chicken into small bite-sized pieces.
  3. Halve the cherry tomatoes. Slice or cube the mozzarella if needed.
  4. In a large bowl, combine the pasta, arugula, tomatoes, chicken, and mozzarella.
  5. Drizzle with olive oil, season with salt, pepper, and herbs.
  6. Add a splash of balsamic vinegar if desired.
  7. Mix gently and serve warm or as a cold salad.

Fresh Mozzarella & Greens Salad

2 servings8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 140 g fresh mozzarella balls (bocconcini)
  • 2 medium tomatoes, diced
  • 2 large handfuls of mixed greens (arugula, beet leaves, spinach, etc.)
  • 2 tsp olive oil
  • 2 tsp lemon juice or balsamic vinegar
  • Salt and pepper to taste

Method

  1. Wash and dry the greens. Place them in a large salad bowl.
  2. Dice the tomatoes and add them to the greens.
  3. Add the mozzarella balls.
  4. Drizzle with olive oil and lemon juice (or balsamic vinegar).
  5. Season with salt and pepper, toss gently, and serve immediately.

Greek Salad

2 servings6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Tomatoes - 2 medium (about 200 g)
  • Cucumber - 1 medium (about 150 g)
  • Bell pepper (yellow or red) - 1 (120-150 g)
  • Red onion - 1/2 small (about 50 g)
  • Feta cheese - 100 g
  • Black olives (pitted) - 10-12 pieces (40-50 g)
  • Extra virgin olive oil - 2 tbsp (≈20 ml)
  • Dried oregano - 1/2 tsp
  • Salt - to taste (careful, feta and olives are already salty)
  • Black pepper - optional

Method

  1. Cut the tomatoes into large chunks.
  2. Slice the cucumber into half-moons or cubes.
  3. Cut the bell pepper into strips or bite-sized pieces.
  4. Slice the red onion thinly (you can soak it in lemon juice or hot water for 5 minutes to mellow the flavor, if you prefer).
  5. Cube the feta cheese.
  6. Combine all the vegetables in a bowl, add the olives and feta.
  7. Drizzle with olive oil, sprinkle with oregano, and season with a little salt and pepper.

Greek-Style Salad

Ingredients

  • 300 g tomatoes, chopped
  • 80 g red onion, thinly sliced
  • 120 g cucumbers, chopped (if you want to include them)
  • 120 g feta cheese, cubed

  • 1 tbsp lemon juice or vinegar (about 15 g)

Method

  2. Chop the tomatoes, slice the onion, and chop the cucumbers if using.

  6. Add herbs

  8. Make the dressing

  11. Pour the dressing over the salad and gently toss.

Green Pasta with Tuna & Crispy Chickpeas

2 servings10 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 150 g high-protein pasta (dry)
  • 1 can tuna in water, drained (160 g)
  • 200 g chickpeas, drained
  • 1 medium avocado (150 g)
  • 10 g sesame seeds
  • 2 tsp olive oil
  • 1 tsp paprika
  • Salt & black pepper, to taste
  • For the green sauce
  • 40 g fresh spinach
  • 80 g Greek yogurt (2%)
  • 1 garlic clove
  • Juice of 1/2 lemon
  • Salt & black pepper

Method

  1. Cook the pasta according to the package instructions.
  2. Toss the chickpeas with olive oil, paprika, salt, and pepper. Roast or air-fry at 200°C for 15-20 minutes until crispy.
  3. Blend the spinach, Greek yogurt, garlic, lemon juice, salt, and pepper until smooth.
  4. Mix the cooked pasta with the green sauce.
  5. Divide between two bowls and top with tuna, sliced avocado, crispy chickpeas, and sesame seeds.

Green Salad with Mango

2 servings10 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 120 g mixed greens (lettuce, spinach, arugula, or your favorite)
  • 1 ripe mango, peeled and diced
  • 1 small cucumber, sliced
  • 1/2 red bell pepper, thinly sliced
  • 1/4 red onion, finely chopped
  • 10 cherry tomatoes, halved
  • 1 tablespoon olive oil
  • 1 tablespoon fresh lemon juice (or lime)
  • Salt and pepper to taste
  • Optional: handful of fresh cilantro or parsley for garnish

Method

  1. In a large bowl, combine the mixed greens, mango, cucumber, bell pepper, red onion, and cherry tomatoes.
  2. In a small bowl, whisk together olive oil, lemon juice, salt, and pepper.
  3. Pour the dressing over the salad and toss gently to combine.
  4. Garnish with fresh cilantro or parsley if you like.
  5. Divide into two plates and serve immediately.

Healthy Veggie Power Bowl (Dinner for 2 servings )

2 servings8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 ripe avocado, diced
  • 2 hard-boiled eggs, chopped
  • 160 g cooked or roasted chickpeas (drained and rinsed if canned)
  • 150 g cherry tomatoes, halved
  • 60 g fresh spinach or mixed greens
  • Salt and black pepper to taste
  • Fresh parsley or cilantro, chopped (optional for garnish)
  • Juice of 1/2 lemon (or use apple cider vinegar as a dressing)
  • 2 tsp extra virgin olive oil

Method

  1. Prepare Chickpeas (optional for extra flavor)
  2. Toss cooked chickpeas in olive oil, paprika, garlic powder, salt, and pepper.
  3. Roast in the oven at 200°C for ~15-20 minutes until crispy (optional step, can use them as-is).
  4. Assemble the bowls
  5. Divide spinach/mixed greens between two bowls.
  6. Top each with half the avocado, one chopped egg, half the chickpeas, and half the tomatoes.
  7. Drizzle with olive oil and lemon juice.
  8. Season with salt, pepper, and fresh herbs to taste.

Ingredients

  • Buckwheat (dry) - 90 g
  • Beets (boiled or baked) - 300 g
  • Green beans - 300 g
  • Olive oil - 1-2 tsp. (5-10 g, optional)

Method

  1. Quinoa
  2. Rinse 90 g of dry Quinoa, pour ~220 ml of water. Cook for 12-15 min under the lid.
  3. Beans
  4. Boil for 6-8 min or quickly simmer. If you add oil - use half of it here.
  5. Beets
  6. Grate and simmer for 5-7 minutes (you can do it without oil, adding a little water).
  7. Herring
  8. Cut into portioned pieces. No need to add salt.
  9. Serving
  10. Divide all ingredients into 2 level plates.

Italian-Style Tuna Salad

8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Canned tuna in its own juice - 1 can (120-150 g drained)
  • Cherry tomatoes or regular tomatoes - 150-200 g
  • Arugula - 50-70 g
  • Cheese (mozzarella, feta, or parmesan) - 50-70 g
  • Olives or capers (optional) - 10-15 pieces
  • Extra virgin olive oil - 1-2 tbsp
  • Lemon juice or balsamic vinegar - 1 tsp
  • Salt and black pepper - to taste
  • Italian herbs (oregano, basil, rosemary) - optional

Method

  1. Rinse and dry the arugula.
  2. Cut tomatoes into halves or chunks.
  3. Cut the cheese into cubes (feta), tear it (mozzarella), or grate it (parmesan).
  4. Drain the tuna and gently flake it with a fork.
  5. In a large bowl, combine arugula, tomatoes, cheese, tuna, and olives.
  6. Drizzle with olive oil and lemon juice or balsamic vinegar. Add salt, pepper, and herbs. Toss gently.

Mashed Potatoes & Roasted Vegetables

2 servings8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the mashed potatoes
  • 4 medium potatoes (about 500-600 g)
  • 2 tbsp butter (or plant-based alternative)
  • 80 ml milk (or plant-based milk)
  • Salt - to taste
  • Optional: black pepper, garlic powder
  • For the roasted vegetables
  • 1 medium zucchini
  • 1 red bell pepper
  • 1 medium carrot
  • 1 small red onion
  • 1 tbsp olive oil
  • Salt, pepper, dried herbs (e.g. oregano, thyme) - to taste

Method

  1. Prepare the mashed potatoes
  2. Peel and chop potatoes into chunks.
  3. Boil in salted water for ~15-20 minutes until soft.
  4. Drain, then mash with butter and warm milk.
  5. Season with salt and pepper to taste.
  6. Roast the vegetables
  7. Preheat oven to 200°C.
  8. Cut all vegetables into similar-sized chunks.
  9. Toss with olive oil, salt, pepper, and herbs.
  10. Spread on a baking tray and roast for 25-30 minutes, flipping halfway, until golden and tender.
  11. Serve
  12. Plate mashed potatoes and top or serve alongside the roasted vegetables.
  13. Optional: add a drizzle of olive oil or sprinkle of nutritional yeast or Parmesan on top.

Mashed Potatoes with Chickpea Meatballs & Light Sauce

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 can (about 240 g drained) cooked chickpeas
  • 1 small onion, finely chopped
  • 1 garlic clove, minced
  • 1 egg (or 1 tbsp flaxseed + 2 tbsp water for vegan option)
  • 1-2 tbsp oat flour or breadcrumbs (whole-grain)
  • Salt, black pepper, paprika, herbs - to taste
  • 1 tsp olive oil (for baking or frying)

Method

  1. Mash the chickpeas with a fork or blend in a food processor until smooth but still slightly textured.
  2. Add onion, garlic, egg, flour, and spices - mix well.
  3. Form small balls and bake in the oven (180°C / 180°C, ~20 min) or cook in a pan until golden brown.
  4. Ingredients for the Mashed Potatoes
  5. 3 medium potatoes
  6. 1 tbsp light butter or olive oil
  7. 2-3 tbsp warm milk (plant-based or low-fat)
  8. Salt and pepper to taste
  9. Boil potatoes until soft, then mash.
  10. Add milk and butter, season to taste, and mix until creamy.
  11. Light Tomato Sauce
  12. 1 tsp olive oil
  13. 1/2 onion, finely chopped
  14. 1 small can (200-250 ml) crushed tomatoes or tomato passata
  15. Salt, pepper, oregano, basil - to taste
  16. Sauté onion in olive oil until soft.
  17. Add tomatoes and spices, simmer for 10 minutes until thickened.
  18. Add the chickpea balls and let them soak in the sauce for 5-7 minutes.

Mashed Potatoes with Sauerkraut & Beetroot

2 servings4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 300 g mashed potatoes (prepared with a little water or plant milk)
  • 200 g sauerkraut
  • 160-200 g cooked beetroot, diced
  • 0-120 g chicken (optional) - a small amount for light protein
  • Salt and black pepper to taste

Method

  2. Boil peeled potatoes until soft.
  3. Mash using a splash of water or plant milk.
  4. Add salt and pepper.
  5. Divide into two portions of about 150 g each.
  6. Sauerkraut
  7. Add 100 g per plate.
  8. Rinse lightly if it's too salty.
  9. Beetroot
  10. Dice boiled or steamed beetroot.
  11. Split into two servings, about 80-100 g per portion.
  12. Optional chicken
  13. Use up to 60 g of chicken per serving, or skip it completely.
  14. Cook in a pan or bake with minimal oil.
  15. Add a small piece to each plate only for light protein.

Meatless Lasagna

2 servings9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Lasagna sheets - 6 (no-boil or regular, pre-cooked if needed)
  • Canned tomatoes or fresh chopped tomatoes - 250 g
  • Bell pepper - 1
  • Zucchini - 1 small
  • Onion - 1 small
  • Garlic - 1-2 cloves
  • Tomato paste - 1 tbsp
  • Olive oil - 1-2 tbsp
  • Salt & pepper - to taste
  • Dried oregano or basil - 1 tsp
  • Shredded cheese (like mozzarella) - 100 g
  • Fresh basil - for garnish (optional)

Method

  1. Make the veggie filling
  2. Finely chop the onion, pepper, zucchini, and garlic.
  3. Sauté in olive oil for 5-7 minutes.
  4. Add tomato paste and chopped tomatoes. Season with salt, pepper, and oregano.
  5. Simmer for 10-15 minutes until the sauce thickens.
  6. Assemble the lasagna
  7. Spread a spoonful of sauce on the bottom of a small baking dish.
  8. Add a layer of lasagna sheets, then sauce, then a bit of cheese.
  9. Repeat layers (about 2-3 total), ending with sauce and cheese on top.
  10. Bake
  11. Bake in a preheated oven at 180°C for 25-30 minutes.
  12. Cover with foil for the first 15 minutes, then uncover to brown the top.
  13. Serve
  14. Let rest for 5-10 minutes before slicing.
  15. Garnish with fresh basil if desired.

Peach, Cheese & Arugula Salad

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 fresh or grilled peaches
  • 80-100 g feta cheese
  • 2 handfuls of arugula
  • 2 tbsp nuts (pine nuts, walnuts, or pecans)
  • 2 tbsp olive oil
  • 1 tsp honey
  • 1 tsp balsamic vinegar or glaze
  • Salt & pepper to taste

Method

  1. Prepare the peaches
  2. Slice them into wedges.
  3. Optionally, lightly grill or sear them on a dry pan for 1-2 minutes per side until slightly caramelized.
  4. Cut or crumble the feta cheese.
  5. In a large bowl, combine arugula, warm peaches, feta, and nuts.

  7. Mix olive oil, honey, balsamic vinegar, salt, and pepper. Drizzle over the salad right before serving.

Pear, Cheese & Walnut Salad

2 servings9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Salad greens (arugula, spinach, or mixed leaves) - 2 handfuls
  • Pear - 1 ripe but firm
  • Cheese (feta, goat cheese, or bryndza) - 50-70 g
  • Walnuts or almonds - 1-2 tbsp (lightly toasted)
  • Honey - 1 tsp
  • Olive oil - 11/2 tbsp
  • Balsamic vinegar or lemon juice - 1 tsp
  • Salt & pepper - to taste

Method

  1. Slice the pear thinly or dice it. You can lightly sear it in a dry pan for 1-2 minutes or leave it fresh.

  3. In a large bowl, add the salad greens.

Quinoa Bowl

2 servings6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Quinoa - 100 g
  • Corn (canned or cooked) - 100 g
  • Tomatoes - 2 medium
  • Avocado - 1 medium
  • Eggs - 2
  • Olive oil - 2 tbsp
  • Lemon juice - 1 tbsp
  • Salt and pepper - to taste
  • Fresh herbs (parsley, cilantro) - optional

Method

  1. Rinse the quinoa under cold water.
  2. In a pot, combine quinoa with 200 ml of water. Bring to a boil, then reduce heat and simmer covered for about 15 minutes, until the water is absorbed. Remove from heat and let it sit for 5 minutes.
  3. Boil the eggs until hard-boiled (about 8-10 minutes). Cool, peel, and slice them.
  4. Dice the tomatoes and avocado.
  5. In a large bowl, mix the cooked quinoa, corn, tomatoes, and avocado.
  6. Drizzle with olive oil and lemon juice, season with salt and pepper, and toss gently.
  7. Add the sliced eggs on top or mix them in.
  8. Garnish with fresh herbs if desired.

Quinoa Bowl with Chickpeas & Vegetables

2 servings6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 100 g dry quinoa
  • 1 can of chickpeas (~250 g), drained and rinsed
  • 1 medium carrot, shredded or julienned
  • 1 cucumber, sliced
  • 6-8 cherry tomatoes (or 2 regular tomatoes, chopped)
  • 1-2 tsp olive oil
  • 1-2 tsp lemon juice
  • Salt, pepper, dried garlic, paprika - to taste
  • Optional: fresh herbs like parsley, spinach, or basil

Method

  1. Cook the quinoa in salted water (1:2 ratio of quinoa to water) for ~15 minutes. Let it cool.
  2. Prepare the chickpeas: rinse well. Optionally, toast in a dry pan with paprika or cumin for extra flavor.
  3. Slice the veggies: shred the carrot, and slice the cucumber and tomatoes.
  4. Assemble the bowl: divide quinoa, chickpeas, and veggies into sections in a large bowl or meal container.
  5. Drizzle the dressing: mix olive oil, lemon juice, salt, and spices, then pour over the bowl.
  6. Top with herbs if using.

Quinoa with Vegetables

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Quinoa - 120 g (dry)
  • Broccoli - 200 g
  • Tomatoes (cherry or regular) - 200 g
  • Boiled carrots - 150 g
  • Olive oil - 1-2 tbsp
  • Garlic - 1 clove (optional)
  • Salt, pepper, spices - to taste
  • Lemon juice or balsamic vinegar - a splash for freshness

Method

  1. Rinse quinoa thoroughly and cook in a 1:2 ratio (quinoa : water) for about 15 minutes until done.
  2. Separate broccoli into florets and boil for 3-4 minutes in salted water or steam until bright and slightly crunchy.
  3. Slice the boiled carrots into rounds or cubes.
  4. Chop the tomatoes (halves if cherry, cubes if regular).
  5. In a pan, lightly sauté garlic in olive oil, then add tomatoes and carrots. Cook for 2-3 minutes.
  6. Add the cooked quinoa and broccoli, season with salt, pepper, and spices.
  7. Before serving, drizzle with lemon juice or balsamic vinegar.

Ratatouille with Bell Pepper and Cheese

2 servings8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Zucchini (green squash) - 1/2 piece
  • Yellow squash - 1/2 piece
  • Bell pepper (red or yellow) - 1/2 piece
  • Tomato - 1 large
  • Hard cheese (e.g., Parmesan, mozzarella, or Gouda) - 40-50 g
  • Olive oil - 2 tbsp
  • Salt, pepper - to taste
  • Herbes de Provence or oregano - 1/2 tsp
  • For the sauce
  • Tomato sauce or crushed tomatoes - 150 ml
  • Onion - 1/2 piece
  • Garlic - 1 clove
  • Olive oil - 1 tbsp
  • Salt, pepper - to taste

Method

  1. Slice the vegetables thinly into rounds: zucchini, yellow squash, and tomato. Slice the bell pepper into thin rings or strips.
  2. Sauce: sauté onion and garlic in olive oil, add tomato sauce, season, and simmer for 5-7 minutes. Pour into a baking dish.
  3. Arrange the vegetables in a circle on top of the sauce. Drizzle with olive oil and add spices.
  4. Cheese: grate the cheese and sprinkle it on top of the vegetables, or add it 10 minutes before the dish is done (for a golden crust).
  5. Baking: bake at 180°C for 30-35 minutes. If cheese is on top, bake uncovered or cover with foil and remove it near the end.

Roasted Potatoes with Zucchini, Feta & Vegetables

2 servings6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Potatoes - 300 g (about 2 medium)
  • Zucchini - 1 medium (≈200 g)
  • Bell pepper - 1 medium (≈150 g)
  • Red onion - 1/2 piece (≈50 g)
  • Feta cheese - 80-100 g
  • Olive oil - 1-1.5 tbsp
  • Salt and pepper - to taste
  • Dried herbs (oregano or Provençal mix) - 1/2 tsp
  • Garlic - 1 clove (optional)
  • Fresh herbs (parsley, dill, or basil) - for serving

Method

Roasted Sweet Potato and Asparagus with Parmesan

2 servings3 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Sweet potato - 300 g (1 medium)
  • Green asparagus - 200 g
  • Olive oil - 2 tbsp
  • Garlic - 1 clove (minced or grated)
  • Salt and pepper - to taste
  • Grated Parmesan - 30-40 g (or to taste)
  • Lemon juice - 1 tsp (optional)

Method

  1. Prepare the vegetables
  2. Wash and peel the sweet potato, then cut it into chunks or half-moons (not too thick).
  3. Trim the tough ends off the asparagus. Leave whole or cut in half.
  4. Make the dressing
  5. In a small bowl, mix olive oil with garlic, salt, pepper, and lemon juice if using.
  6. Roasting
  7. Spread the sweet potato on a baking sheet lined with parchment paper or lightly oiled.
  8. Roast in a 200°C oven for about 15 minutes.
  9. Add the asparagus, drizzle with the remaining oil mixture, and roast for another 10-12 minutes, until the vegetables are tender.
  10. Add Parmesan
  11. In the last 2 minutes, sprinkle the vegetables with grated Parmesan and return to the oven until melted and slightly golden.

Roasted Sweet Potato, Green Beans & Cheese

2 servings5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • For the base
  • 1 medium sweet potato (~300 g), peeled and diced
  • 300-350 g green beans (fresh or frozen)
  • 100-120 g feta cheese or mozzarella (light version if preferred)
  • 1-2 tsp olive oil
  • Salt, pepper, paprika, oregano or rosemary - to taste
  • 1 clove of garlic (optional)

Method

  1. Roasted Sweet Potato
  2. Preheat oven to 200°C.
  3. Dice the sweet potato, drizzle with olive oil, and season with salt, paprika, and a bit of oregano or rosemary.
  4. Place on a baking sheet and roast for about 25 minutes until tender and slightly crispy.
  5. Green Beans
  6. If using frozen green beans, blanch or lightly boil for 5 minutes in salted water.
  7. Alternatively, sauté in a non-stick pan with a small amount of olive oil.
  8. Add minced garlic at the end for extra flavor.
  9. Cheese
  10. Cut feta into cubes or crumble over the plate.
  11. If using mozzarella, add it to the roasted sweet potato during the last 5 minutes of baking to slightly melt.
  12. Serving (for 2 people)
  13. Plate half of the roasted sweet potato and green beans on each plate.
  14. Top with cheese of choice.
  15. Optionally sprinkle with some pumpkin or flax seeds for texture.

Roasted Vegetables

2 servings · ~450-500 kcal4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Potatoes - 500 g (about 3-4 medium)
  • Cauliflower - 300 g

  • Salt, black pepper, paprika, garlic - to taste

  • 1 serving (half) = ~450-500 kcal

Roasted Vegetables with Cheese

2 servings6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Potatoes - 300 g (about 150 g per serving)
  • Zucchini - 200 g (100 g per serving)
  • Bell pepper - 200 g (100 g per serving)
  • Olive oil - 1.5 to 2 tbsp (≈20 g total)
  • Salt, pepper, herbs - to taste (e.g., rosemary, oregano, paprika)
  • Grated cheese (like parmesan, mozzarella, gouda, or feta*) - 40-50 g total (20-25 g per serving)
  • If using feta, don't bake it - just crumble on top before serving.

Method

  1. Preheat your oven to 200°C.
  2. Wash and cut the potatoes into wedges, the zucchini into slices, and the bell pepper into strips.
  3. In a large bowl, toss the vegetables with olive oil, salt, pepper, and herbs.
  4. Spread them out on a baking tray lined with parchment paper.
  5. Roast for about 30-35 minutes, flipping once halfway.
  6. Five minutes before the end, sprinkle grated cheese over the top and return to the oven until melted or lightly golden (depending on the cheese).
  7. Serve warm.

Salmon with Baked Potatoes & Green Beans

Ingredients

  • Salmon fillet - 200 g

  • Green beans or asparagus - 200 g

  • Arugula - 2 handfuls
  • Fresh parsley - for garnish
  • Lemon - 2 slices

  • For salmon marinade
  • 1 tsp Greek or plain yogurt

Salmon with Potatoes & Fresh Salad

9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 400 g potatoes
  • 240 g salmon fillet
  • 400 g mixed salad vegetables (cucumber, tomatoes, lettuce, bell pepper)
  • or 400 g fresh asparagus as an alternative to the salad
  • 10 g olive oil
  • 100 g Greek yogurt
  • Lemon juice
  • Salt, pepper and herbs to taste

Method

  1. Preheat the oven to 200°C.
  2. Wash and cut the potatoes into wedges. Season with salt, pepper and herbs, add half of the olive oil and bake for 30-35 minutes, until golden and tender.
  3. Season the salmon with salt, pepper and lemon juice. Bake for 12-15 minutes, depending on the thickness.
  4. Prepare the salad by chopping the vegetables and mixing them with the remaining olive oil and a little lemon juice.
  5. Alternatively, you can have fresh asparagus instead of the salad. Simply boil or steam the asparagus for a few minutes until tender.
  6. Mix the Greek yogurt with lemon juice, salt and herbs to make a simple sauce.
  7. Divide everything into 2 equal portions - one for today and one for tomorrow.
  8. If you don't want fish, you can replace the salmon with 240 g chicken breast (120 g per serving). Season it with salt, pepper and herbs and bake or grill until fully cooked.

Salmon with Potatoes and Fresh Salad

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Salmon fillets - 2 pieces (~150 g each)
  • Potatoes - 400-500 g (peeled)
  • Lamb's lettuce or baby spinach - ~100 g
  • Extra virgin olive oil - 1-2 tbsp
  • Lemon juice - 1 tbsp
  • Salt and pepper - to taste
  • Fresh or dried herbs (parsley, dill, basil) - optional

Method

  1. Potatoes
  2. Peel and cut into medium chunks.
  3. Boil in salted water until tender (about 15-20 minutes).
  4. Drain, toss with 1 tsp olive oil and sprinkle with chopped herbs.
  5. Salmon
  6. Season fillets with salt, pepper, lemon juice, and herbs.
  7. Sear in a non-stick pan for 2-3 minutes per side, or
  8. Bake at 180°C for 12-15 minutes.
  9. Salad
  10. Rinse the greens thoroughly.
  11. Dress with lemon juice and a few drops of olive oil.
  12. (Optional: mix in a bit of mustard or plain yogurt for a creamy dressing.)

Salmon with Quinoa and Vegetables

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Salmon fillet - 300 g (150 g per serving)
  • Quinoa (dried) - 80 g
  • Broccoli - 300 g
  • Cucumber - 1 large (200 g)
  • Cherry tomatoes - 200 g
  • Options
  • Olive oil - 2 tsp (10 g)
  • Salt, pepper, and lemon juice - to taste
  • Paprika/dried garlic - optional

Method

  1. Quinoa
  2. Rinse the quinoa, cover with 160 ml of water, and bring to a boil. Simmer, covered, for 12-15 minutes until the water is absorbed. Let stand for 5 minutes.
  3. Salmon
  4. Season with salt, pepper, and paprika.
  5. Fry in a non-stick frying pan with 1 teaspoon of oil for 3-4 minutes on each side (or bake for 12-15 minutes at 180°C).
  6. Vegetables
  7. Boil or steam the broccoli for 5-7 minutes (to keep it slightly crisp).
  8. Slice the cucumber and cherry tomatoes. You can sprinkle with lemon juice and add another teaspoon of oil.

Shrimp Salad

9 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Cooked & peeled shrimp - 100-150 g
  • Salad mix or fresh spinach - 2 handfuls
  • Avocado - 1/2 piece, sliced
  • Cherry tomatoes - 5-7 pieces, halved
  • Cucumber - 1 small, sliced
  • Red onion - 1/4 piece, finely chopped (optional)
  • Olive oil - 1 tsp
  • Lemon or lime juice - 1-2 tsp
  • Garlic - 1 clove, minced
  • Salt, pepper, spices (e.g. paprika or dried basil) - to taste
  • Pine nuts or sesame seeds - 1 tsp (optional)

Method

  1. If using raw shrimp, cook them in a pan for 2-3 minutes per side until pink and cooked through. Let them cool slightly.
  2. In a large bowl, combine the salad mix (or spinach), halved cherry tomatoes, sliced cucumber, avocado, and chopped red onion (if using).
  3. In a small bowl, mix olive oil, lemon or lime juice, minced garlic, salt, pepper, and your favorite spices to make the dressing.
  4. Add the cooked shrimp to the salad, pour the dressing over the top, and gently toss everything together.
  5. Sprinkle with pine nuts or sesame seeds if desired.
  6. Tip
  7. Prepare a double portion to save time - store the second serving in an airtight container in the fridge for the next day. Keep the dressing separate until serving, if possible

Stewed Cabbage with Beef

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Beef (lean, tenderloin) - 80 g (or substitute with chickpeas)
  • Braised cabbage - 200 g
  • Olive oil - 5 g (1 tsp)
  • Carrots - 30 g
  • Onions - 30 g
  • Spices to taste (salt, pepper, coriander, paprika, bay leaf)

Method

  1. Prepare the beef: cut it into thin slices, season with spices.
  2. Fry the beef: in a dry frying pan (or with 1/2 tsp olive oil), quickly fry on both sides, then simmer under the lid for 20-30 minutes over low heat, adding a little water.
  3. Prepare the braised cabbage: heat the frying pan, add 1/2 tsp oil, fry the carrots and onions, then add cabbage.
  4. Simmer for 15-20 minutes over medium heat, adding a little water as needed.
  5. Place the beef on a plate next to the stewed cabbage.

Stuffed Bell Peppers with Chicken, Rice & Cheese

2 servings10 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Bell peppers - 2 medium
  • Chicken breast - 150 g (cut into small cubes)
  • Rice (dry) - 40 g (~3 tbsp)
  • Carrot - 1/2 (grated)
  • Onion - 1/2 (finely chopped)
  • Tomato - 1/2 (or 2 tbsp tomato puree)
  • Hard cheese (20-30% fat) - 20 g (grated)
  • Plain yogurt (unsweetened) or light sour cream (5%) - 1 tbsp
  • Olive oil - 1/2 tbsp
  • Salt, black pepper, paprika, oregano - to taste

Method

  1. Cook the rice until half done.
  2. Heat the olive oil in a pan, sauté onion and carrot, then add chicken and cook lightly until golden.
  3. Add tomato, yogurt, and spices. Simmer for 3-4 minutes.
  4. Mix the chicken with the rice.
  5. Stuff the peppers with the mixture and sprinkle with cheese on top.
  6. Place in a baking dish, add a little water to the bottom, and cover with foil.
  7. Bake at 180°C for 30-35 minutes. Remove foil and bake for another 5-7 minutes until cheese is golden.

Stuffed Sweet Potatoes with Chicken, Peas, Cherry Tomatoes & Parmesan

2 servings · ~420-450 kcal5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 medium sweet potatoes (about 250-300 g each)
  • 200 g cooked chicken breast (grilled or baked, diced)
  • 70 g green peas (fresh or frozen)
  • 6-8 cherry tomatoes, chopped
  • 2 tbsp grated Parmesan cheese
  • 1 tsp olive oil
  • Salt, black pepper, and paprika - to taste
  • Optional: a pinch of garlic powder or Italian herbs

Method

  1. Bake the sweet potatoes
  2. Preheat the oven to 200°C. Wash and cut each sweet potato in half lengthwise. Place on a baking tray, brush lightly with olive oil, and bake for 25-30 minutes, until soft.
  3. Prepare the filling
  4. While the sweet potatoes bake, heat a small pan. Add the diced chicken, peas, and tomatoes. Season with salt, pepper, and herbs. Warm everything together for 3-5 minutes.
  5. Assemble
  6. When the sweet potatoes are ready, scoop out a bit of the inside (make a small hollow) and mix it into the chicken filling. Then spoon the mixture back into the potato halves.
  7. Top and finish
  8. Sprinkle with Parmesan and return to the oven for 5 minutes, until the cheese melts.

Vegetable Boats in Parchment

Vegetable Casserole with Broccoli, Tomatoes & Zucchini

Vegetable Stew with Beans (Oil-Free)

~340-360 kcal8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Cooked beans (chickpeas, white or red beans) - 200 g
  • Eggplant - 1 medium (about 200 g)
  • Bell pepper - 1
  • Onion - 1 small (about 60 g)
  • Tomato - 1 large or 2 medium (150-200 g)
  • Carrot - 1 small
  • Garlic - 1 clove
  • Spices to taste: salt, pepper, paprika, oregano or cumin
  • Water - 2-3 tbsp (for cooking instead of oil)
  • Calories: approximately 340-360 kcal per serving

Method

  1. Dice all the vegetables.
  2. In a non-stick pan, add a bit of water and lightly sauté the onion, garlic, and carrot until soft.
  3. Add the eggplant and bell pepper. Cook for 5-7 minutes, stirring occasionally and adding more water if needed.
  4. Add the chopped tomato and spices. Cover and simmer for 10-15 minutes.
  5. Stir in the cooked beans and simmer for another 5 minutes.
  6. Serve warm.

Vegetable Stew with Carrots & Parmesan

2 servings10 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 2 medium carrots (≈ 200 g)
  • 1 medium zucchini (≈ 250 g)
  • 1 bell pepper (≈ 120 g)
  • 2 tomatoes or 200 g canned tomatoes
  • 1 small onion
  • 1 garlic clove
  • 1 tbsp olive oil
  • 200 ml vegetable broth or water
  • Salt, black pepper, dried herbs (oregano, basil, thyme) - to taste
  • A few walnut pieces (optional, for topping)
  • Fresh parsley or dill
  • 20 g grated Parmesan (for serving)

Method

  1. Peel and chop the carrots into cubes or half-moons.
  2. In a pan, sauté the carrots and onion in olive oil for 3-4 minutes.
  3. Add zucchini, bell pepper, and garlic, cook for another few minutes.
  4. Stir in the tomatoes and broth, cover with a lid.
  5. Simmer for 15-20 minutes, until the carrots are tender but not mushy.
  6. Season with salt, pepper, and herbs.
  7. Serve hot, sprinkled with fresh herbs, walnuts (optional), and grated Parmesan on top.

Baked Apples with Cottage Cheese, Honey, and Nuts + Tea

3 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 medium apple
  • 3 tbsp low-fat cottage cheese
  • 1 tsp honey (or more, to taste)
  • 1 tbsp chopped walnuts (or any nuts you like)
  • A pinch of cinnamon (optional)
  • 1 cup of your favorite tea

Method

  1. Preheat the oven to 180°C.
  2. Wash the apple, cut off the top, and scoop out the core, creating a small cavity.
  3. In a small bowl, mix the cottage cheese with honey and cinnamon.
  4. Fill the apple cavity with the cottage cheese mixture.
  5. Sprinkle chopped nuts on top.
  6. Place the apple in a baking dish and bake for 15-20 minutes, until the apple is tender but still holds its shape.
  7. Serve warm with a cup of your favorite tea and enjoy!

Ingredients

Green Berry Smoothie with Spinach, Banana & Berries

Ingredients

  • Fresh spinach - 1 handful (about 30 g)
  • Ripe banana - 1
  • Mixed berries (blueberries, raspberries, strawberries, etc.) - 70 g (fresh or frozen)
  • Plant-based milk or water - 200-250 ml
  • Chia seeds or ground flaxseeds - 1 tsp
  • Honey or Jerusalem artichoke syrup - 1 tsp (optional)
  • Fresh lemon or lime juice - 1/2 tsp (optional, for brightness)
  • Ice cubes - a few (optional, for a chilled smoothie)

Method

  1. In a blender, combine the liquid (milk or water), banana, spinach, and berries.
  2. Add the seeds, sweetener (if using), and a splash of lemon/lime juice.
  3. Blend until smooth and creamy.
  4. Add ice cubes and blend briefly again if you prefer it cold.
  5. Pour into a glass and top with extra berries or seeds if desired.

Green Smoothie

2 servings4 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Fresh spinach - 2 handfuls (about 60 g)
  • Banana - 2 medium
  • Apple - 1 medium (peeled and cored)
  • Lemon juice - 2 tsp (optional)
  • Water or almond milk - 300-400 ml
  • Honey or maple syrup - 2 tsp (optional)
  • Ice cubes - a few (optional)

Method

  1. Wash the spinach and apple.
  2. Add all ingredients to a blender.
  3. Blend until smooth and creamy.
  4. If too thick, add more water or almond milk and blend again.
  5. Pour into two glasses and enjoy!

Green Smoothie with Apple, Kiwi, Spinach & Cucumber

5 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Apple - 1 medium (peeled if desired)
  • Kiwi - 1 (peeled)
  • Cucumber - 1/2 medium (about 100 g)
  • Fresh spinach - 1 large handful (about 30 g)
  • Water or plant-based milk (e.g. almond, coconut) - 150-200 ml
  • Lemon juice - 1 tsp (optional, for brightness)
  • Ice cubes - optional (or use chilled ingredients)

Method

  1. Chop the apple, kiwi, and cucumber into smaller pieces.
  2. Add everything to a blender.
  3. Pour in the water (or milk), add lemon juice and ice if using.
  4. Blend until smooth and creamy - about 30-60 seconds.
  5. Taste and adjust: you can add more lemon juice or a touch of honey/maple syrup if you'd like it sweeter.

Green Smoothie with Banana, Spinach & Seeds

1 serving6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 ripe banana
  • 1 handful of fresh spinach (about 30 g)
  • 200 ml plant-based milk (e.g. oat, almond, or coconut)
  • 1 tsp chia seeds or ground flax seeds
  • (Optional) 1-2 dates or 1 tsp honey for sweetness
  • (Optional) a few ice cubes for a refreshing touch

Method

  1. Rinse the spinach, peel the banana, and remove date pits if using.
  2. Place all ingredients into a blender.
  3. Blend until smooth and creamy (about 30 seconds).
  4. Pour into a glass and serve immediately.
  5. You can sprinkle some extra seeds on top or garnish with a mint leaf.

Green Smoothie with Kiwi, Berries & Spinach

1 serving6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Kiwi (peeled) - 80 g
  • Mixed berries (fresh or frozen) - 70 g
  • Spinach (fresh) - 30 g
  • Banana (peeled) - 50 g (1/2 medium banana)
  • Water / plant-based milk / kefir - 150 ml
  • Chia or flax seeds (optional) - 5 g
  • Ice cubes - a few (optional)

Method

  1. Combine all ingredients in a blender.
  2. Blend for 30-60 seconds until smooth.
  3. Serve immediately. Enjoy cold!

Healthy Nectarine Almond Galette

2 servings8 ingredients

Ingredients

  • Dough
  • Oat flour - 60 g
  • Almond flour (or ground almonds) - 20 g
  • Greek yogurt (2%) - 40 g
  • Egg white - 1 (≈30 g)
  • Honey / erythritol / date syrup - 1 tsp (≈5 g)
  • Baking powder - 1/2 tsp
  • Filling
  • Nectarine (or peach) - 1 large (≈120-130 g)
  • Erythritol (or sweetener of choice) - 1 tsp
  • Cornstarch - 1/2 tsp (≈2 g)
  • For the crust
  • Almond flakes - 10 g
  • Egg yolk (optional, for brushing) - 1/2

Method

  1. Make the dough
  2. Mix oat flour, almond flour, and baking powder.
  3. Add yogurt, egg white, and sweetener.
  4. Knead a soft dough. If too sticky, add a bit more oat flour.
  5. Form into a disk and chill in the fridge for 10 minutes.
  6. Prepare the filling
  7. Slice the nectarine thinly.
  8. Toss with erythritol and cornstarch to absorb excess juice.
  9. Assemble
  10. Roll out the dough into a round (~18-20 cm diameter).
  11. Place nectarine slices in the center, leaving a ~2-3 cm border.
  12. Fold the edges over slightly, pinching as needed.
  13. Brush the crust with egg yolk or yogurt and sprinkle with almond flakes.
  14. Bake
  15. Preheat oven to 180°C / 180°C.
  16. Bake for 25-30 minutes, or until golden brown.
  17. Let cool for 10 minutes before serving.
  18. Nutrition per 1 portion (approx. 130-150 g) + Tea

Smoothie with Carrot, Apple & Orange

3 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 1 medium carrot (peeled and chopped)
  • 1 medium apple (cored and chopped)
  • 1 medium orange (peeled and segmented)
  • 150 ml water (adjust to desired consistency)

Method

  1. Put the carrot, apple, and orange into a blender.
  2. Add water.
  3. Blend until smooth. Add more water if needed.
  4. Pour into a glass and enjoy immediately!

Spinach, Apple, Banana & Kiwi Smoothie

6 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 30 g fresh spinach leaves
  • 1 apple, cored and chopped
  • 1 banana, peeled
  • 1 kiwi, peeled and sliced
  • 240 ml water or your choice of milk (almond, oat, etc.)
  • Ice cubes (optional)
  • Honey or maple syrup to taste (optional)

Method

  1. Place the spinach, apple, banana, and kiwi into a blender.
  2. Add the water or milk.
  3. Blend until smooth and creamy.
  4. If you want a colder smoothie, add a few ice cubes and blend again.
  5. Taste and add honey or maple syrup if you prefer it sweeter.
  6. Pour into a glass and enjoy immediately!

Strawberry Yogurt Dessert

2 servings3 ingredients

Ingredients

  • 300 g Greek yogurt (2% or fat-free)
  • 150 g fresh strawberries
  • 1 tsp honey or Jerusalem artichoke syrup (optional)
  • A few fresh mint leaves
  • Optional: pinch of vanilla or cinnamon

Method

  1. Prepare the strawberries
  2. Wash and slice the strawberries. You can cut a few in half and press them against the inside of the glass for a decorative look.
  3. Assemble the dessert
  4. Divide the Greek yogurt between two glasses (150 g each).
  5. Add sliced strawberries on top.
  6. Drizzle with honey or syrup if desired. You can also add a touch of vanilla or cinnamon for extra flavor.
  7. Garnish
  8. Top with fresh mint leaves. You may also sprinkle with crushed nuts or coconut flakes if it fits your calorie goals.
  9. Optional - Chill
  10. Place in the fridge for 10-15 minutes before serving to let the flavors blend and enhance.
